S27-S30 come from the "more features" request (context subsection "Level, offsets, CC icons, CooldownVoice and media context (S27-S31)"). They run strictly in order S27 → S28 → S29 → S30 (shared =Nameplates.lua=, TOC and =.luacheckrc=), ship as one release =v0.16.0-s30.1=, and are closed together by G-S30-GAME, which also carries the open G-FU-NP steps (D-69) only if G-S9-GAME left them unanswered (D-90 moved them to G-S9-GAME).
S31-S33 come from the follow-up "all modules should benefit from a SharedMedia addon" (context subsection "Media for all modules (S31-S33)"; Q-14 answered more broadly than the old optional Nameplates-only S31, which they replace). They run strictly in order after S30, ship as =v0.17.0-s33.1= and are closed together by G-S33-GAME (D-77).

S23-S26 come from the 0.14.0 in-game test (context subsection "0.14.0 in-game findings (S23-S26)"). S23 is user-only and runs first. S24 and S25 run in that order. S26 is independent of them and waits for Q-11. S18-S22 were closed =PASSED= on the user's instruction (D-42).

Board order is not run order. Recommended order now: S27 → S28 → S29 → S30 → S31 → S32 → S33 (the user's latest requests), then S7-S10 (which adopt the Media contract, D-76). (Earlier: S23 → S24 → S25, S26 whenever Q-11 was answered; all done.) Every game gate is "=/mui test= + paste the log" (debug track SD1-SD5).

Allowed statuses: =PENDING=, =IN_PROGRESS=, =PASSED=, =BLOCKED=.

* Goal
Build *MerrinxUI*, a personal World of Warcraft addon suite for the "World of Warcraft: Forever" client.

Development and distribution:
- It is developed on the Linux machine with a flake-parts =flake.nix=.
- It is published as a zip on GitHub Releases, built by a GitHub Actions workflow when a tag is pushed.
- The user downloads that zip on the gaming PC and installs it into =Interface/AddOns=.

The suite:
- has one options hub (=/mui=, ElvUI-style) that every module plugs into;
- works for every class out of the box.

Modules, in delivery order:
1. A movable range-to-target text.
   - Green when the class's spell (or a user-chosen spell) is in range, red when it is out of range, white when that is unknown.
   - Font, size, outline and position are configurable.
2. HideBlizzard: toggles for the bag bar, micro menu, XP/reputation bar, Blizzard action bars and Blizzard's damage meter.
3. Simple enemy nameplates with few options, using EllesmereUI's approach.
4. Action bars using EllesmereUI's approach, with paging for every class's forms, stances and stealth, and keybinds.
5. A damage meter window in the visual style of EllesmereUI's meter, drawing Blizzard's =C_DamageMeter= data.
6. EnemyCooldowns: a hub module that shows icons with a cooldown swipe on enemy *player* nameplates when they use a spell from a curated per-class table (interrupts, PvP trinket and racials, major defensives, key CC). Options: enabled, icon size, max icons, anchor and offset. Feasibility is gated by the S11 probes.
7. A modern options window, modelled on EllesmereUI's look (sidebar, header, styled switches, sliders and dropdowns), that replaces the AceConfigDialog window behind =/mui=. It renders the modules' existing AceConfig tables, so the module contract doesn't change.
8. A "MerrinxUI" button in the Esc game menu, directly above "AddOns" (below Options), that opens the options window.
9. Debug mode (SD1-SD5): a toggle; with it on, a "Run tests" button (=/mui test=) self-tests *every* feature and writes PASS / FAIL / SKIP / INFO lines to a log; a log window shows the log preselected for Ctrl+C so the user pastes it back instead of running =/run= probe scripts; features also log short trace lines at state changes. Every feature added later appends its own checks, and a busted guard fails the build when a runtime file has none.
10. Font dropdown (S20-S22): every user-settable font (today only the RangeText "Font" option, a free text field) becomes a dropdown of LibSharedMedia-3.0 font names, the way ElvUI and EllesmereUI do it. The options window draws each list entry in its own font.
11. Light-blue accent (S19): the "MerrinxUI" title and every accent-coloured control use light blue instead of EllesmereUI's default teal.
12. Nameplate follow-ups from the 0.14.0 test (S23-S26):
    - while our plate is on, only our plate is visible, in combat too (S24);
    - our debuffs show on our plate in combat, drawn by Blizzard's secure =AuraContainer= (S25; the user reversed the S18 "no debuffs in combat" choice);
    - the RangeText Font list hides fonts this client can't load (S26, if Q-11 says so).
13. More nameplate features and a voice module (S27-S31):
    - the unit's level, difficulty-coloured, just right of the health bar (S27);
    - X/Y offset sliders for the debuff row, next to the existing four-way anchor (S28);
    - crowd-control auras in their own, larger icons on the left of the plate, never duplicated in the debuff row (S29);
    - CooldownVoice: a new module that speaks "<spell> ready" through the client's text-to-speech when a spell the user typed into its list comes off cooldown, in and out of combat (S30);
    - fonts from SharedMedia-style addons already reach the RangeText Font list without code changes (D-70).
14. Media for all modules (S31-S33): one global font and one global bar texture (from LibSharedMedia, so any SharedMedia-style addon adds choices) on a "Media" page, and in every module that draws text or bars a per-module Font / Bar texture dropdown that defaults to "Use global". Today that is RangeText (font), Nameplates (font and bar texture) and the options window (font, after =/reload=); later modules (S7 action bars, S9 damage meter) follow the same contract (D-76).

* Scope
In scope:
- =flake.nix=: dev shell, pre-commit hooks, checks, the addon package with libraries from pinned flake inputs, and a release zip package.
- =.github/workflows/ci.yml=:
  - runs checks on pushes and pull requests;
  - publishes a GitHub Release with the zip when a =v*= tag is pushed.
- Addon source under =MerrinxUI/=.
- busted specs under =spec/= for the pure logic.

Supported client: "WoW Forever" only (TOC interface =16001=).

Every class available on Forever must have working defaults. That class list is confirmed by S0.

* Acceptance criteria (observable)
| ID    | Criterion |
|-------+-----------|
| AC-01 | =nix flake check -L= exits 0 in =/home/merrinx/Projects/merrinxui= after every code section, and the CI =check= job is green for the pushed commit. |
| AC-02 | =nix develop= provides lua 5.1 with =luac=, luacheck, busted, stylua and lua-language-server. =nix fmt= is nixfmt. |
| AC-03 | =nix build .#default= produces =result/MerrinxUI/= containing the TOC, =embeds.xml=, the Lua files and =Libs/=. The build fails, naming the path, if a TOC or =embeds.xml= reference is missing. |
| AC-04 | =nix build .#release= produces =result/MerrinxUI.zip=. The zip is deterministic (same inputs give the same hash), has a single top-level =MerrinxUI/= folder, and includes =Libs/=. |
| AC-05 | Pushing tag =vX.Y.Z= (or =vX.Y.Z-suffix=) whose base matches the TOC =## Version:= creates a GitHub Release with asset =MerrinxUI-<tag>.zip=. Tags with a =-suffix= are marked as prereleases. A tag that doesn't match fails the job and publishes nothing. |
| AC-06 | The workflow has default =permissions: {}=. =contents: write= is granted only to the release job. Third-party actions are pinned to full commit SHAs. Untrusted input is never interpolated into scripts, and =pull_request_target= is not used. |
| AC-07 | No third-party Lua is committed, and no code or media is copied from EllesmereUI or Bartender4 (both are all-rights-reserved). |
| AC-08 | The zip from a release, extracted on the gaming PC, loads with no Lua errors and is not shown as "out of date". |
| AC-09 | =/mui= and =/merrinxui= open the "MerrinxUI" AceConfigDialog window, with one tree node per module and a Profiles node. (Passed at S2. From S17 on, AC-38 replaces the window type; the node set stays.) |
| AC-10 | Creating, switching, copying or resetting a profile refreshes the modules immediately, and the active profile survives =/reload=. |
| AC-11 | The range text shows LibRangeCheck's estimate as =min - max=, =min+= or =?=. |
| AC-12 | The range text is green when the effective spell is in range, red when it is out of range, and white when it is unknown or the spell doesn't apply. The effective spell is the user's spell, or the class default when that is empty. A secret result still shows green or red, through =SetAlphaFromBoolean=. |
| AC-13 | Every class on Forever gets a default range spell, so the range text works with no setup. With a shared profile, alts of other classes each use their own class default. |
| AC-14 | A global "Unlock frames" toggle (root option, =/mui unlock=, =/mui lock=) is never saved. While on, it shows drag overlays. Positions persist. Unlocking is refused in combat, and entering combat locks again. |
| AC-15 | RangeText font, size, outline and enabled settings apply immediately and persist. With no target while locked, the text is hidden. |
| AC-16 | =busted=, =luacheck= (0 warnings) and =stylua --check= are clean for =MerrinxUI= and =spec=. |
| AC-17 | Each HideBlizzard toggle (bags, micro, xp, actionBars, damageMeter) hides its target after =/reload=. Edit Mode still works and there is no =ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED=. Turning a frame toggle off says a =/reload= is needed. The damage meter CVar is restored only if we changed it. |
| AC-18 | Enemy nameplates show health (reaction or class colour, grey when tap-denied), the name, and a target highlight. The options are width, height and name size. Friendly plates are untouched. |
| AC-19 | Enemy nameplate cast bar, driven by duration objects, with spell text and a not-interruptible shield. It can be toggled off. |
| AC-20 | Up to 8 of our own bars, each with enabled, perRow, size, spacing and position settings. Bar 1 pages for every class's forms, stances, stealth and shadowform (=[bonusbar:N]=), and for possess. Layout changes made in combat are applied when combat ends. Blizzard's bars are hidden automatically. |
| AC-21 | Blizzard keybinds (=ACTIONBUTTONn=, =MULTIACTIONBAR1..7BUTTONn=) fire our buttons exactly once, with =ActionButtonUseKeyDown= set to 0 or 1. |
| AC-22 | The damage meter window matches the EllesmereUI style spec in the S9 table. It offers Current/Overall and DamageDone/HealingDone, shows ="total (per-second)"= values, class icons and colours when they are readable, and has a Reset control. It works in combat with secret values, and Blizzard's meter UI is hidden. |
| AC-23 | (Optional, S10) LuaLS in Emacs resolves WoW API and Ace3 symbols through the generated =.luarc.json=. |
| AC-24 | When a hostile player casts a curated spell (duel or battleground), their nameplate immediately shows an icon with the spell texture and a cooldown swipe. The icon is gone no later than 0.5 s after the curated cooldown ends. |
| AC-25 | NPCs, friendly players, non-curated spells and forbidden plates get no icons. Secret inputs (spell ID, GUID, player/attackable flags) are ignored and never raise Lua errors. |
| AC-26 | Icons are ordered by remaining time ascending and capped at =maxIcons=. A recast refreshes the existing icon instead of duplicating it. |
| AC-27 | Icons survive the plate leaving and returning (state keyed by GUID) with the correct remaining time. =/reload= or zoning clears everything. Dropped if S11 shows the GUID is secret and the user accepts the degraded variant. |
| AC-28 | All EnemyCooldowns options apply immediately to visible plates and persist across =/reload=. Disabling hides every icon and stops event handling. |
| AC-29 | EnemyCooldowns icons are visible whether the S5 Nameplates module is enabled or disabled. |
| AC-30 | Every curated base spell ID resolves on Forever, the resolved names are unique, and each =cd= matches the probed =GetSpellBaseCooldown= value where one is available. |
| AC-31 | =OptionsLogic= mirrors the shipped AceConfigDialog semantics: ordering, inheritance, handler and literal resolution, =info=, hidden/disabled, =values= sort, range snapping, validate and confirm. All busted examples in "OptionsLogic" pass. |
| AC-32 | The window renders toggle, range, select, input, execute, description, header and inline nested groups, honouring =disabled= and =hidden=. Changes apply immediately, and state that depends on them re-renders. |
| AC-33 | The Profiles page in the window supports new, choose, copy, delete (with a confirm) and reset, and AC-10 still holds. |
| AC-34 | The window moves by its header, stays clamped to the screen, closes on Esc without opening the game menu, works in combat, is centred again after =/reload=, uses stock assets only and matches the Theme table. |
| AC-35 | The Esc game menu shows a "MerrinxUI" button directly above AddOns, at the same size, with no overlap, and the menu grows to fit it. The placement stays stable across reopening and re-layout. |
| AC-36 | Out of combat, a click closes the game menu and opens the options. In combat, a click opens the options with no blocked-action message. The button is hidden in gamepad UI. There is no =ADDON_ACTION_*= and =Logs/taint.log= has no MerrinxUI lines. |
| AC-37 | No fields are written on Blizzard frames or tables. The only exceptions are the =hooksecurefunc(GameMenuFrame, "Layout", ...)= post-hook and, in the S14-degraded Esc variant only, one =UISpecialFrames= entry. Amended by S24 (D-44): from S24 on, one =hooksecurefunc(UnitFrame, "SetAlpha", ...)= post-hook is also allowed per hostile nameplate =UnitFrame= (plus =SetAlphaFromBoolean= if S23 P2 shows it). The hook only calls =SetAlpha(0)= on that frame while Nameplates owns it. |

| AC-D1 | =/mui= shows a "Debug" tree node with: a "Debug mode" toggle; "Run tests" (greyed out while debug mode is off); "Show log"; "Clear log" (asks for confirmation); a line "Log: N/500 lines". Only D-27 option types are used. |

| AC-D4 | With debug mode off nothing is logged. No secret value is ever compared, concatenated, =tostring='d, shown or stored; a secret is written as =<secret>=. SavedVariables hold only a boolean and log strings. No GUIDs, player names or profile names are logged. |
| AC-D5 | "Run tests" writes a header line (version, interface, class, locale, combat), then every check source in a stable order (=Debug= first, then alphabetical), then a summary line. Chat shows the summary and up to 10 FAIL lines. The log window opens. A check *or a check provider* that throws is reported as FAIL with its error and never aborts the run. |

| AC-D7 | Checks are read-only (forbidden-call list in the check contract). Target-dependent checks SKIP without a target; running-state checks SKIP while their module is disabled. |

At runtime, =Debug/coverage <Module>= is FAIL for any module without =GetDebugChecks=. |

No trace call sits on =UNIT_HEALTH=, =UNIT_MAXHEALTH=, =UNIT_POWER_UPDATE=, =UNIT_AURA=, =OnTick= or =OnUpdate= paths except RangeText's state-change-only line. All =FontLogic= busted examples pass. |
| AC-81 | (S21) =RangeText/font= is PASS only when =LSM:Fetch("font", key, true)= equals the applied path and size and flags match; it logs key, path, size and flags. |

* Non-goals
- Clients other than Forever: Classic Era, TBC Anniversary, retail. No per-client TOC files.
- Other distribution: CurseForge, Wago, =.pkgmeta= or the BigWigs packager.
- A local deploy-to-AddOns app (the gaming PC is a separate machine).
- Automatic version bumping.
- A minimap button, localisation, the Blizzard Settings panel (=Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutCategory=). (LibSharedMedia was a non-goal until the user chose it for the font dropdown, S20-S22.)
- In the font dropdown and accent work (S19-S22):
  - font options for Nameplates (hard-coded =FONT=), the options window's =Theme.font= or the S9 meter, until those sections adopt the D-36 contract;
  - AceGUI-3.0-SharedMediaWidgets (the window renders the preview itself);
  - statusbar, background, border and sound media (statusbar media is withdrawn from this list by S31-S33, D-72); registering MerrinxUI's own fonts with LSM; a UI for LSM's =SetGlobal= override;
  - an accent or theme picker, a drawn slider fill (=Theme.color.fill= stays unused by the renderer);
  - truncating long font names, a search box in the dropdown;
  - a chat notice when a legacy font path is migrated.
- In the options window (S15-S17):
  - search, a theme or accent picker, UI scaling, a persisted window position, a collapse-to-mini mode;
  - tooltips (=desc= is shown inline instead);
  - AceConfig =color=, =multiselect= and =keybinding= types, and the =plugins=, =pattern=, =softMin=/=softMax=/=bigStep=, =width=, =image=/=icon=, =tristate=, =multiline= and =childGroups= variants;
  - an AceConfigDialog fallback command, and removing AceGUI/AceConfigDialog from =embeds.xml= or the flake;
  - EllesmereUI's fonts, textures, sidebar icons and theme art.
- For the game-menu button: gamepad UI support, coexistence with other addons that also move game-menu buttons (EllesmereUI, ElvUI), and any anchor other than AddOns.
- Configurable colours or update intervals, a yard-threshold mode, units other than =target=, per-spec range spells.
- Friendly nameplates, nameplate auras, threat colouring, class power.
- Stance bar, pet bar, Masque, LibKeyBound, LibActionButton. LAB is only a fallback via replan.
- In the damage meter:
  - multiple windows, per-spell breakdown, hover tooltips;
  - the extra meter types (Interrupts, Deaths, ...);
  - EllesmereUI's style menus;
  - EllesmereUI's fonts and textures;
  - combat log (CLEU) parsing.
- In EnemyCooldowns (S11-S13):
  - NPCs, friendly players, pets (for example Spell Lock);
  - custom or user-added spells, a spell-list UI, category filters or colours;
  - CLEU, even if S11 shows it can be registered;
  - talent or item cooldown reductions, shared cooldown groups (shocks, traps), cooldown resets (Preparation, Cold Snap);
  - casts made while the caster's plate isn't shown;
  - arena or unit frames, sounds, alerts, tooltips, Mover integration.
- Restoring hidden Blizzard frames without =/reload=. The meter CVar is the one exception.
- =.envrc=, README, CHANGELOG, darwin systems.
- In the debug track (SD1-SD5):
  - active tests that change settings, CVars, frames or combat state (toggle-and-revert, the S11 P7 swipe frame); read-only checks plus SD5 traces only (Q-D1);
  - catching arbitrary Lua errors (=seterrorhandler=, BugGrabber);
  - a live-updating or styled log window, closing it with Esc before S14/D-31 decide;
  - per-character logs, echoing every log line to chat, export other than copy/paste and the SavedVariables file;
  - converting the S14 game-menu probes (they hook Blizzard frames);
  - any =flake.nix=, =flake.lock= or =.github/= change.
- In the S18 secret-aura fix (the earlier non-goal "showing nameplate debuffs in combat" is withdrawn by D-46; S25 delivers it):
  - a refresh event when secrecy ends (=PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED= or a restriction-state event);
  - per-aura secrecy guards (=ShouldUnitAuraInstanceBeSecret=, =issecretvalue(aura.auraInstanceID)=) and =pcall= around aura calls;
  - reading the =UNIT_AURA= payload;
  - busted specs for this change, because there is no pure-logic change (D-11).
- In the nameplate follow-ups (S23-S26):
  - reparenting, parking or unregistering events on Blizzard's =UnitFrame=, its =AurasFrame= or its cast bar (EllesmereUI does this; D-44 rejects it);
  - buffs on nameplates; skinning Blizzard's =AuraButton=s;
  - per-aura secrecy guards;
  - coexistence with other nameplate addons that hook the same frames (Plater, EllesmereUI);
  - restoring Blizzard's own computed alpha on unstyle (we restore 1, as before);
  - filtering fonts for modules other than RangeText (S26).
- In the S27-S31 features:
  - level on friendly plates, Blizzard's level frame or classification icons (dragon, skull art); a level font size or colour option;
  - an anchor choice or X/Y offsets for the CC icons (fixed left), buffs, purge/dispel highlighting, CC from a curated spell list, Lua-side CC detection (=C_Spell.IsSpellCrowdControl= can't read secret auras);
  - corner anchors for debuffs (the user chose offsets instead);
  - CooldownVoice: curated or automatic spell lists, per-class defaults, item and trinket cooldowns, pet spells, per-charge announcements (a charge spell announces when its first charge returns from zero), cooldowns that were already running at =/reload=, custom message text, sound files or LSM sounds, raid/party announcements, icons or bars, =C_TTSSettings.GetVoiceOptionID= (the voice-type enum is unverified);
  - shipping fonts with MerrinxUI, or depending on a SharedMedia addon.
- In the media work (S31-S33):
  - sound, border and background media; font size, outline or colour as global settings (sizes stay per module);
  - fonts of Blizzard frames (chat, game fonts), the Debug AceGUI log window, Mover overlays, EnemyCooldowns (no text; Blizzard's cooldown countdown numbers stay Blizzard's), CooldownVoice (no visuals);
  - live font change of an already built options window (it needs =/reload=), a texture preview on the closed select button;
  - honouring or editing LSM's =SetGlobal= override (another addon calling it silently wins inside =LSM:Fetch=, R-59);
  - registering MerrinxUI's own media, a per-text font choice inside one module.

* Confirmed repository and environment context (observed facts)
** This repository
- =/home/merrinx/Projects/merrinxui=: git on =main=, *no commits*. The only files are an empty, untracked =flake.nix= and this plan.
- No GitHub remote has been confirmed. The planner's read-only Git commands did not include =git remote=.
- Nix only sees tracked or intent-to-add files, so new paths need =git add --intent-to-add=. That is a Git write and needs the user's approval.
- The =nix__*= MCP tools can't target this repo. Verifiers must run =nix= through the shell.
- The lead never pushes branches or tags. The user does.
** Reference flakes and workflows (user conventions)
- =/home/merrinx/Projects/workspace/platform/flake.nix=:
  - flake-parts, nixpkgs-unstable, a git-hooks.nix =pre-commit-check= (statix, deadnix, nil, nixfmt);
  - =formatter = pkgs.nixfmt=;
  - several devShells and =# ===== SECTION ===== = banners.
- =/home/merrinx/Projects/elmugge/flake.nix=: x86_64 and aarch64 Linux only; =runCommand= checks end with =touch "$out"=.
- =/home/merrinx/Projects/neovim/flake.nix=:
  - =git-hooks= input with =nixpkgs.follows=;
  - =flake = false= source inputs;
  - =devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell { inherit (pre-commit-check) shellHook; ... }=.
- =/home/merrinx/Projects/workspace/platform/.github/workflows/*.yml= use =actions/checkout@v4/v5= and =cachix/install-nix-action@v30/v31=, unpinned by SHA. This plan pins them by SHA, per the CI rules.
** Verified tools and actions
- nixpkgs-unstable (rev 8edc0c72): =lua5_1= 5.1.5 (with =luac=), =lua51Packages.luacheck= 1.2.0, =lua51Packages.busted= 2.3.0, =stylua= 2.5.2, =lua-language-server= 3.19.1, =nixfmt= 1.5.0. =zip= is assumed to be in nixpkgs; the S2 build proves it.
- git-hooks.nix (rev a0e4241b): hooks =luacheck=, =stylua= (=stylua --respect-ignores=), =statix=, =deadnix=, =nil=, =nixfmt=; =enabledPackages= exists.
- GitHub Actions, from =git ls-remote=:

  | Action | Tag | SHA |
  |---|---|---|
  | =actions/checkout= | =v5.1.0= | =fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09= |
  | =cachix/install-nix-action= | =v31.11.1= | =13d8dd58da0234aa297dedd986986ccb8e7f3e24= |

  - The checkout SHA is the =v5= / =v5.1.0= ref. A newer =actions/checkout v7.0.1= also exists.
  - The lead must confirm each is a commit SHA with =gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/commits/<sha> --jq .sha= before using it.
- The =ubuntu-24.04= runner ships GitHub CLI 2.102.0 (runner-images =Ubuntu2404-Readme.md:112=).
** Third-party sources (verified by fetching)
- =github:WoWUIDev/Ace3= (rev 40f4cc13, MIT, =LICENSE.txt=):
  - =LibStub/=, =CallbackHandler-1.0/=, =AceAddon-3.0/=, =AceEvent-3.0/=, =AceConsole-3.0/=, =AceDB-3.0/=, =AceDBOptions-3.0/=, =AceGUI-3.0/=;
  - =AceConfig-3.0/AceConfig-3.0.xml=, which includes Dialog, Registry and Cmd;
  - =Ace3.toc= lists =16001=.
- =github:WeakAuras/LibRangeCheck-3.0= (rev 95a6313a, MIT):
  - the library file is =LibRangeCheck-3.0/LibRangeCheck-3.0.lua=; it needs LibStub and CallbackHandler-1.0;
  - API: =lib:GetRange(unit, checkVisible, noItems, maxCacheAge) -> min, max=;
  - =isForever = interfaceVersion >= 16000 and interfaceVersion < 20000=, and =isRetail = WOW_PROJECT_ID == WOW_PROJECT_MAINLINE= (line 53).
  - Lines 205-375 hold the per-class spell tables. The classic spell IDs are used when =not isRetail=.
- =github:Ketho/vscode-wow-api= (rev d0b5b51f): =Annotations/Core=, 3.8 MB.
** Target client facts (EllesmereUI rev 7979e925; custom all-rights-reserved license, so study only)
- *Client:* Forever is interface =16001= and runs the Mainline (Midnight 12.x) API with *secret values*. =EllesmereUI_ClientGate.lua=: =iface >= 16000 and iface < 20000=.
- *Range:*
  - =C_Spell.IsSpellInRange(id)= can return a secret, which Ellesmere guards with =issecretvalue=.
  - Raid frames consume secret booleans through =SetAlphaFromBoolean(b, a1, a2)= (=EUI_RaidFrames_Visibility.lua:88-102=).
- *Nameplates:*
  - =NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED/REMOVED=; =C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit(unit)= is called unconditionally.
  - The enemy plate's Blizzard =UnitFrame= is set to alpha 0.
  - =StatusBar:SetMinMaxValues/SetValue= accept secret values.
  - Casts use =UnitCastingInfo= and duration objects.
- *Action bars:*
  - Buttons: =CreateFrame("CheckButton", name, parent, "ActionBarButtonTemplate, SecureActionButtonTemplate")=.
  - Headers: =SecureHandlerStateTemplate= with =RegisterStateDriver(frame, "page", ...)=.
  - Blizzard frames and pages: =MainActionBar= / =MainMenuBar=; =MultiBarBottomLeft= (6), =MultiBarBottomRight= (5), =MultiBarRight= (3), =MultiBarLeft= (4), =MultiBar5/6/7= (13/14/15).
- *Hiding Blizzard frames:*
  - Bartender4 =HideBlizzard.lua=, in order:
    1. optionally =UnregisterAllEvents()=;
    2. purge the Edit Mode =isShownExternal= key if =frame.system=;
    3. =HideBase()= or =Hide()=;
    4. =SetParent(hidden)=.
  - Bartender applies this to =BagsBar=, =MicroMenu= and =MicroMenuContainer=.
  - XP/rep: =StatusTrackingBarManager:UnregisterAllEvents(); :Hide()=.
- *Damage meter (=EllesmereUIDamageMeters/EllesmereUIDamageMeters.lua=):*
  - *Data calls:* =C_DamageMeter.GetCombatSessionFromType(Enum.DamageMeterSessionType.Current|Overall, Enum.DamageMeterType.DamageDone|HealingDone)= returns =session.combatSources[]=. Each source has =name=, =classFilename=, =totalAmount=, =amountPerSecond=, =isLocalPlayer= and =sourceCreatureID=. Also used: =GetSessionDurationSeconds=, =ResetAllCombatSessions=.
  - *Events:* =DAMAGE_METER_COMBAT_SESSION_UPDATED=, =DAMAGE_METER_CURRENT_SESSION_UPDATED=, =DAMAGE_METER_RESET=.
  - *Bars:* =SetMinMaxValues(0, sources[1].totalAmount)= then =SetValue(src.totalAmount)= (line 4272). We infer that the sources arrive sorted.
  - *Value text:* =format("%s (%s)", Abbrev(amt), Abbrev(perSec))= for number format 2, the default (lines 1702-1716).
  - *Class icon:* hidden when =classFilename= is secret (line 1978).
  - *Blizzard meter:* hidden with =SetCVar("damageMeterEnabled", 0)=; the code notes "C_DamageMeter API still works" (line 6533).
- *Damage meter default style values (lines 82-170):*
  - fonts: =fontSize 11=, =leftFontSize 11=, =rightFontSize 11=;
  - bars: =barHeight 18=, =barSpacing 2=, =barFillAlpha 1=, bar background alpha 0;
  - =numberFormat 2=, =iconStyle "spec"= (classic: class icon), =classIconZoom 0.06=, =showClassColor true=;
  - =barColorUseAccent true=, with =barColor {0.35, 0.55, 0.8}= when the accent isn't used;
  - left and right text white;
  - window background black at alpha =0.75=, with no window border;
  - header: background =#1B1B1B= at alpha 1, =hdrHeight 22=, =hdrFontSize 11=, =hdrIconSize 22=, text in the accent colour;
  - =refreshRate 1= (seconds, floor 0.5);
  - =showPinnedSelf false=, =windowCount 1=;
  - the default accent is =12/255, 210/255, 157/255= (line 1524).
** Enemy cooldown tracker context (S11-S13)
Observed in the repository (commit =c42707c=):
- =MerrinxUI/Core.lua=: =OnEnable= adds =module:GetOptions()= under =root.args[name]= for every module; =RefreshModules= calls =module:Refresh()= on profile changes. A new module plugs into the hub with no Core change.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeText.lua= is the reference module: =NewModule(name, "AceEvent-3.0")=, =RegisterNamespace(name, defaults)=, =SetEnabledState=, =Refresh()= calling =Enable=/=Disable=, option getters/setters reading =self.db.profile= at call time.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeTextLogic.lua= + =spec/range_text_logic_spec.lua= are the pattern for a pure logic file and its spec (=loadfile(path)("MerrinxUI", ns)=).
- =flake.nix= =checks.busted= runs =busted spec= over a =lib.fileset= union containing =./spec=, so a new spec file is picked up without a flake change.
- =.luacheckrc= =read_globals= currently has no =GetTime=, =UnitGUID=, =C_NamePlate=, =UnitIsPlayer= or =UnitCanAttack=.
- TOC is =## Version: 0.2.0=, last lines =Modules\RangeTextLogic.lua=, =Modules\RangeText.lua=.

External claims (researcher report, haiku; medium or low confidence, all UNVERIFIED for Forever; S11 decides):
- Retail 12.x removed =COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED= from addons. Sources: https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patch_12.0.0/API_changes, https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/COMBAT_LOG_EVENT.
- =UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED= args are =unitTarget, castGUID, spellID, castBarID=; =spellID= may be secret for hostile units (at least in instances). Sources: https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Event:UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED, https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patch_12.0.0/Planned_API_changes.
- Secret values can't be table keys, compared, or used in arithmetic; =string.format= and concatenation accept them. Source: https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Secret_Values.
- NameplateCooldowns was discontinued because it couldn't adapt to the Midnight API (https://github.com/casualshammy/NameplateCooldowns). OmniBar and Gladius are all-rights-reserved: study only, never copy (AC-07).
- The researcher's claims that OmniBar "works on combat logs" in 12.x and that Blizzard explicitly blocks enemy cooldown tracking contradict each other or cite unconfirmed pages; the plan relies on neither.
- S0 already showed Forever is less restrictive than retail in places (range and reaction not secret in combat), so retail behaviour can't be assumed.

** Options window and game menu context (S14-S17)
Observed in this repository (commit =c42707c=):
- =Core.lua= =OnEnable= builds =root= (=childGroups="tree"=, root-level =unlock= toggle at order 1, =profiles= from AceDBOptions at order 100, then =root.args[name] = module:GetOptions()=) and registers it with =AceConfig-3.0:RegisterOptionsTable("MerrinxUI", root)=. =SlashCommand= opens =AceConfigDialog-3.0:Open("MerrinxUI")= for anything other than =unlock=/=lock=.
- =Util/Mover.lua:129= calls =AceConfigRegistry-3.0:NotifyChange("MerrinxUI")= after every lock change, including the combat relock.
- =RangeText:GetOptions()= uses =toggle=, =input= (with a function =desc=), =range= (=min=, =max=, =step=) and =select= (table =values= with a =""= key). Getters and setters are closures with the =(_, value)= signature.
- =flake.nix= copies the whole =MerrinxUI/= tree (=cp -r ${./MerrinxUI}=), and the =busted= check takes a =lib.fileset= union of =MerrinxUI= and =spec=. New files under =MerrinxUI/Options/= and =spec/= need no flake change; =embeds.xml= doesn't change either.
- Shipped Ace3 (local =result/MerrinxUI/Libs/=, rev =40f4cc13=):
  - =AceConfigRegistry:NotifyChange(appName)= fires =callbacks:Fire("ConfigTableChange", appName)=; =GetOptionsTable(appName, uiType, uiName)= returns the validated table.
  - AceDBOptions' Profiles table uses a =handler= with method-name strings (=get="GetCurrentProfile"=, =set="SetProfile"=, =values="ListProfiles"=, =disabled="HasNoProfiles"=, =func="Reset"=), =get=false=, =arg=, =validate= (the =new= input) and =confirm=true= plus =confirmText= (the =delete= select).
  - AceConfigDialog (architect read): =set=, =get=, =func=, =confirm=, =validate=, =disabled= and =hidden= inherit down the path; =name=, =desc= and =usage= are literal strings, while any other string member is a handler method name. Sorting is by =order= (default 100; negative orders after all non-negative ones), then =name:upper()=. Ranges snap with =floor((v-min)/step+0.5)*step+min= and clamp, with defaults 0..100. Validation: =false=/=nil= gives "name: usage" or "name: Invalid Value", and a string is the message. AceConfigDialog never calls =NotifyChange= after a set. The AceGUI Dropdown sorts =values= keys numerically when both are numbers, otherwise by =tostring=.

EllesmereUI (rev =c2a6f460b7f5fe3d78bb68177d352307afd0f056=, all-rights-reserved, so study only; researcher report):
- =EllesmereUI_Panel.lua= builds a custom frame =EllesmereUIFrame= (not AceConfigDialog): a sidebar (=SIDEBAR_W 295= on a 1300×946 click area, =NAV_ROW_H 40=, a 3 px accent bar on the active row, a hover gradient), a header (=HEADER_H 138=), scrolling content and a footer. Module pages are built imperatively through a widget factory (=EllesmereUIOptions/EllesmereUI_Widgets.lua=).
- Style values: panel background (0.069, 0.058, 0.047); border white at alpha 0.05; dim text alpha 0.53; section text alpha 0.41; alternating row backgrounds at alpha 0.1/0.2; toggle switch track off (0.267, 0.267, 0.267, 0.65) and on = accent at 0.75 with a white knob; slider track white 0.16, fill accent 0.75; dropdown background (0.103, 0.095, 0.088, 0.9) with a white 0.2 border; labels at 14 pt. The default accent is (12, 210, 157)/255; on Forever EllesmereUI uses bronze (220, 167, 127)/255. The font is the addon's own =Expressway.ttf=, which we can't use.
- Esc closes it through one proxy name in =UISpecialFrames=. It refuses to open in combat. It also registers a canvas category in the Blizzard Settings panel.
- Game menu (=EllesmereUI.lua:3771-3983=): its own =Button= from =MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate=, parented to =GameMenuFrame=, positioned by =hooksecurefunc(GameMenuFrame, "Layout", ...)=. In combat it hides its buttons and returns. It anchors below Shop (falling back to Options), sizes itself to the anchor, shifts each pooled button below the anchor down with =ClearAllPoints= / =SetPoint(p, rel, rp, x, y - extraH)=, and then calls =GameMenuFrame:SetHeight(base + extraH)=. The click checks =InCombatLockdown()=, calls =HideUIPanel(GameMenuFrame)= and opens its window.

ElvUI (=ElvUI/Game/Shared/General/API.lua:1155-1189= at =7901ef41=): the same technique (own button, =Layout= post-hook, buttons found by =GetText()= against global strings, nudging pooled buttons, growing the menu height). It also writes =GameMenuFrame.ElvUI= and =GameMenuFrame.MenuButtons=, which we don't do (AC-37).

Blizzard UI source, Gethe/wow-ui-source branch =forever= (=943764493e6b16d63ded3ab304150d1f05e58b57=; researcher and architect reads):
- =GameMenuFrame= inherits =MainMenuFrameTemplate= (a =VerticalLayoutFrame=; =buttonTemplate= ="MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate"=, =topPadding= 48, =bottomPadding= 34, left and right 28, =spacing= 0) and =CallbackRegistrantTemplate=, strata =DIALOG=.
- =GameMenuFrameMixin:InitButtons= sets =self.buttons = {}=, calls =self:Reset()= (=buttonPool:ReleaseAll()=, =nextLayoutIndex = 1=) and adds, in order: [external event, gap], Options (=GAMEMENU_OPTIONS=), Shop (=BLIZZARD_STORE=, if the store is on), gap, Rewards (if there is a store front), AddOns (=ADDONS=, if =GameRulesUtil.ShouldShowAddOns()=), What's New, Edit Mode, Ratings, Support, Macros, gap, Log Out, Exit Game, gap, Return to Game; then =SetupGamepadButtons()=.
- =MainMenuFrameMixin:AddButton(text, callback, isDisabled, disabledText)= acquires from the pool, sets a sequential =layoutIndex=, =topPadding= (from =AddSection(20)=), text and =OnClick=, then calls =MarkDirty()=.
- =VerticalLayoutMixin:LayoutChildren= anchors each child with =SetPoint("TOPLEFT", leftOffset, -topOffset)= relative to the menu, so pooled buttons are anchored absolutely, not chained. =LayoutMixin:Layout= then calls =SetSize(w, h)=. Children without =layoutIndex= are skipped.
- =HideUIPanel= and =ShowUIPanel= call =CheckProtectedFunctionsAllowed()= (=InCombatLockdown() and not issecure()= shows the blocked-action message): addons can't show or hide UI panels in combat.
- Esc on Forever goes through =RegisterGameMenuEscHandler(priority, fn)=, called through =securecallfunction=; =CloseSpecialWindows= still reads =UISpecialFrames= at =GameMenuEscPriority.AddOnPost=.
- =GameMenuFrameMixin:OnHide= calls =UpdateMicroButtons()=, =ResetGamepadButtons()= (gamepad UI) and the protected =SetGamePadCursorControl(false)= when =CanAutoSetGamePadCursorControl(false)=.

NaowhForever PR #328 (https://github.com/naowh-gaming/NaowhForever/pull/328, merged): on Forever =GameMenuFrame:AddButton= also appends to =GameMenuFrame.buttons=. In gamepad mode =OnHide= walks that list and calls the protected =SetGamePadCursorControl(false)=, so an entry added from addon code makes every menu close forbidden. They switched to =MainMenuFrameMixin.AddButton= and hide their button when the gamepad owns panels. We use neither =AddButton= (D-32).

Settings panel API (https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Settings_API): =Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutCategory(frame, name)=, =Settings.RegisterAddOnCategory(category)=, =Settings.OpenToCategory(category)=. Recorded for reference only; the Settings panel is a non-goal.

Not verified on the live client (S14 decides): the live button list and anchors, =ADDONS= text, whether =Blizzard_GameMenu= is load-on-demand, the Esc registry, =Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF=, =MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate= size, and how =HideUIPanel(GameMenuFrame)= behaves from addon code.

** Debug track context (SD1-SD5)
Observed at HEAD =cdc0764= (read-only git, source reads and a read-only =nix build .#default=):

Tags =v0.2.0-s3.1=, =v0.7.0-S13.1=; =v0.8.0-np.1= was never cut, so E-NP-GAME is still open, and its probe block text is not recorded in this file. TOC =## Version: 0.8.0=. busted has 56 =it= cases. =EnemyCooldownLogic.SPELLS= already carries the four D-22 corrections.
- TOC order: =embeds.xml=, =Core.lua=, =Util\MoverLogic.lua=, =Util\Mover.lua=, =Util\CombatQueue.lua=, =Util\Combat.lua=, =Util\Hider.lua=, then RangeText, HideBlizzard, Nameplates, EnemyCooldowns pairs (Logic first).
- =Core.SlashCommand= trims and lowercases into =command=; =unlock= / =lock= go to the Mover; anything else opens AceConfigDialog.
- Module state checks can read:

  | Module | DB keys (profile) | State |
  |---|---|---|
  | RangeText | enabled, spell, font, fontSize, outline, position | =self.frame=, =fsMain=, =fsOut=, =lastGoodFont=, =reportedBadFont= |
  | HideBlizzard | enabled, bags, micro, xp, actionBars, damageMeter; global =meterCVarOwned= | =self.forced=, =missingWarned=, =activeGroups=; =self:CurrentGroupSet()= gives active keys (honours =forced=) |
  | Nameplates (=enabled = false=, =powerBar = false= by default) | enabled, width, height, nameSize, castBar, healthText, powerBar, powerHeight, powerText, debuffs, debuffsMineOnly, debuffAnchor, debuffSize, debuffMax | =self.units=, =unitPlates=, =plateFrames= (weak, plate → styled frame), =castMode=, =castEventsOn=; styled frame fields =healthBar=, =healthText=, =highlight=, =nameText=, =powerBar=, =powerText=, =castBar=, =castText=, =castShield=, =debuffIcons[i]= (=.texture=, =.cooldown=, =.count=); file-local =DEBUFF_SPACING = 2= |
  | EnemyCooldowns | enabled, iconSize, maxIcons, anchor, x, y | =self.state= (guid → records with =expires=), =units= (unit → guid), =unitPlate=, =containers= (weak, plate → container with =icons[i]= =.texture=, =.cooldown=), =index=, =ticker=; file-locals =LAYOUT_SPACING = 2=, =TICK_INTERVAL = 0.5= |
- All four modules create their state tables in =OnInitialize=, so state exists when a module starts disabled.
- Lazy creators that call =CreateFrame= (checks must never call them): =EnemyCooldowns:GetContainer=, =EnemyCooldowns:GetIcon=, =Nameplates:GetDebuffIcon=, =Nameplates:CreateStyledFrame=.
- =Util/Mover.lua=: file-local =isUnlocked= and =entries= (frame → ={label, getPosition, overlay, dragging, savePosition}=); =Apply= is only called from RangeText =OnEnable=/=Refresh=, so the frame of a module that started disabled has no anchor. =Util/Combat.lua=: file-local =queue= with public =order= / =byKey=. =Util/Hider.lua=: =Hider.parent=, file-local weak =hidden= set.
- Shipped Ace3 (package build): AceGUI has =AceGUIContainer-Frame.lua= (=SetTitle=, =SetStatusText=, =OnClose=, no =UISpecialFrames=) and =AceGUIWidget-MultiLineEditBox.lua= (=SetLabel=, =SetText=, =DisableButton=, =SetFocus=, =HighlightText(from, to)=, =SetMaxLetters(0)= default, =OnEscapePressed = ClearFocus=; =OnTextSet= and =OnEditFocusLost= call =HighlightText(0, 0)=). AceAddon mixes =Enable=, =Disable=, =IsEnabled=, =SetEnabledState= into every module, =IterateModules= is unordered =pairs=, =OnInitialize= runs in creation order and Core's =OnEnable= runs before module =OnEnable=s. AceDB =RegisterNamespace= supports =global= defaults, copies table defaults, strips defaults at logout, and creates an empty profile when =db.profile= is read. AceConsole names handlers =SlashCmdList["ACECONSOLE_" .. COMMAND]=. =AceConfigRegistry:GetOptionsTable(app, uiType, uiName)= returns the table; =execute= accepts =func= and =confirm=; =name= may be a function.
- Tooling: the flake =busted= check uses the fileset =MerrinxUI= + =spec= with cwd at the source root, so a spec can =io.open= the TOC. luacheck (=std = "lua51"= includes =io=) ignores only =211/addonName= and =212/self=, so an unused =ctx= warns. =.luacheckrc= lacks =date=, =GetLocale=, =C_AddOns=, =GetSpellBaseCooldown=, =SlashCmdList=.

** Nameplate secret-aura bug context (S18)
User report (BugSack, in combat against a mob; 20 repeats):
#+begin_example
GetUnitAuras(): Auras cannot be accessed when secret while tainted by 'MerrinxUI'
[MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua]:435: in function 'UpdateDebuffs'
[MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua]:190: in function '?'
... AceEvent-3.0 ... Fire
Locals: unit="nameplate1", filter="HARMFUL|PLAYER", profile.debuffs=true, debuffsMineOnly=true, debuffMax=4
#+end_example

Observed at HEAD =2ad2a1a fix(debug): handle restricted anchoring in point checks= (read-only git and source reads). The working tree is clean apart from the untracked =merrinxui-plan.org= and =debug-mode-plan.org=. TOC =## Version: 0.12.0=.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua=:
  - =AURA_EVENTS = { UNIT_AURA = "OnUnitAura" }= is registered only while =profile.debuffs= is on (=SetEventsEnabled=).
  - =OnUnitAura(_, unit)= calls =UpdateDebuffs(unit, frame)= for styled units and ignores the payload (line 187-192).
  - =UpdateDebuffs= is also called from =StyleUnit= (=NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED=, =UNIT_FACTION=, =OnEnable=) and =Refresh= (every option setter). Each path can run in combat.
  - =UpdateDebuffs= (line 424-479):
    - when =not profile.debuffs=, it hides every =frame.debuffIcons= entry and returns;
    - otherwise it calls =C_UnitAuras.GetUnitAuras(unit, filter, profile.debuffMax)= (line 435), and for each aura =aura.icon=, =C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDuration= (line 463, guarded by =issecretvalue=) and =C_UnitAuras.GetAuraApplicationDisplayCount= (line 470).
  - Debug checks:
    - ="apis"= lists the three =C_UnitAuras= paths (line 972-974).
    - ="debuffs"= calls =GetUnitAuras("target", ...)= twice for INFO counts (line 1374-1375).
    - ="secret flags"= calls =GetUnitAuras= and the two per-instance APIs (line 1402-1419).
    - =DebugLogic.RunCheck= is pcall-wrapped, so in combat these checks would show as a FAIL with an =error:= detail, not as a BugSack entry.
- No other file calls an aura API. A grep for =C_UnitAuras|GetAuraData|UnitAura|UnitDebuff|UnitBuff|AuraUtil|C_Secrets= over =*.lua= matches only =Nameplates.lua=. =C_Secrets= isn't used anywhere yet, and =.luacheckrc= =read_globals= has =C_UnitAuras= but not =C_Secrets=.
- E-NP-GAME and G-SD4-GAME passed out of combat. The in-combat steps (G-SD4-GAME step 9) were never reported, which is why this regression wasn't caught.

External facts (researcher, 2 passes; URLs read by the researcher, not by the planner):
- =C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret()= returns =hasSecretAuras= (bool, not nilable, no arguments), "Returns true if queries for aura data will generally produce secret values." Siblings =ShouldUnitAuraIndexBeSecret(unit, index, filter?)=, =ShouldUnitAuraInstanceBeSecret(unit, auraInstanceID)= and =ShouldUnitAuraSlotBeSecret(unit, slot)= all have =SecretArguments = "AllowedWhenUntainted"=. Source: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gethe/wow-ui-source/forever/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_APIDocumentationGenerated/SecretPredicateAPIDocumentation.lua
- =GetUnitAuras= has =RequiresUnitAuraAccess = true=, =SecretArguments = "AllowedWhenUntainted"=, and returns =auras= with =ConditionalSecretContents = true=. =GetAuraDuration=, =GetAuraApplicationDisplayCount= and =GetAuraDataByAuraInstanceID= also have =RequiresUnitAuraAccess = true=. Source: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gethe/wow-ui-source/forever/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_APIDocumentationGenerated/UnitAuraDocumentation.lua
- Forever addon authors report the same error and the same guard:
  - https://github.com/Thunderz96/forever-addon-kit/issues/1: "In combat C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDataByIndex throws Auras cannot be accessed when secret while tainted. C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret() flipped exactly with combat."
  - https://github.com/Noobygames/wow-statistics/pull/15: "The reminder now checks C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret() first".
  - Similar reports: https://github.com/GovtGeek/Outfitter/issues/169 and https://github.com/jncl/Skinner/issues/353.
- https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patch_12.1.0/API_changes says "The UNIT_AURA event now delivers a fully secret payload while auras are secret. AuraData structs are now always fully secret." It also says "Added AuraContainer and AuraButton intrinsic frames"; that path is out of scope (non-goals).
- https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Secret_Values: restrictions apply in combat, encounters, challenge mode and PvP matches.
- UNVERIFIED:
  - the researcher's repository commit (it reported the =forever= head inconsistently as =9437644=);
  - whether =ShouldAurasBeSecret= flips before or after =PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED= (irrelevant under D-S18-2);
  - whether =GetUnitAuras= can return secret contents, or throw, while the predicate is false (R-S18-3).

** Font dropdown and accent context (S19-S22)
User request: "everywhere where we set font, now it is a text field. We should have a dropdown where we can pick which font we want. Look at how ElvUI and EllesmereUI have done it", and "the colours for the logo MerrinxUI are greenish. Can we make it light blue instead? It looks too much like EllesmereUI." Answers to the planner: replace the *whole* accent, not only the logo; use LibSharedMedia-3.0 rather than a built-in list.

Observed in this repository (HEAD =2ad2a1a=, TOC =0.12.0=, working tree clean apart from the two =.org= files; read-only reads):
- User-settable fonts: =grep 'type = "input"'= under =MerrinxUI/= matches only RangeText =spell= and =font=. So "everywhere" is today exactly one option:
  - =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeText.lua=: =defaults.profile.font = "Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF"= (line 18); option =font= is =type = "input"= with a plain get/set that calls =ApplyFont= (lines 187-198).
  - =RangeText:ApplyFont()= (lines 70-87) calls =fsMain:SetFont(profile.font, fontSize, outline)=. On success it mirrors to =fsOut= and sets =lastGoodFont = profile.font=. On failure it falls back to =lastGoodFont= or the default path and prints "invalid font path" once per value.
  - Debug check =RangeText/font= (lines 250-266) is PASS when =lastGoodFont == profile.font= and =GetFont= size/flags match.
  - Fixed fonts with no option: =Nameplates.lua= =local FONT = "Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF"= (line 26); =OptionsWindow.lua= =setFont= uses =Theme.font= (ARIALN) with a FRIZQT fallback and records =fontUsed= for the =OptionsWindow/font= check (lines 51-59, 1313-1322).
- Accent:
  - =MerrinxUI/Options/Theme.lua= line 9 =accent = { 0.047, 0.824, 0.616, 1 }=, line 21 =switchOn = { 0.047, 0.824, 0.616, 0.75 }=, line 24 =fill = { 0.047, 0.824, 0.616, 0.75 }=. That is EllesmereUI's default accent (12, 210, 157)/255 (D-34).
  - =OptionsWindow.lua= uses the accent only at line 259 (sidebar active bar), line 565 (switch track when on) and line 1064 (header title "MerrinxUI"). =Theme.color.fill= is defined but no file under =MerrinxUI/= uses it.
  - =GameMenuButton.lua= sets the text "MerrinxUI" on a stock =MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate= button; it doesn't use the accent.
  - =spec/options_logic_spec.lua= =describe("Theme")= (lines 379-410) checks only shapes and ranges.
  - The plan's S9 style table hard-coded the accent (12,210,157)/255 for the meter header and hover; it now references =ns.Theme.color.accent= (D-40).
- Options window select path:
  - =configureSelect= (=OptionsWindow.lua= 648-674) reads =values= with =L.Value=, shows =displayLabel(values[current])= on the closed button, and opens =openDropdown(row.button, path, values)=.
  - =openDropdown= (814-851) sorts keys with =L.SortedValues= and fills pooled items (=pools.dropItem=, =createDropItem= 520-545, =item.text= set once with =setFont(text, Theme.size.value)=). Select rows are pooled too (=pools.select=).
  - =L.Member(root, path, name)= (=OptionsLogic.lua:71=) returns a node's own member, so the window can read =dialogControl= without an OptionsLogic change.
  - The renderer shows the *value* side of =values=. =LSM:HashTable("font")= maps key → path, so passing it directly would list file paths.
- Libraries: =embeds.xml= ends with =<Script file="Libs\LibRangeCheck-3.0\LibRangeCheck-3.0.lua"/>=. =flake.nix= =addonLibs= copies the Ace3 dirs and LibRangeCheck with their license files. =Debug.lua= =libraries= check lists 12 names (lines 235-250). =.luacheckrc= already excludes =MerrinxUI/Libs= and has =LibStub=.
- Coverage: =spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua= exempts any =*Logic.lua=, so a new =Util/FontLogic.lua= needs busted tests, not debug checks.

External facts (researcher reports, architect and verifier reads):
- ElvUI (=tukui-org/ElvUI= @ =41ce96ad7b37d45180dde6c24f4e8574dc8d3782=; researcher): font options are built by =ACH:SharedMediaFont= in =ElvUI_Options= (=Game/Shared/General.lua= line 122) → =type = 'select', dialogControl = 'LSM30_Font', values = function() return LSM:HashTable('font') end=. The DB stores the LSM key, resolved with =LSM:Fetch('font', key)= (=ElvUI/Game/Shared/General/Fonts.lua= line 97). The AceGUI-3.0-SharedMediaWidgets =LSM30_Font= widget draws each list entry in its own font (=SetFont(Media:Fetch('font', text), size, outline)=). https://github.com/tukui-org/ElvUI
- EllesmereUI (=EllesmereGaming/EllesmereUI= @ =8982a34785dc421da1b0f13b4fc7c5784f0025ce=, all-rights-reserved, behaviour only; researcher, medium confidence): its own dropdown widget (=EllesmereUI_Widgets.lua=) draws each item in the font the item carries; the font page (=EUI_Fonts_Options.lua=) lists LSM fonts, stores the LSM key (or a "use global" token), and resolves through =LSM:Fetch= with LSM's default as fallback. https://github.com/EllesmereGaming/EllesmereUI
- LibSharedMedia-3.0 official source: CurseForge SVN =https://repos.curseforge.com/wow/libsharedmedia-3-0/trunk=, revision 182, =MINOR 12000004=, header "License: LGPL v2.1", no LICENSE file in trunk. Revision-pinned file URL =https://repos.curseforge.com/wow/libsharedmedia-3-0/!svn/bc/182/trunk/LibSharedMedia-3.0/LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua=. Verifier (read-only =nix flake prefetch --json "file+https://…"=): exit 0, narHash =sha256-4jw8Nk/U1dFLF1O45pdq5lqWhZo8w0xoKzh1MXvzakI==, file sha256 =35230ddd390aebe0a7f607508e05a15d14341f44c9f151da7032393ea08b07ef=, line 13 =local MAJOR, MINOR = "LibSharedMedia-3.0", 12000004=, line 240 =local IsKnownFile = C_UIFileAsset.IsKnownFile= (read at load).
  - Architect: the same file equals =WeakAuras/Libs/LibSharedMedia-3.0/LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua= in the =WeakAuras-5.22.1.zip= GitHub release asset (zip sha256 =d0d4406b62e84d5e023f2af95e93d229907a41074d2749dd46643c8843536c33=) except line 4 (=$Revision$= vs =$Revision: 181 $=). ElvUI and WeakAuras2 *git* repos don't commit LSM; their packager pulls it in through =.pkgmeta= externals.
  - =lib.xml= in trunk is only =<Script file="LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua"/>=, so loading the file directly is equivalent.
  - The GitHub mirror =NoobTaco/LibSharedMedia-3.0= @ =7aaa31c3= is the old =MINOR 8020003= (uses =WOW_PROJECT_ID=, no =IsKnownFile= checks); not used.
- LSM API (official file): =Register(mediatype, key, data, langmask) -> bool= fires =LibSharedMedia_Registered(mediatype, key)=; =Fetch(mediatype, key, noDefault)= checks the =SetGlobal= override, then the key, then the default unless =noDefault=; =IsValid(mediatype, key)=; =HashTable(mediatype) -> {key = path}=; =List(mediatype)= sorted keys; callbacks through CallbackHandler (=LSM.RegisterCallback(obj, event, method)=). Western defaults include "Arial Narrow" = =Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF=, "Friz Quadrata TT" = =Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF= (the default), "Morpheus" = =Fonts\\MORPHEUS_CYR.TTF=, "Skurri" = =Fonts\\SKURRI_CYR.TTF=, "2002", "2002 Bold", "MoK", "Nimrod MT" and the AR CJK fonts; the built-in fonts are added without an =IsKnownFile= check.
- =C_UIFileAsset.IsKnownFile(asset) -> bool= is documented on =Gethe/wow-ui-source= branch =forever= @ =943764493e6b16d63ded3ab304150d1f05e58b57= (=UIFileAssetAPIDocumentation.lua=; architect read). Existence on the live client is A-25.
- Locked Ace3 (=40f4cc1356ae7fcc0cf71fc0a19dc7d50d6ae6c2=; verifier): =AceConfigRegistry-3.0.lua= line 101 lists =dialogControl=optstring= for =select=, so the option table stays valid.

** 0.14.0 in-game findings (S23-S26)
User input after installing =v0.14.0-s22.1= (Mage, enUS): two =/mui test= runs, one out of combat and one in combat, plus the report "When fighting a mob, the Blizzard nameplate came up behind ours and it looked wrong. The debuffs seem to be anchored to the Blizzard ones and not ours; it should only be my nameplate as long as it is on." The user asked to close what is closed now and to create tasks for the new findings.

Log lines that matter (verbatim values; the rest is PASS or INFO as before):
| Check | Out of combat | In combat | Reading |
|---+---+---+---|

| =Nameplates/target plate= | PASS =129.99992370605 9.9999761581421 10 0= | FAIL =129.99992370605 9.9999761581421 10 1= | the last value is =plate.UnitFrame:GetAlpha()=: Blizzard's plate is visible again in combat |

| =OptionsWindow/font preview= | INFO =6 AR CrystalzcuheiGBK Demibold AR ZhongkaiGBK Medium (Combat) MoK Morpheus Nimrod MT Skurri= | same | A-26 resolved: these six fonts can't be set on this client and fall back without an error |

Observed in this repository (HEAD =b2d2cac=, tag =v0.14.0-s22.1=, =origin/main= equal, CI run =38216238319= on the tag succeeded; only the two =.org= files are untracked):
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua=:
  - =StyleUnit= (line ~194) sets =plate.UnitFrame:SetAlpha(0)= once.
  - =UnstyleUnit= (~237) and =OnDisable= (~92) set it back to 1.
  - Nothing re-applies alpha 0, and there is no hook.
  - =plate.namePlateUnitToken= is read in =OnEnable= (~84), in the =styled plates= check (~1017) and in the =target cast= check (~1202).
  - =UpdateDebuffs= (~425) is the S18 version: it returns early while secret, and otherwise uses =C_UnitAuras.GetUnitAuras= plus the manual icon pool (=GetDebuffIcon=, =CooldownFrameTemplate=), positioned by =NameplateLogic.DebuffLayout=.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/EnemyCooldowns.lua:62-66=: =RescanPlates= calls =self:HandleUnit(plate.namePlateUnitToken)= for every =C_NamePlate.GetNamePlates()= entry. It runs from =OnEnable=. =HandleUnit= (line 111) passes the token to =C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit=.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeText.lua=:
  - =OnInitialize= (line 29) creates =self.frame=, =fsMain= and =fsOut=.
  - The =font= option's =values= is =FontLogic.Values(LSM:HashTable("font"))=.
- =.luacheckrc= already lists =hooksecurefunc=, =C_NamePlate=, =C_UnitAuras= and =C_Secrets=.
- busted is 144 / 0 / 0 (E-INT-VER of the S18-S22 run).

External facts (researcher reports, haiku; the URLs were read by the researcher, not by the planner; UNVERIFIED on the live client until S23):
- Blizzard UI source, branch =forever= @ =943764493e6b16d63ded3ab304150d1f05e58b57=:
  - =Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_NamePlates/Blizzard_NamePlateBase.lua:29-30=: =function NamePlateBaseMixin:SetUnit(namePlateUnitToken)= / =self.unitToken = self.explicitUnitToken or namePlateUnitToken;=. No field assignment to =namePlateUnitToken= exists in =Blizzard_NamePlates/*.lua=. This explains =styled plates 0 0 0=, =target cast none= and the no-op =RescanPlates=.
  - The researcher found no Lua =SetAlpha= on the plate or its =UnitFrame= in =Blizzard_NamePlates=, so the cause of alpha 1 in combat is unknown (S23 P2).
  - =Blizzard_NamePlates.xml= gives the =UnitFrame= an =AurasFrame= (=NamePlateAurasMixin=) with =DebuffListFrame=, =BuffListFrame= and =CrowdControlListFrame=. These are the debuffs the user saw.
  - =Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_AuraContainer/=:
    - =Blizzard_AuraContainer.xml:7= =<Frame name="AuraContainer" intrinsic="true">=;
    - =Blizzard_CustomAuraContainer.xml:7= =<AuraContainer name="CustomAuraContainerTemplate" virtual="true">= (secure mixins);
    - =Blizzard_AuraButton.xml:7= =<Button name="AuraButton" intrinsic="true">=;
    - =Blizzard_CustomAuraButton.xml:2= =<AuraButton name="CustomAuraButtonTemplate" virtual="true">=, which carries a duration cooldown and an application count;
    - the =.toc= has no =LoadOnDemand=, =AllowLoad: game= and =UseSecureEnvironment: 1=.
  - =Blizzard_CustomAuraContainer.lua= (=CustomAuraContainerSharedMixin=) has these methods:
    - =AddAuraGroup(groupKey, filterString, options)= (286), which reads =options.templateNames=, =initializeFrame=, =sortMethod=, =sortDirection=, =candidateFilters=, =maxFrameCount= and =layout=;
    - =HasAuraGroup= (330), =GetAuraGroupFrame(groupKey, i)= (335), =GetAuraGroupFrameCount= (348);
    - =SetAuraGroupEnabled= (363), =SetAuraGroupFilterString(groupKey, filterString)= (370), =SetAuraGroupMaxFrameCount(groupKey, n)= (381);
    - =SetAuraGroupSortMethod= (401), =SetAuraGroupLayout(groupKey, layoutOptions)= (409), =AddAuraSlot= (417).

    The flow layout defaults set the axis, anchor point, growth direction, padding and maximum line size (760-766). =GetElementSize= returns =group.elementWidth or width= (789-792). The base widget exposes =SetUnit(unitToken)= and =UpdateAllAuras()= (wiki https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/INTRINSIC_AuraContainer; Patch 12.1.0 "Added AuraContainer and AuraButton intrinsic frames").
- EllesmereUI (=EllesmereGaming/EllesmereUI= @ =eb69bb6b0924d7cd2eff866bdfac4d2ae695b465=, all-rights-reserved, behaviour only; active on Forever through its client gate):
  - =EllesmereUINameplates/EUI_Nameplates_Plate.lua= =HideBlizzardFrame= (about lines 66-245), called from its =SetUnit= on =NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED=, does the following:
    - sets the =UnitFrame= to alpha 0 and parks =AurasFrame= offscreen;
    - reparents most =UnitFrame= children to an offscreen parent;
    - calls =UnregisterAllEvents= on the =UnitFrame= and re-registers three target events;
    - unregisters the cast bar's events;
    - installs =hooksecurefunc(uf, "SetAlpha", ...)=, which re-applies alpha 0 under a recursion lock while it owns the unit;
    - hooks =selectionHighlight= Show/SetShown.

    It keys its own =ns.plates[unit]= from events and never iterates =GetNamePlates()=.
  - =EllesmereUI_AuraKit.lua:1559= creates =CreateFrame("AuraContainer", nil, parent, "CustomAuraContainerTemplate")=, then calls =SetUnit(unit)= and =UpdateAllAuras()= (1790-1791), and adds groups with =AddAuraGroup=. Its nameplate debuffs render in combat through these containers.
- Plater (=Tercioo/Plater-Nameplates=, no license file): =hooksecurefunc(plateFrame.UnitFrame, "Show", Plater.OnRetailNamePlateShow)= (=Plater.lua:3740=), then =Hide()=, =SetParent(nil)= and unregisters events (4537-4598). Study only.

User decision (planner question, this pass): "Show my debuffs on our plate in combat" (recommended option). Keep Blizzard's plate hidden (S24), and draw our debuffs in combat with Blizzard's secure =AuraContainer= (S25). This reverses the S18 non-goal and amends D-S18-1 (D-46).

** Level, offsets, CC icons, CooldownVoice and media context (S27-S31)
User request: "The nameplates should show level on right side of the mob/player. What happens if I add a media addon to WoW Forever, will I have access to more fonts? We should add voice for ability back off cooldown as a new module. We should also have option to decide where we want debuff to show on nameplates, and have CC show larger icons on left side." Answers to the planner's questions (this pass):
- Voice spells: "Spells I type in": an options field with spell names or IDs, comma-separated, starting empty.
- Debuff position: "X/Y offset sliders", kept next to the existing four-way anchor.
- Level position: "Outside, just right of the health bar".

Observed in this repository (HEAD =f8e30e8=, tag =v0.15.0-s25.4=, TOC =0.15.0=, only the two =.org= files untracked; busted 147/0/0 per E-S25-RB3-B2-VER; read-only reads):
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua= (1677 lines):
