  - constants (26-35): =FONT = "Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF"=, =DEBUFF_SPACING = 2=, =DEBUFF_GROUP = "debuffs"=, =DEBUFF_COUNT_SIZE = 10=.
  - events: base events in =OnEnable= (101-121); =CAST_EVENTS=, =POWER_EVENTS=, =AURA_EVENTS= toggled with =SetEventsEnabled= in =OnEnable= and =Refresh= (141-161).
  - =StyleUnit= (229-280) calls =UpdateHealth=, =UpdateName=, =UpdateColor=, =UpdateHighlight=, =SyncCastBar=, =UpdatePower=, =UpdateDebuffs=.
  - debuff container: =InitializeDebuffButton= (313-337), =ResizeDebuffButtons(debuffs, size)= (339-346, returns early while =ShouldAurasBeSecret=), =ApplyPendingDebuffResize= (348-358), =CreateDebuffContainer(frame, profile)= (360-378; filter =debuffsMineOnly and "HARMFUL|PLAYER" or "HARMFUL"=), created under =pcall= in =CreateStyledFrame= (455-462).
  - =UpdateDebuffs= (523-572): =relativeTo= is =castBar=/=powerBar=/=healthBar= for BOTTOM, =frame= for LEFT/RIGHT, =nameText= otherwise; =SetPoint(DebuffLayout(anchor, 1, 1, debuffSize, DEBUFF_SPACING))=; filter, max and layout set every call; the S25 pending-resize path while auras are secret.
  - =healthBar= covers =frame= (=SetAllPoints=); =healthText= is centred in it; =nameText= sits =BOTTOM= to =frame= =TOP=.
  - checks =debuffs= (1470-1531) and =debuff icons= (1532-1610) duplicate the =relativeTo= selection and the filter string.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/NameplateLogic.lua=: =Color=, =FormatValue=, =PowerColor=, =DebuffLayout(anchor, i, n, size, spacing)= (LEFT → ="RIGHT", "LEFT", -spacing - (i-1)*step, 0=; RIGHT → ="LEFT", "RIGHT", spacing + ..., 0=). =spec/nameplate_spec.lua= loads it with =loadfile(...)("MerrinxUI", ns)=.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/EnemyCooldowns.lua=: defaults =iconSize = 20=, =anchor = "TOP"=, =x = 0=, =y = 2=; its container is parented to the base plate (D-21), so its RIGHT/LEFT anchors can overlap the level text or the CC icons (R-51).
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeTextLogic.lua:18= =NormalizeSpell(v)=: positive integers (number or numeric string) → number; other trimmed non-empty strings → string; anything else → nil.
- =MerrinxUI/Modules/RangeText.lua=: the =font= select uses =FontLogic.Values(LSM:HashTable("font"), isUsable, keep)= evaluated when the list opens (S26), and =LibSharedMedia_Registered= re-applies a late-registered stored key (D-38). It is the only font option; Nameplates uses the hard-coded =FONT=.
- =.luacheckrc= =read_globals= has no =UnitEffectiveLevel=, =UnitClassification=, =GetCreatureDifficultyColor= or =C_VoiceChat=; it has =C_Spell=, =C_Secrets=, =C_Timer=, =C_UnitAuras=, =Enum=.
- TOC ends with =Modules\EnemyCooldownLogic.lua=, =Modules\EnemyCooldowns.lua=. =spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua= requires every non-=*Logic.lua= runtime file to define =GetDebugChecks= or call =RegisterChecks=.

External facts (Gethe/wow-ui-source branch =forever= @ =943764493e6b16d63ded3ab304150d1f05e58b57=, researcher reads (haiku) quoting raw files under https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gethe/wow-ui-source/943764493e6b16d63ded3ab304150d1f05e58b57/; UNVERIFIED on the live client until G-S30-GAME):
- Level:
  - =Blizzard_APIDocumentationGenerated/UnitDocumentation.lua=: =UnitLevel= (2474), =UnitEffectiveLevel= (1124) and =UnitClassification= (966) carry only =SecretArguments = "AllowedWhenUntainted"=; nothing marks their *returns* secret.
  - Events (same file): =UNIT_LEVEL= (4350-4362, payload =unitTarget=), =UNIT_CLASSIFICATION_CHANGED= (4158-4165, =unitTarget=), =PLAYER_LEVEL_UP= (3830-3849, =level=, ...).
  - =Blizzard_FrameXMLUtil/Mainline/DifficultyUtil.lua= 68-74: global =GetCreatureDifficultyColor(level)= → =DifficultyUtil.GetRelativeDifficultyColor(UnitEffectiveLevel("player"), level)=, returning a =QuestDifficultyColors= entry. =Blizzard_FrameXMLBase/Constants.lua= 195-212: entries are ={ r =, g =, b =, font = }= for =trivial=, =standard=, =difficult=, =verydifficult=, =impossible= (the TOC line carries =[AllowLoadGameType mainline]=; that it loads on Forever is A-35). Level -1 compares as a very low level, so it must be special-cased.
  - =Blizzard_NamePlates/Blizzard_NamePlateLevelFrame.lua=: Blizzard shows a level only for other players, under a game rule.
- Aura filters and containers:
  - =Blizzard_FrameXMLUtil/AuraUtil.lua= 270-320: filter tokens =HELPFUL=, =HARMFUL=, =PLAYER=, =RAID=, =CANCELABLE=, =INCLUDE_NAME_PLATE_ONLY=, =MAW=, =EXTERNAL_DEFENSIVE=, =CROWD_CONTROL=, =RAID_IN_COMBAT=, =RAID_PLAYER_DISPELLABLE=, =BIG_DEFENSIVE=, =IMPORTANT=, =DISPELLABLE=; =AuraFilterNegationPrefix = "!"= (296); =IsValidFilterString= accepts ="HARMFUL|PLAYER|!CROWD_CONTROL"= and ="HARMFUL|CROWD_CONTROL"=. No Blizzard code passes =CROWD_CONTROL= in a filter string: Blizzard's nameplates route CC in Lua with =C_Spell.IsSpellCrowdControl(aura.spellId)= into a =CrowdControlListFrame= (=maxAuraItemsDisplayed= 2), mutually exclusive with the debuff list. Whether the C side accepts the token and the negation is A-39.
  - =Blizzard_AuraContainer/Blizzard_CustomAuraContainer.lua=: =AddAuraGroup= (286-325) validates the filter with =AuraUtil.IsValidFilterString=, registers the group and calls =UpdateAllAuras=; =SetAuraGroupLayout= (409-413). All groups of one container share *one* flow layout (580, 741-753), and there is no cross-group dedupe of =auraInstanceID=. Container layout defaults (=Blizzard_AuraContainerShared.lua= 114-126): horizontal axis, =anchorPoint "TOPLEFT"=, growth Right/Down.
- Cooldowns and speech:
  - =C_Secrets= (=SecretPredicateAPIDocumentation.lua=, namespace =C_Secrets=): =ShouldAurasBeSecret= (119-126) and =ShouldCooldownsBeSecret= (129-136).
  - =SpellDocumentation.lua= 309-340: =C_Spell.GetSpellCooldown(spellIdentifier)= → =SpellCooldownInfo=, =SecretWhenCooldownsRestricted = true=, =MayReturnNothing=. =SpellSharedDocumentation.lua= 19-28: =startTime=, =duration=, =modRate= may be secret; =isEnabled=, =isActive=, =isOnGCD= are =NeverSecret=; =isOnGCD= doc: "do not trust this field unless responding to a SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN event". =C_Spell.GetSpellInfo= (416-429) → ={ name, iconID, originalIconID, castTime, minRange, maxRange, spellID }=, =MayReturnNothing=. =C_Spell.GetSpellName= (518-532).
  - =SpellBookDocumentation.lua=: =SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN= (927-941; payload =spellID?=, =baseSpellID?=, =category?=, =startRecoveryCategory?=, =itemID?=), =SPELL_UPDATE_CHARGES= (920-925), =SPELLS_CHANGED= (968-972). The wiki (https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN) says the event does not fire when a cooldown *finishes*, so the end needs polling (A-36).
  - =VoiceChatDocumentation.lua= 864-877: =C_VoiceChat.SpeakText(voiceID, text, rate, volume, overlap = false)=, =SecretArguments = "AllowedWhenTainted"=, =text= =ConditionalSecret=, no restriction flag; =GetTtsVoices()= → ={ voiceID, name }[]= (399-404, 1564-1572); =StopSpeakingText()= (879-881). Blizzard's call: =Blizzard_ChatFrame/Shared/TextToSpeechFrame.lua= 175-182 =C_VoiceChat.SpeakText(voice.voiceID, text, C_TTSSettings.GetSpeechRate(), C_TTSSettings.GetSpeechVolume(), ...)=. =C_TTSSettings.GetVoiceOptionID(voiceType)= needs =Enum.TtsVoiceType=, which wasn't found. =Blizzard_CooldownViewer= has no spoken "ready" alert on this branch.
  - =FrameAPICooldownDocumentation.lua= 295-326: =SetCooldownFromDurationObject= has =SecretArguments = "AllowedWhenUntainted"=, so a hidden Cooldown widget with =OnCooldownDone= is not a usable end detector for tainted code in combat.
- Media addons (researcher; sources https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/TOC_format, https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/API_C_AddOns.GetAddOnInterfaceVersion, https://www.wowinterface.com/forums/showthread.php?t=60046, https://github.com/Myrroddin/sharedmedia-classicalfonts, https://www.curseforge.com/wow/addons/font-manager):
  - SharedMedia-style packs call =LSM:Register("font", name, [[Interface\AddOns\<Pack>\...\x.ttf]])=. LibStub keeps the highest LSM MINOR loaded, and every addon's registrations land in that one registry.
  - Packs whose TOC doesn't list 16001 are "out of date" and skipped unless "Load out of date AddOns" is ticked; comma-separated =## Interface:= lines are supported (UNVERIFIED for specific packs).
  - New font files need a full client restart, not =/reload=; =.ttf= is the reliable format.

** Media for all modules (S31-S33)
User request: "You have added S31, I want all modules to be able to benefit if I download an addon like SharedMedia." Answers: media types "Fonts and bar textures"; organisation "One global choice + per-module override" ("Use global" default, ElvUI style). This replaces the optional Nameplates-only S31 (Q-14 answered more broadly).

Observed in this repository (HEAD =f8e30e8=, read-only reads; S27-S30 not yet implemented):
- Media consumers today (=grep SetFont|SetStatusBarTexture|FONT|Theme.font|WHITE8X8|LSM= under =MerrinxUI/=):
  - =Modules/RangeText.lua=: =LSM= at file top; =defaults.profile.font = FontLogic.DEFAULT=; =MigrateFont= in =OnInitialize= and =Refresh=; =OnEnable= registers =LSM.RegisterCallback(self, "LibSharedMedia_Registered", "OnMediaRegistered")= (D-38), =OnDisable= unregisters it; =ApplyFont= fetches =LSM:Fetch("font", profile.font, true)=, falls back to =lastGoodFont= or =LSM:Fetch("font", FontLogic.DEFAULT)= and prints once per =reportedBadFont=; =self.fontProbe= (S26) feeds the option =values=; check =font= compares =lastGoodFont= with =LSM:Fetch=.
  - =Modules/Nameplates.lua=: =FONT = "Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF"= (26) for name/health/power/cast texts in =ApplySize= (466-479) and the debuff count (=InitializeDebuffButton=, 335); =BAR_TEXTURE = "Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8X8"= (27) for the health, power and cast StatusBars, their backgrounds and the target highlight; the cast colour =SetStatusBarColor(0.9, 0.6, 0.1)= is set once at creation (426). =initializedDebuffButtons= is a weak-keyed set.
  - =Options/OptionsWindow.lua=: =LSM= at file top; =setFont(fs, size, flags)= (53-60) uses =Theme.font= (ARIALN) with an FRIZQT fallback and records =fontUsed=; =isFontSelect= (63-65) and =setPreviewFont= (67-71, =LSM:Fetch("font", key, true)=, failures in =previewFailed=); =createDropItem= (537-565, a =bg= colour texture with hover colours); =configureSelect= (664-697) and =openDropdown= (~820-880) apply the preview only for ="LSM30_Font"=. Checks =font= and =font preview= (~1340-1360).
  - =Options/Theme.lua=: =font = "Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF"=, =texture = "Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8X8"=.
  - =Util/Mover.lua= backdrops use =WHITE8X8=; =EnemyCooldowns.lua= sets only icon textures; no other text.
- =Util/FontLogic.lua=: =DEFAULT = "Friz Quadrata TT"=, =IsPath=, =Values(hash, isUsable, keep)= (returns ={}= for a nil hash), =Migrate(value, hash, default)= (=""=/non-string → default; non-path strings kept; paths matched case/slash-insensitively).
- =Options/OptionsLogic.lua:236= =L.SortedValues= sorts keys numerically when both are numbers, else by =tostring=, so a ="*"= key (byte 42) sorts before digits and letters.
- =Core.lua=: root options are built from every module's =GetOptions= in =OnEnable=; =RefreshModules= calls every module's =Refresh= (unordered =pairs=) on profile changed/copied/reset. AceDB switches every namespace before firing the callback, so a module reading another module's =db.profile= at call time always sees the new profile.
- =Debug/Debug.lua= 104-122: for every module except Debug with =db.profile=, an automatic =settings= INFO and an =enabled state= check (=IsEnabled() == (profile.enabled == true)=); a module without =profile.enabled= would FAIL it.
- Shipped Ace3: AceEvent's =OnEmbedDisable= unregisters all events and messages of a disabled module; AceAddon runs =OnInitialize= in creation (TOC) order before any =OnEnable= (architect read, AceEvent 112-114, AceAddon 615-616).
- LibSharedMedia-3.0 r182 (=/nix/store/yqi84m25dnimxww5j2l99lii2k0c3xp8-merrinxui-libs/LibSharedMedia-3.0/LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua=, planner read):
  - media types =background=, =border=, =font=, =statusbar=, =sound= (54-58);
  - statusbar defaults (194-198): "Blizzard" (=Interface\TargetingFrame\UI-StatusBar=, the LSM default), "Blizzard Character Skills Bar", "Blizzard Raid Bar", "Solid" (=Interface\Buttons\WHITE8X8=, today's look);
  - =Register= (242-280) rejects fonts and statusbar/background/border/sound files that =C_UIFileAsset.IsKnownFile= doesn't know, reporting through =geterrorhandler()= (a BugSack line attributed to LSM), and non-western-flagged fonts on western clients;
  - =Fetch(mediatype, key, noDefault)= (291), =HashTable= (302, key → path; =#= of it is 0), =List= (306, sorted keys), =SetGlobal= (320) overrides =Fetch= for every addon.
  - Default font key: "Friz Quadrata TT" on western clients; koKR, zhCN, zhTW register other default keys (134-153), so a hard-coded fallback key isn't portable; =LSM:Fetch("font", key)= without =noDefault= returns the locale default.

Architect critique (opus, this pass) adopted: the message relay carries the key; consumers re-apply only when the key matters; no Media =Refresh= (profile changes already reach every consumer); count fonts tracked so they follow changes; the cast colour re-applied after a texture swap; the statusbar preview tinted; =#LSM:List(mt)= for counts; a separate release 0.17.0.

** Unverified claims (S0 probes decide)
- =WOW_PROJECT_ID= equals =WOW_PROJECT_MAINLINE= on Forever. If so, LibRangeCheck treats the client as retail and uses retail spell IDs, which could break the yard estimates (R-04).
- The class list on Forever. Assumed to be the nine classic classes.
- That the default spell IDs exist on Forever.
- That =FontString:SetAlphaFromBoolean=, =StatusBar:SetTimerDuration= and the =Unit*Duration= APIs exist.
- That =AbbreviateNumbers= and =string.format= accept secret values.
- That CLEU is forbidden. Nothing in this plan uses it.
- The license of LibActionButton (not used).

* Decisions
| ID   | Decision | Origin |
|------+----------+--------|
| D-01 | Ace3 hub: AceAddon; AceDB with one namespace per module and a shared "Default" profile; AceConfigDialog tree; AceDBOptions. | User |
| D-02 | "In range" means the effective spell is in range, using =C_Spell.IsSpellInRange(spell, "target")= only. The number shown is LibRangeCheck's estimate. The spell can be given as a name or an ID. | User + verified API |
| D-03 | =nil= shows white. A secret result uses two overlaid FontStrings (green and red), switched with =SetAlphaFromBoolean=. | Design |
| D-04 | HideBlizzard covers bags, micro, xp, actionBars and damageMeter. ActionBars and DamageMeter force their group on with =HideBlizzard:Ensure=. | User |
| D-05 | Libraries are =flake = false= inputs, copied into =Libs/= at build time and never committed. | Design |
| D-06 | Distribution is a tag-triggered GitHub Actions workflow that publishes =nix build .#release= as =MerrinxUI-<tag>.zip= on a GitHub Release. The local deploy app is dropped. | User |
| D-07 | TOC =## Interface: 16001=, confirmed by S0. TOC =## Version: X.Y.Z= is the release version, and the tag must be =vX.Y.Z= or =vX.Y.Z-*=. A =-*= tag is a prerelease and is used for in-game gate builds. | Design |
| D-08 | One global "Unlock frames" toggle that is never saved (=ns.Mover=). There are no per-module =locked= options. | Design |
| D-09 | Action bars use our own =ActionBarButtonTemplate, SecureActionButtonTemplate= buttons and =SecureHandlerStateTemplate= headers, not LAB. Paging is the generic Blizzard mapping (page = 6 + bonus bar offset), which covers every class's forms, stances, stealth and shadowform. | Design |
| D-10 | The damage meter reskins =C_DamageMeter=: no CLEU, no sorting, no Lua arithmetic or comparisons on secret values. It redraws on its events, throttled to once per second (EllesmereUI's default =refreshRate=). | Verified + design |
| D-11 | Tooling: luacheck (=std = "lua51"= plus an explicit =read_globals=), stylua, =luac -p=, and busted for the =*Logic.lua= files only. | Design |
| D-12 | Every file starts with =local addonName, ns = ...=. Utilities live on =ns.*=. The pure =*Logic.lua= files read no WoW globals when they load. | Design |
| D-13 | Systems are x86_64-linux and aarch64-linux. The input is named =git-hooks= and follows nixpkgs. The formatter is =nixfmt=. | Convention |
| D-14 | EllesmereUI and Bartender4 are used only as references for techniques, facts and style values. Their code and media are never copied. | License |
| D-15 | Per-class default range spells (table below). An empty =spell= setting resolves to the player's class default when it is used, so one shared profile works on alts. A class with no entry resolves to =nil=, which shows white. | User ("cover each class") |
| D-16 | The damage meter follows the EllesmereUI default look with our own code and stock assets only: flat =Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8X8= bars, Blizzard class icons, =Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF=. The values are in the S9 style table. | User |
| D-17 | Workflow security: =permissions: {}= at the top. The check job gets =contents: read=; the release job gets =contents: write=. Actions are pinned by SHA with =persist-credentials: false=. =github.ref_name= is passed through =env=, and the release is published with the preinstalled =gh= CLI rather than a third-party release action. | CI rules |
| D-18 | EnemyCooldowns scope: enemy players only, curated built-in table, options enabled/iconSize/maxIcons/anchor/x/y. Detection is =UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED= on eligible nameplate tokens only; CLEU is never used. | User + design |
| D-19 | Spell identity: each curated entry has one base (rank 1) spell ID. =Lookup= matches the event spell ID first, then the client-resolved name (=C_Spell.GetSpellName=) against names resolved once from the base IDs. Every rank matches without rank-ID lists; both names come from the same client locale, so this isn't localisation. | Design |
| D-20 | State is keyed by =UnitGUID(unit)= in module-owned tables; the plate-to-container map is weak-keyed; nothing is written onto Blizzard frames. If S11 shows the GUID is secret and the user accepts, the key becomes the unit token and state is cleared on =NAME_PLATE_UNIT_REMOVED=. | Design |
| D-21 | The icon container is parented to the Blizzard base plate from =C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit=, never to =plate.UnitFrame= or S5 frames. No shared interface with S5 and no edits to S5/S6-owned files. | Design |
| D-22 | Cooldowns are fixed table values. Where S11's =GetSpellBaseCooldown= differs, the probe value replaces the table value before S12 starts. | Design |
| D-23 | Version rule for S12 and S13 (and any section run out of order): set =## Version= to the next minor of the TOC value at the start of the section (X.Y.Z → X.(Y+1).0); the fixed 0.3.0..0.8.0 of S4..S9 apply only when sections run in order. Tag =v<Version>-s12.<n>= / =-s13.<n>=. | Design |
| D-24 | EnemyCooldowns =enabled= defaults to =true=: it only adds icons and hides nothing. | Design |
| D-25 | A 0.5 s =C_Timer.NewTicker= prunes expired records and re-renders changed units; it runs only while the module is enabled. The Cooldown swipe animates itself. | Design |
| D-26 | The options UI is our own window that renders the registered AceConfig tree (=AceConfigRegistry:GetOptionsTable("MerrinxUI", "dialog", "MerrinxUI")=). The module contract is unchanged. AceConfigDialog stays embedded but goes unused after S17. There is no fallback command. | User + design |
| D-27 | The renderer mirrors the shipped AceConfigDialog 3.0 semantics (see the context subsection). Supported types: =group=, =toggle=, =range=, =select=, =input=, =execute=, =description=, =header=. Supported fields: =name=, =desc=, =order=, =get=, =set=, =func=, =values=, =min=, =max=, =step=, =disabled=, =hidden=, =confirm=, =confirmText=, =validate=, =usage=, =arg=, =handler=, =dialogHidden=/=guiHidden=, =dialogDisabled=/=guiDisabled=. Anything else is ignored; an unsupported type renders as a dim "Unsupported option: <name> (<type>)" row. Amended by D-39 (S22): =dialogControl= is supported on =select= with the single value ="LSM30_Font"=. | Design |
| D-28 | Pages: an optional "General" page (root-level non-group args, such as =unlock=) first, then the visible root groups in AceConfig order. Nested groups render inline as titled sections. Core sets =root.args.profiles.order = -1= so Profiles sorts last. | Design |
| D-29 | Refresh: the window schedules its own re-render after every =set=/=func= it calls, coalesced to the next frame with =C_Timer.After(0, ...)=. A =ConfigTableChange= callback for ="MerrinxUI"= covers external changes (=Mover:SetUnlocked=, the combat relock). Re-rendering waits while a slider is dragged or an EditBox has focus. The window never calls =NotifyChange=. | Design |
| D-30 | Confirmation uses an in-window overlay with Confirm and Cancel; =desc= is shown inline as dim text. No =StaticPopupDialogs=, =GameTooltip=, =UIDropDownMenu=/=MenuUtil= or =ScrollBox=: only plain =Frame=, =Button=, =Slider=, =EditBox= and =ScrollFrame=. | Design (taint surface) |
| D-31 | The window is unnamed, strata =FULLSCREEN_DIALOG=, 920×640, moves by its header, is clamped and isn't persisted. Esc closes it through =RegisterGameMenuEscHandler(GameMenuEscPriority.AddOn, fn)=. If S14 shows that registry doesn't work, the degraded variant names the frame =MerrinxUIOptionsFrame=, adds it to =UISpecialFrames= and calls =SetDontSavePosition(true)=. It works in combat and uses stock assets only. | Design (S14 decides the variant) |
| D-32 | Game-menu button: our own =MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate= button parented to =GameMenuFrame=, placed by a =hooksecurefunc(GameMenuFrame, "Layout", ...)= post-hook at the AddOns button's anchor. Pooled buttons at or below AddOns shift down by AddOns' height, and the menu grows by the same amount, computed from the height =Layout= just set. The button is hidden in gamepad UI and when no button reads =ADDONS=. The click calls =HideUIPanel(GameMenuFrame)= only out of combat, then =ns.addon:OpenOptions()=. We never use =GameMenuFrame:AddButton= or =MainMenuFrameMixin.AddButton=, never write fields on Blizzard frames, and never set =layoutIndex= on our button. The post-hook is the only D-20 exception. | User ("above AddOns") + design |
| D-33 | A section with no game gate doesn't bump =## Version= (S15). Sections with a game gate follow D-23: next minor at the section start (S16 → 0.3.0 and S17 → 0.4.0 if they run before S4). | Design |
| D-34 | The theme is our own =ns.Theme= table (values in the Theme subsection), close to EllesmereUI's observed values but with the stock =Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF= and =Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8X8=. The accent is the same teal as the S9 damage meter, (12, 210, 157)/255. Amended by D-40 (S19): the accent becomes light blue (102, 191, 255)/255, and S9 uses =ns.Theme.color.accent=. | User ("look at EllesmereUI") + license |
| D-D1 | Debug is an Ace module created with =ns.addon:NewModule("Debug")= (no mixins) and exposed as =ns.Debug=. It never calls =SetEnabledState=; it is always running, and "debug mode" is the stored flag =db.global.enabled=. Its options come from the existing =GetOptions= discovery; Core needs only slash routing. Debug never reads its own =db.profile=. | User + design |
| D-D2 | Public on/off API is =IsActive()= / =SetActive(value)=; never define =IsEnabled=, =Enable=, =Disable= or =SetEnabledState= on Debug (AceAddon mixins). | Verified collision |
| D-D3 | TOC: =Debug\DebugLogic.lua= and =Debug\Debug.lua= go directly after =Core.lua=, before =Util\MoverLogic.lua=, so Util (and later Options) files can call =ns.Debug:RegisterChecks= at file load. | Design |
| D-D4 | Debug state is account-wide: namespace ="Debug"=, defaults ={ global = { enabled = false } }=; =db.global.log= is created lazily, never as a default. | Design + AceDB behaviour |
| D-D5 | Two check sources, one shape: Ace modules implement =Module:GetDebugChecks()=; non-module runtime files call =ns.Debug:RegisterChecks(source, provider)= at file load. Both return a checks array at run time. A repeated =RegisterChecks= with the same source appends a provider. Provider calls are protected (=DebugLogic.RunSource=). | User ("append to the debug test") + design |
| D-D6 | Checks return a status and raw values; only =DebugLogic.Describe= turns values into text. Checks never =tostring= or concatenate game values. | Secret safety |
| D-D7 | Log window: AceGUI =Frame= + =MultiLineEditBox= (=DisableButton(true)=), one instance reused, released on close, snapshot (no live updates), no Esc hook. Every =ShowLog= runs =SetText= → =SetFocus= → =HighlightText()= in that order. AceGUI stays embedded after S17 (D-26). | Design; verified widget behaviour |
| D-D8 | Log cap 500 lines, line cap 400 chars, value cap 200 chars; timestamps =date("%H:%M:%S")=. | Design |
| D-D9 | Coverage is enforced twice: statically by =spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua= (TOC-driven with an exempt list, so new runtime files are included without anyone listing them) and at runtime by =Debug/coverage <Module>=. | User ("new features included automatically") |
| D-D10 | The pending nameplate game gate (E-NP-GAME) is folded into the SD4 release =v0.9.0-sd4.1=: the Nameplates checks replace its probe step 0, and its option steps are written out in G-SD4-GAME. Q-D2 lets the user override. | Design; user may override |
| D-D11 | Versions per D-23/D-33: SD1-SD3 have no game gate and don't bump; SD4 sets =0.9.0= (tag =v0.9.0-sd4.1=); SD5 sets =0.10.0= (tag =v0.10.0-sd5.1=, may share a release with the next section). If another section bumps first, apply D-23 at the section start instead. | D-23/D-33 |
| D-D12 | Combat checks read =#queue.order= inside =Combat.lua=; no new CombatQueue API. | Smallest diff |
| D-D13 | Privacy: log unit tokens, spell IDs and names, Blizzard frame names, option values and counts; never GUIDs, unit/player names or profile names. | SEC-S0 precedent |
| D-D14 | Trace calls pass only values the code already holds; a disabled-mode call costs one method call and an early return. Traces fire on state changes only, never per NPC plate or per enemy cast. | Performance, 500-line cap |
| D-D15 | SD1 has no game gate; the window, =date=, =C_AddOns= and taint are first exercised at the SD4 gate, keeping one round trip to the gaming PC (R-D12). | User preference |
| D-D16 | Module contract extension (from SD1): =GetDebugChecks()= is part of the module contract and =RegisterChecks= is required for non-module runtime files; load order becomes embeds, Core, Debug, Util, Options, Modules. Every later section (S7-S9, S14-S17) adds checks for its feature and runs its game gate as "=/mui test= + paste the log". | User |
| D-S18-1 | While auras are secret, hide the icons. Extend the existing early return in =UpdateDebuffs= to =if not profile.debuffs or C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret() then <existing hide loop>; return end=. No new helper; every caller (UNIT_AURA, StyleUnit, Refresh) is covered. Amended: from S25 on, D-46 supersedes this for runtime rendering. The guard stays until S25 lands. | User ("Hide while secret") + architect |
| D-S18-2 | No new event. Icons redraw on the first =UpdateDebuffs= call after secrecy ends (the next =UNIT_AURA=, plate add or option change). | User + smallest diff |
| D-S18-3 | Call =C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret()= directly, with no existence guard and no =issecretvalue= guard. It is documented on the forever branch, and =Nameplates/apis= plus the last value of =Nameplates/secret flags= prove at the game gate that it exists and isn't secret. | Architect; no speculative fallbacks |
| D-S18-4 | No per-aura guard and no =pcall=. A secret =auraInstanceID= would also throw in =ShouldUnitAuraInstanceBeSecret= (=AllowedWhenUntainted=), so the general predicate is the only gate. Any residual error is an S18 stop condition (R-S18-3). | Architect |
| D-S18-5 | Debug checks: =apis= adds ="C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret"=. While secret, =debuffs= keeps its icon and layout validation but reports both counts as ="auras secret"= without calling =GetUnitAuras=; the return positions don't change. =secret flags= skips both aura blocks while secret and appends the predicate value as its new last return value. | Architect (check contract) |
| D-S18-6 | =## Version: 0.13.0= per D-23; tag =v0.13.0-s18.1=. No TOC file lines change, so =/reload= is enough after installing. No trace calls (AC-D12, D-D14). No busted change. The literal =0.13.0= holds only if S18 starts while the TOC is =0.12.0=; if S22 has already bumped it, D-23 applies (next minor at the section start). | D-23 |
| D-35 | LibSharedMedia-3.0 comes from the official CurseForge SVN trunk, pinned to revision 182 through a =file+https= =flake = false= input (=libsharedmedia=). Only the single Lua file is shipped, as =Libs/LibSharedMedia-3.0/LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua=, loaded by a =<Script>= line after LibRangeCheck. No mirror, no =lib.xml=, no AceGUI-3.0-SharedMediaWidgets. | User ("LibSharedMedia-3.0") + architect (official source, verified hashes) |
| D-36 | Font option contract for every module that offers a font (RangeText now; S9 and later when they add one): the DB stores the LSM key; the option is =type = "select", dialogControl = "LSM30_Font", values = function() return ns.FontLogic.Values(LSM:HashTable("font")) end=; the font is applied with =LSM:Fetch("font", key, true)= plus a module fallback; legacy values go through =FontLogic.Migrate= in =OnInitialize= and =Refresh=. No shared runtime helper (=ns.Media=) yet; each consumer calls =LibStub("LibSharedMedia-3.0")= at file top. Revisit when a second module needs the fallback and callback logic. Superseded from S31 by D-72/D-76 (shared =ns.Media= helper, "Use global" default). | User ("look at ElvUI / EllesmereUI") + smallest diff |
| D-37 | Pure =MerrinxUI/Util/FontLogic.lua= → =ns.FontLogic= (=DEFAULT=, =IsPath=, =Values=, =Migrate=), busted-tested and coverage-exempt as a =*Logic.lua= file. TOC line =Util\FontLogic.lua= directly after =Util\Hider.lua=. | Design |
| D-38 | RangeText registers =LibSharedMedia_Registered= in =OnEnable= and unregisters it in =OnDisable=; the handler re-applies the font only when =mediatype == "font"= and =key == profile.font=. No trace call on it (D-D14). Superseded from S31 by D-74 (Media relays the callback as =MERRINXUI_MEDIA_CHANGED=; same key filter on the resolved key). | Design |
| D-39 | Font preview in the window: when =L.Member(root, path, "dialogControl") == "LSM30_Font"=, the closed select button and every list item are drawn in =LSM:Fetch("font", key, true)= at =Theme.size.value=; if that fails, the Theme font is used and the key is remembered for =OptionsWindow/font preview=. Every pooled select row and drop item resets its font on each configure/acquire. The preview never writes =fontUsed=. Amends D-27. | User ("like ElvUI / EllesmereUI") + architect |
| D-40 | Light-blue accent (102, 191, 255)/255: =accent = {0.4, 0.749, 1, 1}=, =switchOn= and =fill= with the same RGB at 0.75. It replaces the whole accent (title, sidebar active bar, switch-on track; =fill= for consistency although unused). The S9 style table uses =ns.Theme.color.accent= instead of the teal literal. Amends D-34 and A-22. | User ("whole accent, light blue") |
| D-41 | Versions: S19, S20 and S21 have no game gate and don't bump (D-33). S22 sets =## Version= to the next minor of the TOC at S22's start (=0.13.0= if S18 hasn't run, =0.14.0= if it has) and tags =v<Version>-s22.1=. G-S22-GAME covers S19-S22. The new TOC file (=Util\FontLogic.lua=) means a full client restart (R-09). | D-23/D-33, D-D15 precedent |
| D-42 | S18-S22 are closed =PASSED= on the user's instruction ("close the ones that are closed now") after the 0.14.0 test. Steps with no pasted evidence are listed in E-S18-GAME and E-S22-GAME as accepted by the user, not as verified. | User |
| D-43 | Plate unit token: read the S23 P1 field (expected =plate.unitToken=) directly and read-only at the four former =namePlateUnitToken= sites (=Nameplates.lua= =OnEnable=, =styled plates=, =target cast=; =EnemyCooldowns.lua= =RescanPlates=). No fallback chain. | S23 P1 + no speculative fallbacks |
| D-44 | Keep Blizzard's plate hidden with a post-hook on =UnitFrame:SetAlpha= (and on =SetAlphaFromBoolean= too if S23 P2 shows it). Weak-key =hookedUnitFrames= / =ownedUnitFrames= sets on the module. The frame is owned after =SetAlpha(0)= and un-owned before =SetAlpha(1)=. A file-local recursion lock; the hook never reads its arguments; an =alphaResets= counter for the =target plate= check. =AurasFrame= and the other children aren't touched; they disappear through the alpha cascade (S23 P2c). EllesmereUI's reparenting and =UnregisterAllEvents= are rejected (more Blizzard writes, taint risk). | User + architect |
| D-45 | (Superseded by D-55: S24 + S25 + S26 ship as =0.15.0=, tag =v0.15.0-s25.1=; see Handoff.) Versions per D-23: S24 =0.15.0= (tag =v0.15.0-s24.1=), S25 =0.16.0= (=v0.16.0-s25.1=). If one release carries both, there is one bump. S23 and S26 don't bump; S26's game step rides on the next release's gate. Neither S24 nor S25 adds a TOC file, so =/reload= is enough after installing. | D-23/D-33 |
| D-46 | Debuffs render through =AuraContainer= in one code path for every secrecy state. The manual icon pool and the runtime =C_UnitAuras= calls are removed. This supersedes D-S18-1/D-S18-2 for runtime and withdraws the S18 non-goal. Two renderers (container only while secret) are rejected: they would hand over on every combat change and need every option implemented twice. | User ("Show my debuffs on our plate in combat") + architect |
| D-47 | Container mapping: one container per styled frame, created in =CreateStyledFrame= (that runs in combat too, so S23 P5c-P5e are STOP rows; amended by D-61: waived until G-FU-NP); group key ="debuffs"=. =debuffsMineOnly= → =SetAuraGroupFilterString=; =debuffMax= → =SetAuraGroupMaxFrameCount=. =debuffSize= → =SetAuraGroupLayout(group, {elementWidth, elementHeight, elementSpacing})= if S23 P5f shows the icons resize, else =SetScale(size / 18)= with offsets divided by the scale (P5g). The anchor comes from =DebuffLayout(anchor, 1, 1, ...)=. Variant A: the container sizes to its content (centred). Variant B: fixed width, with left-aligned rows the user accepts. =UNIT_AURA= is removed if the container updates by itself; otherwise it only calls =UpdateAllAuras=. Option changes in combat go direct (P5b PASS) or through =ns.Combat= (P5b degraded). T-31 freezes the variant set from the S23 verdicts. | S23 + architect + T-29 source read |
| D-48 | Keep =NameplateLogic.DebuffLayout= and its specs unchanged. Runtime and the check call it with =i = n = 1=. busted stays 144. | Smallest diff |
| D-49 | =debuffs= check: same name and return positions; "shown" becomes the container's =IsShown()=. The check-contract forbidden list gains the container mutators (=SetUnit=, =UpdateAllAuras=, =AddAuraGroup=, =SetAuraGroup*=, =SetScale=) and loses =GetDebuffIcon=. From S24 it also lists =hooksecurefunc=. | Check contract |
| D-50 | (only if Q-11 = hide) =FontLogic.Values(hash, isUsable, keep)= stays pure. RangeText passes =fontProbe:SetFont(path, 12, "")= on a hidden, module-owned font string as the predicate, and the stored key as =keep=. A =nil= =isUsable= keeps today's behaviour (the 7 existing cases). | Q-11 default |
| D-62 | Level text (S27): a font string =frame.levelText= on the styled frame, =SetPoint("LEFT", frame.healthBar, "RIGHT", LEVEL_GAP, 0)= with =LEVEL_GAP = 2=; font =FONT= at =nameSize - 1=, ="OUTLINE"=, set in =ApplySize=. Level from =UnitEffectiveLevel(unit)=, classification from =UnitClassification(unit)=; a secret value becomes =nil= (no text). Text and colour from the pure =NameplateLogic.LevelText= / =LevelColor=; runtime calls =GetCreatureDifficultyColor(level)= directly (no existence guard, D-S18-3 precedent) only for a non-secret level > 0. Option =levelText = true= ("Show level", toggle). | User ("Outside, just right of the health bar") + architect |
| D-63 | Level events =LEVEL_EVENTS = { UNIT_LEVEL = "OnUnitLevel", UNIT_CLASSIFICATION_CHANGED = "OnUnitLevel", PLAYER_LEVEL_UP = "OnPlayerLevelUp" }=, toggled with =SetEventsEnabled(LEVEL_EVENTS, profile.levelText)= in =OnEnable= and =Refresh=. =UpdateLevel(unit, frame)= also runs from =StyleUnit= (after =UpdateName=) and the =Refresh= loop. All three events are documented on the forever branch. No trace calls. | Architect + forever docs |
| D-64 | Debuff offsets (S28): =debuffX = 0=, =debuffY = 0=, ranges −100..100 step 1, disabled while =debuffs= is off; added at the =UpdateDebuffs= call site (=x + profile.debuffX=, =y + profile.debuffY=). =DebuffLayout= and its specs stay unchanged (D-48). The duplicated =relativeTo= selection becomes one file-local, side-effect-free =DebuffAnchorFrame(frame, profile)= used by =UpdateDebuffs= and the =debuffs= check. S27 introduces it, because its RIGHT branch must return =frame.levelText= when =levelText= is on (debuffs anchored RIGHT then sit right of the level); S29 adds the LEFT → =frame.cc= branch. | User ("X/Y offset sliders") + architect |
| D-65 | CC icons (S29) live in a second =AuraContainer= per styled frame, =frame.cc=, group key =CC_GROUP = "cc"=, filter =NameplateLogic.CC_FILTER=, the "HARMFUL plus CROWD_CONTROL" string (any caster; literal in "Shared interfaces: S27-S30"). A second group in the debuff container is rejected: all groups share one flow layout, so they can't sit on different sides. =CreateDebuffContainer= is generalised to =CreateAuraContainer(frame, group, filter, maxCount, size)=, =ResizeDebuffButtons= to =ResizeAuraButtons(container, group, size)=; =InitializeDebuffButton= is reused. The debuff filter comes from =NameplateLogic.DebuffFilter(mineOnly, ccSeparate)=, with =ccSeparate = profile.cc and frame.cc ~= nil=, so a CC aura never shows twice. Options =cc = true=, =ccSize = 26= (10..48), =ccMax = 2= (1..4). | User ("CC larger icons on left") + architect |
| D-66 | CC anchoring: =UpdateCC(unit, frame)= anchors =frame.cc= with =DebuffLayout("LEFT", 1, 1, ccSize, DEBUFF_SPACING)= (container =RIGHT= to =frame= =LEFT=, x −2), sets filter, max and layout, =SetUnit=, =UpdateAllAuras=, =Show=; hidden when =cc= is off. Called from =StyleUnit= and =Refresh=. =AURA_EVENTS= are on when =debuffs or cc=; =OnUnitAura= also updates =frame.cc=. =ApplyPendingDebuffResize= resizes both groups; the single =debuffResizePending= flag covers both. With =debuffAnchor == "LEFT"= and =frame.cc= present and shown, =DebuffAnchorFrame= returns =frame.cc=. | Architect |
| D-67 | CooldownVoice detection: =SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN= → =Scan(true)=; observation per tracked spell: no info → =unknown=; =isActive= and =isOnGCD= → =gcd=; =isActive= → =cooling=; else =ready=. A 0.2 s =C_Timer.NewTicker= runs only while =L.AnyCooling(states)=; it calls =Scan(false)=, where =isActive= → =busy=, else =ready= (=isOnGCD= ignored outside the event). Transitions come from =L.Step=. Only the =NeverSecret= fields =isActive= / =isOnGCD= are read; =startTime=, =duration= and =modRate= never are. =SPELLS_CHANGED= → =Resolve()=; states of removed ids are dropped, new ids start =ready=. | Architect + forever docs |
| D-68 | Speech: the module's own "Voice" select (=values= from =C_VoiceChat.GetTtsVoices()=, =voiceID → name=, resolved by =L.ResolveVoice=; =C_TTSSettings.GetVoiceOptionID= isn't used). All spells that became ready in one scan are batched into one =C_VoiceChat.SpeakText(voiceID, L.Message(names), rate, volume, false)=. Defaults =rate = 0= (−10..10), =volume = 100= (0..100, step 5). | Architect |
| D-69 | One release for S27-S30: S27 sets =## Version: 0.16.0= at its start (D-23); S28-S30 don't bump; tag =v0.16.0-s30.1= (fallbacks =-s30.2=, ...). S27, S28 and S29 have no game gate of their own: after their code gates they stay =IN_PROGRESS= and are closed with S30 by G-S30-GAME (D-41 precedent). G-S30-GAME carries the G-FU-NP steps (D-61); a G-FU-NP failure reopens S25, not S27-S30. The TOC gains two files, so installing needs a full client restart (R-09). | D-23/D-33/D-41/D-61 |
| D-70 | Media addons: no code. A SharedMedia-style pack registers its fonts in the shared LSM registry, so they appear in the RangeText Font list (S26 hides any whose =SetFont= fails; D-38 re-applies a stored key registered late). The pack must be enabled (with "Load out of date AddOns" if its TOC lacks 16001) and the client fully restarted after installing it. Amended by D-72..D-79: from S31-S33 every module that draws text or bars uses LSM through =ns.Media= with a global choice and per-module overrides. | User question + researcher |
| D-71 | CooldownVoice pure logic (=Modules/CooldownVoiceLogic.lua= → =ns.CooldownVoiceLogic=): =ParseList(text, normalize)= takes the normaliser as an argument (runtime passes =ns.RangeTextLogic.NormalizeSpell=), so the Logic file has no cross-module dependency. The "Spells" option has no rejecting =validate=; unresolved tokens are reported, not refused, because a shared profile carries other classes' spells. The module is =NewModule("CooldownVoice", "AceEvent-3.0")=, =enabled = true= by default (an empty list does nothing; D-24 precedent). | User ("Spells I type in") + architect |
| D-72 | Media helper (S31): a new always-on Ace module =ns.addon:NewModule("Media", "AceEvent-3.0")= in =MerrinxUI/Util/Media.lua= (=ns.Media=), TOC line directly after =Util\FontLogic.lua=. Namespace ="Media"=, =profile = { font = "Friz Quadrata TT", statusbar = "Solid", optionsFont = "Arial Narrow" }=; it never calls =SetEnabledState=. API (interface block in "Shared interfaces: Media"): =GlobalKey=, =Resolve=, =Fetch=, =FontPath=, =SetFont=, =SetStatusBarTexture=, =Values=. It reads =self.db.profile= live and caches only the set of unusable font paths; no Media API is called before =OnEnable=. Supersedes D-36's "no shared helper yet" (this is the revisit) and D-50's per-module probe (the probe moves into Media). Statusbar media is withdrawn from the S19-S22 non-goals. | User ("all modules", "fonts and bar textures", "global + override") + architect |
| D-73 | "Use global" is the stored token =FontLogic.GLOBAL = "*"= (not =""=, which =Migrate= maps to the default; ="*"= sorts first in =L.SortedValues=; AceDB wildcards apply to keys only). =FontLogic.Resolve(key, globalKey)= maps =nil= and ="*"= to =globalKey=; =FontLogic.Values(hash, isUsable, keep, globalLabel)= adds =values["*"] = globalLabel= when a label is given (also for a nil hash). =Migrate= is unchanged and keeps ="*"=. Stored keys are never rewritten at load beyond today's legacy-path =Migrate= (packs load after MerrinxUI). | Architect |
| D-74 | Change propagation: Media relays =LibSharedMedia_Registered= for ="font"= / ="statusbar"= and its own global-setting changes as the AceEvent message =MERRINXUI_MEDIA_CHANGED(mediatype, key)=, =key= =nil= meaning "the global choice changed". A consumer re-applies only when =key == nil= or =key == ns.Media:Resolve(mediatype, ownKey)=; it registers the message in =OnEnable= (AceEvent drops it on disable). Profile changes need no relay: AceDB switches every namespace before =RefreshModules=, and each consumer's =Refresh= re-applies. No trace on the relay (D-D14); one =Media= trace line ="global", mediatype, key= on a global change. Supersedes D-38 for RangeText. | Architect |
| D-75 | The automatic =enabled state= check in =Debug.lua= runs only when =profile.enabled ~= nil=; always-on modules (Media) keep the =settings= INFO line. Amends AC-D10. | Architect (verified collision) |
| D-76 | Media contract for every module that draws text or bars, now and later: per-module keys (=font=, and =barTexture= where it has StatusBars) default to ="*"=; options are =select= with =dialogControl= ="LSM30_Font"= / ="LSM30_Statusbar"= and =values = function() return ns.Media:Values(mediatype, profile.key) end=; apply with =ns.Media:SetFont= / =SetStatusBarTexture=; listen to =MERRINXUI_MEDIA_CHANGED= per D-74. Applies to RangeText (S31), Nameplates (S33), S7 action bars (hotkey and count texts) and S9 damage meter (texts and bars; S9 keeps "Arial Narrow" / "Solid" as its own defaults per D-16, with "Use global" selectable). EnemyCooldowns and CooldownVoice draw no text or bars. Supersedes D-36 for new consumers. | User + architect |
| D-77 | Release: S31 sets =## Version= to the next minor after the last tagged release at its start (=0.17.0= when S27-S30 shipped as =0.16.0=; =0.18.0= if the Handoff's early =v0.16.0-s29.1= cut happened); S32 and S33 don't bump; tag =v<Version>-s33.1=; one game gate G-S33-GAME closes S31-S33 (D-41 precedent). The TOC gains =Util\Media.lua=, so a full client restart is needed (R-09). A separate release from S27-S30, whose gated packets stay untouched. | Architect + D-23/D-33 |
| D-78 | Options window (S32; amends D-27 and D-39): =dialogControl= ="LSM30_Statusbar"= is supported on =select=: each drop item gets a module-owned =item.preview= texture (inset 1 px, below the text, =SetVertexColor(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)=) shown with =ns.Media:Fetch("statusbar", key)= in statusbar lists (the ="*"= row shows the global texture) and hidden in every other list, reset on every acquire; the closed button shows text only. The window font is resolved once at the top of =build()= into a file-local =windowFont= (=ns.Media:FontPath(optionsFont)=), used by =setFont= with today's =Theme.font= → FRIZQT fallback, so a change applies after =/reload=. =setPreviewFont= resolves through =ns.Media:Fetch= (S31 moves this one line, so ="*"= previews in the global font). | User + architect |
| D-79 | Nameplates adoption (S33): =profile.font = "*"=, =profile.barTexture = "*"= (options "Font" order 21, "Bar texture" order 22). =ApplySize= sets name, health, power, cast and level texts with =ns.Media:SetFont= and the health, power and cast StatusBars with =ns.Media:SetStatusBarTexture=, swapping a bar's texture only when the resolved path changed (=frame.barPath=) and re-applying the cast colour after a swap; backgrounds and the highlight stay =WHITE8X8=. =initializedDebuffButtons= stores the count FontString as its value (=[button] = count=) so count fonts of debuff and CC buttons follow changes (re-applied only while auras aren't secret; otherwise with the existing pending path). =local FONT= is removed; =self.appliedFont= records the applied text font path for the =media= check. | Architect |

* Assumptions (unverified until the named gate)
- A-01 (S0): the probe values match the facts above (interface, APIs, frame globals, class list, spell IDs).
- A-02 (S3 gate): LibRangeCheck's =GetRange("target")= works in combat on Forever and gives plausible yards.
- A-03 (S3): =FontString:SetAlphaFromBoolean= exists.
- A-04 (S6): duration objects and =StatusBar:SetTimerDuration= exist.
- A-05 (S9):
  - =AbbreviateNumbers=, =string.format= and =FontString:SetText= accept secret values;
  - =combatSources= arrives sorted;
  - with =damageMeterEnabled=0= the API still works;
  - =CLASS_ICON_TCOORDS= exists.
- A-06 (S7): buttons built from =ActionBarButtonTemplate= refresh after =_childupdate= changes their action.
- A-07 (S7): Forever's bonus bar offsets match Blizzard's mapping:

  | Class | Form | Offset |
  |---|---|---|
  | Druid | cat | 1 |
  | Druid | prowl | 2 |
  | Druid | bear | 3 |
  | Druid | moonkin | 4 |
  | Rogue | stealth | 1 |
  | Warrior | battle | 1 |
  | Warrior | defensive | 2 |
  | Warrior | berserker | 3 |
  | Priest | shadowform | 1 |
- A-08 (S2): the user has a GitHub repository for this project and pushes =main= and the tags.
- A-09 (S11): =UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED= fires for hostile player nameplate units, including instant casts and item uses, with a readable =spellID= both in and out of combat.
- A-10 (S11): =UnitGUID=, =UnitIsPlayer= and =UnitCanAttack= are readable (not secret) for nameplate units.
- A-11 (S11): a higher-rank cast resolves to the same name as rank 1.
- A-12 (S11): a =CooldownFrameTemplate= child of an enemy plate works in combat without taint.
- A-13 (S11): =C_Spell.GetSpellTexture= exists.
- A-14 (S11): =GetSpellBaseCooldown= exists (if not, cooldowns stay UNVERIFIED and are checked by watching the swipe).
- A-15 (S11): enemy player plates are not forbidden in the open world or in battlegrounds.
- A-16 (S14): =Blizzard_GameMenu= is not load-on-demand, so =GameMenuFrame= exists when our file loads.
- A-17 (S14): the live client lays out the game menu as the =forever= source does (absolute =TOPLEFT= anchors, equal button heights), and the AddOns button exists with text =ADDONS=.
- A-18 (S14): =RegisterGameMenuEscHandler= and =GameMenuEscPriority.AddOn= exist on the live client and let an addon frame close on Esc without opening the menu.
- A-19 (S14/S16): =HideUIPanel(GameMenuFrame)= called from our code out of combat and outside gamepad UI leaves no MerrinxUI taint and no blocked action.
- A-20 (S14): =Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF= exists on Forever.
- A-21: placing our button at the top of the AddOns section is what the user means by "under Options, above AddOns": the section gap sits above our button, with no gap between it and AddOns. On a client with Shop or Rewards between Options and AddOns, our button still sits directly above AddOns.
- A-22 (amended by D-40): the light-blue accent (102, 191, 255)/255 is the shade the user wants. The shade is the planner's pick for "light blue"; changing it is three lines in =Theme.lua= (=accent=, =switchOn=, =fill=) and is checked at G-S22-GAME step 3.
- A-D1 (SD4 gate): =date=, =GetLocale=, =C_AddOns.GetAddOnMetadata= exist on Forever. A missing =C_AddOns= gives FAIL in =Debug/version=, not an error; =date= missing is a stop condition.
- A-D2 (SD4 gate): =HighlightText()= with no arguments after =SetText= and =SetFocus= selects all (Ctrl+A is the fallback); escaped =||= copies as =||=.
- A-D3 (SD4 gate): =FontString:GetFont()= returns the path, a size that rounds to the set size and the flags; =GetTextColor= / =GetStatusBarColor= return the set colour within 0.01. If not, those checks become INFO (remediation, not redesign).
- A-D4 (SD4 gate): =frame:GetPoint(1)= on Mover-managed frames returns =(point, UIParent, point, x, y)= with x/y within 0.5 of the saved values.
- A-D5 (SD4): the =Nameplates/secret flags= list is reconstructed from the calls in =Nameplates.lua=; the original NP probe block is not recorded.
- A-23 (S18 gate): =C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret= exists on the live Forever client, returns a non-secret boolean, and is =true= whenever =GetUnitAuras= would throw for tainted code (in combat, and in encounter, challenge-mode and PvP-match restrictions).
- A-24 (S18 gate): when the predicate is =false=, =GetUnitAuras= on a hostile nameplate returns readable contents, as observed out of combat at G-SD4-GAME.
- A-25 (S22 gate): =C_UIFileAsset.IsKnownFile= exists on the live Forever client, so LibSharedMedia-3.0 r182 loads (it reads that function at load).
- A-26 (S22 gate): the LSM western default font files exist on Forever (=MORPHEUS_CYR=, =SKURRI_CYR=, =2002=, =2002B=, =ARHei=, =ARKai_T=, =ARKai_C=, =NIM_____=, =K_Pagetext=), and =FontString:SetFont= returns false for a missing file. Missing files show up in =OptionsWindow/font preview=, not as errors.
- A-27: stored RangeText fonts today are either the default or a stock path, so silently resetting an unknown hand-typed path to "Friz Quadrata TT" is acceptable.
- A-28: the client locale is western (enUS). On other locales LSM registers a different key set; migration still falls back to the default.
- Resolved by the 0.14.0 log:
  - A-23 is confirmed. =ShouldAurasBeSecret= exists and returns a plain boolean: =false= out of combat, =true= in combat.
  - A-25 is confirmed. LSM loads (=Debug/libraries= 13).
  - A-26 is resolved. On Forever enUS, =SetFont= fails with no error for the files behind "AR CrystalzcuheiGBK Demibold", "AR ZhongkaiGBK Medium (Combat)", "MoK", "Morpheus", "Nimrod MT" and "Skurri". The other western defaults set fine.
- A-29 (S23 P1): on Forever the plate's unit token field is =unitToken=; =namePlateUnitToken= is never set.
- A-30 (S23 P2): Blizzard's in-combat alpha reset is a Lua =SetAlpha= / =SetAlphaFromBoolean= call on the same =UnitFrame= instance, so a post-hook sees it.
- A-31 (S23 P2c): =UnitFrame.AurasFrame= is a child of =UnitFrame=, so alpha 0 also hides Blizzard's debuffs.
- A-32 (S23 P3-P5): tainted addon code can create, re-point and reconfigure an =AuraContainer= in combat. The container updates by itself (or can be told to) and renders secret auras with a swipe.
- A-33 (S26): =SetFont= returning false means the font can't be used on this client, and the result stays the same for the session.
- A-34 (G-S30-GAME): =UnitEffectiveLevel= and =UnitClassification= return non-secret values for hostile nameplate units in and out of combat (docs mark only their arguments). =Nameplates/level= reports a secret as INFO, so a wrong assumption shows as "no level", not an error.
- A-35 (G-S30-GAME): =GetCreatureDifficultyColor= is loaded on Forever and returns a table with numeric =r=, =g=, =b= (=QuestDifficultyColors= shape on the forever branch). =Nameplates/apis= lists it; a missing global is a stop condition.
- A-36 (G-S30-GAME): =SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN= fires when a listed spell's cooldown starts, with =isActive= true and =isOnGCD= false for that spell; =isActive= turns false when the cooldown ends, also while cooldowns are restricted, and polling sees it.
- A-37: =C_Spell.GetSpellInfo(name)= resolves only spells this character knows (other classes' names stay unresolved); =C_Spell.GetSpellInfo(id)= resolves any valid id, and =GetSpellCooldown(id)= for a spell the character doesn't know returns info with =isActive= false (the spell never announces).
- A-38: TTS rate is −10..10 and volume 0..100 (Blizzard's TTS settings ranges); the gaming PC has at least one TTS voice (Windows voices). =CooldownVoice/voices= shows the count.
- A-39 (G-S30-GAME): the client accepts =CROWD_CONTROL= and the =!= negation in =AuraContainer= filters and in =C_UnitAuras.GetUnitAuras=, and a CC aura matches =CROWD_CONTROL= on enemy plates. =Nameplates/cc filters= proves it out of combat.
- A-40: =SpeakText= with =overlap= false queues (or replaces) rather than talking over itself; a batched message per scan keeps the queue short either way.
- A-41 (G-S33-GAME): =StatusBar:SetStatusBarTexture= with a new file may reset the bar's colour; re-applying the cast colour after a swap (D-79) plus the existing =UpdateColor= / =UpdatePower= calls restore every bar colour. =Nameplates/health colour= and a visual line on the cast bar check it.
- A-42: a SharedMedia-style addon's fonts and statusbars register with the shared LSM instance during its own load (after MerrinxUI's, alphabetically), so they appear in the lists the next time a list opens and stored keys apply through the D-74 relay.

* Open questions
- Q-01 (resolved in S0): =Gako358/merrinxui=, configured as =origin=.
- Q-02 (resolved in S0): Druid and Mage now, the other classes later. Classes you can't test stay UNVERIFIED in the S3 and S7 per-class evidence.
- Q-03: Is it OK that bars 1-3 are enabled by default out of 8, given that ActionBars hides all Blizzard bars?
- Q-05: Should S10 (LuaLS) be done?
- Q-06: Can the user get a duel partner, a battleground, or both on Forever? S11 needs at least a duel partner; the S13 multi-enemy step needs a battleground. Without either, S11 is =BLOCKED=.
- Q-07: Keep S13 separate from S12 (recommended: S12 alone proves detection), or merge them into one gate?
- Q-08: Run the options track (S14-S17) before S4 (recommended: every later module then gets checked in the new window as it lands), or after S13? S14 is user-only and can run at any time either way.
- Q-D1 (non-blocking): active tests (toggle a setting or create a test frame, then revert) are a non-goal; they would need their own design (combat safety, revert on error).
- Q-D2 (confirm at SD4 start): fold the nameplate game gate into =v0.9.0-sd4.1= (D-D10, recommended), or cut =v0.8.0-np.1= now with the manual probes? SD4 proceeds unchanged either way.
- Q-11 (S26, resolved: "hide", user answer at the S23-S26 run start; D-50 applies): "Six fonts in the RangeText Font list can't be loaded on this client: AR CrystalzcuheiGBK Demibold, AR ZhongkaiGBK Medium (Combat), MoK, Morpheus, Nimrod MT, Skurri. Should they be hidden from the list (your selected font always stays listed), or should the list stay as it is?" Recommended default: hide (D-50). S26 doesn't start without an answer.
- Resolved (media pass):
  - Q-14 (Nameplates font select): answered more broadly: "I want all modules to be able to benefit if I download an addon like SharedMedia" → S31-S33 (D-72..D-79); the shared helper is due (architect pass done).
  - Q-18 (media types): "Fonts and bar textures" (no sounds).
  - Q-19 (organisation): "One global choice + per-module override".
- Resolved (S27-S30 planning pass):
  - Q-15 (CooldownVoice spells): "Spells I type in": a comma-separated names/IDs field, empty by default (D-71).
  - Q-16 (debuff position): "X/Y offset sliders" next to the existing anchor (D-64).
  - Q-17 (level position): "Outside, just right of the health bar" (D-62).
- Resolved:
  - Q-09 (font list source, S20-S22): LibSharedMedia-3.0, like ElvUI and EllesmereUI, rather than a built-in list of stock fonts.
  - Q-10 (accent scope, S19): replace the whole accent (title, sidebar active bar, switch-on track, =fill=), not only the logo text.
  - Q-04 (per-second): yes, ="total (per-second)"=, following EllesmereUI's default number format.
  - Former Q-01 (local AddOns path): no longer needed, because releases replace local deploy.

* Risks
| ID   | Risk | Mitigation |
|------+------+------------|
| R-01 | The interface or API surface differs from expectations | Run the S0 probes first; stop on any mismatch. |
| R-02 | Secret values make Lua comparisons throw | Branch only after an =issecretvalue= check; use widget APIs that accept secrets. |
| R-03 | Taint or =ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED= | Use the Bartender-style hide technique and the combat queue; check =taintLog=; stop on taint. |
| R-04 | LibRangeCheck mis-detects Forever as retail (=WOW_PROJECT_ID=) and gives wrong or =?= yards | Probe =WOW_PROJECT_ID= in S0 and test in-game at S3. If yards are wrong, stop (a fork or replacement means a replan). |
| R-05 | Untracked files are invisible to Nix | User-approved =git add -N=. |
| R-06 | Release supply chain: a mutable action tag or an over-broad token | Pin SHAs, least-privilege permissions, no third-party release action, =persist-credentials: false=. |
| R-07 | The tag and TOC version drift apart | The workflow checks that they match. |
| R-08 | The =damageMeterEnabled= CVar is account-wide and persists | The =global.meterCVarOwned= flag; the CVar is restored when the toggle is turned off. |
| R-09 | A newly added TOC file needs a full client restart | Stated in every game gate. |
| R-10 | The actionBars toggle without our bars leaves no bars | Warned in the option description. |
| R-11 | A per-class default spell is wrong for a spec (for example a feral druid with Wrath) | The user can override the spell; per-spec defaults are a non-goal. |
| R-12 | The zip timestamps make it non-deterministic | Copy, =touch -d 1980-01-02=, sort the input list, use =zip -X -D=. |
| R-13 | Enemy =spellID= is secret on Forever, or the event doesn't fire for hostile players | S11 stop rows; replan with the user before any code. |
| R-14 | Talent or item reductions make the shown timer too long | Non-goal; accepted. |
| R-15 | Casts made while the caster's plate isn't shown are missed | Accepted (nameplate-only detection). |
| R-16 | Two curated entries share a name, so name matching is ambiguous | P3 shows duplicates; one row is dropped before S12; busted checks ID uniqueness. |
| R-17 | Icons overlap the S5 health bar or name | Anchor and offset options. |
| R-18 | Battleground event volume | O(1) =self.units[unit]= filter before any other work; ticker work bounded by players × entries. |
| R-19 | Taint from child frames on Blizzard plates | No fields written on Blizzard frames; =taint.log= checked at every gate; stop on taint. |
| R-20 | PvP trinket IDs are low confidence | P3d checks them; unresolved rows are dropped. |
| R-21 | Hiding the game menu from our code runs its =OnHide= tainted (forbidden in gamepad UI) and is blocked in combat | =HideUIPanel= only out of combat; button hidden in gamepad UI; S14 P7; =taint.log= at G-S16. |
| R-22 | A Blizzard game-menu layout change breaks our placement | S14 P3; =GameMenuLogic.Plan= spec; the post-hook is idempotent because every =Layout= re-anchors and re-sizes first; stop on a mismatch. |
| R-23 | The renderer drifts from AceConfig semantics, so a later module's options or the Profiles page misbehave | busted examples mirror AceConfigDialog; the Profiles game-gate step; D-27 subset in the module contract. |
| R-24 | A re-render recycles a control while the user is using it (slider drag, EditBox focus) | D-29 deferral; G-S17 steps 2-5. |
| R-25 | =/mui= regresses while the window is half-built | S17 switches =/mui= atomically with every widget type; until then =OpenOptions= opens AceConfigDialog. |
| R-26 | Esc doesn't close the window, or opens the game menu as well | S14 P8/P9 picks the variant; G-S17 step 5. |
| R-27 | Other addons that move game-menu buttons overlap ours | Non-goal; documented. |
| R-D1 | Defining =IsEnabled= on Debug breaks AceAddon state | D-D2; verifier grep gate |
| R-D2 | A secret leaks into a string, the log or SavedVariables | D-D6; =Describe= checks =isSecret= first; SEC gate reads every check |
| R-D3 | A check has a side effect (taint, CVar, frame) | Forbidden-call list; verifier grep *and* a read of every check body; SEC gate |
| R-D4 | Util files reach =ns.Debug= before it exists | D-D3; the coverage spec asserts the order; Util code looks =ns.Debug= up at call time |
| R-D5 | Traces on hot paths cost frame time or flood the 500-line log | AC-D12, D-D14; hot-path grep gate |
| R-D6 | =IterateModules= order is random | =SortSources= |
| R-D7 | New files invisible to =nix flake check= | =git add --intent-to-add= with user approval before the verifier runs (R-05) |
| R-D9 | Esc does not close the log window | Close button; revisit after S14/D-31 |
| R-D10 | Checks reach into module internals and drift when a module changes | Checks live in the module's own file; a module edit updates its checks in the same section |
| R-D11 | A check FAILs because of a wrong expectation, not a bug | Every FAIL line carries raw values; the lead fixes the check or the code with the user's paste as evidence, never by deleting or loosening a check without saying why |
| R-D12 | SD1-SD3 code first runs in game at SD4 | Accepted (D-D15); SD4 steps 1-3 exercise the framework before feature steps |
| R-D13 | A throwing provider would lose the whole log | =RunSource= protects provider calls (AC-D5) |
| R-D14 | The 0.5 s EnemyCooldowns ticker makes icon counts lag at expiry | =icons <unit>= SKIPs when a record expired within the last =2 * TICK_INTERVAL= (1 s; R-SD3-01: the ticker fires on the first frame after each period) |
| R-S18-1 | UX: applying a debuff usually starts combat, so with the default "only mine" nameplate debuffs will rarely show in open-world PvE, and never in encounters, challenge mode or PvP | Accepted by the user ("Hide while secret"). The 12.1 =AuraContainer= follow-up was declined; it would need its own probe section. |
| R-S18-2 | Icons drawn just before secrecy stay up until that plate's next aura update. Their swipe keeps running, but they can be stale | Accepted (D-S18-2). The first in-combat =UNIT_AURA= hides them. =debuffs= keeps validating their layout, so this can't FAIL the check. |
| R-S18-3 | =GetUnitAuras= has =ConditionalSecretContents=: some contents could be secret, or a call could throw, while the predicate is false | Stop condition at G-S18-GAME (any error at the =GetAuraDuration= / =GetAuraApplicationDisplayCount= lines). Replan with the user, not a speculative guard (D-S18-4). |
| R-S18-4 | =ShouldAurasBeSecret= is missing or returns a secret on the live client | =Nameplates/apis= FAIL or a nil-index error; the last value of =secret flags= shows =<secret>=. Both are stop conditions. |
| R-28 | =C_UIFileAsset= is missing on Forever, so LSM r182 errors at load and every LSM consumer breaks | A-25 (forever-branch docs); G-S22-GAME step 3. Stop and replan the LSM source with the user. |
| R-29 | Another addon embeds a different LSM MINOR; LibStub keeps the highest | Same public API; accepted. |
| R-30 | Supply chain: the fetched LSM file is tampered with or changes | Official source, revision-pinned URL, locked narHash and file sha256, diff against the WeakAuras 5.22.1 release copy at G-S20-SEC. |
| R-31 | CurseForge SVN is unreachable when Nix or CI fetches the input | Stop condition; no fallback mirror without user approval. After the first fetch the store path is cached locally. |
| R-32 | Pooled select rows or drop items keep a preview font after the Font list | Reset on every configure and acquire (D-39); AC-83; G-S22-GAME step 5. |
| R-33 | Migration drops a hand-typed custom font path | Accepted (A-27). |
| R-34 | Long font names or very large lists (SharedMedia packs) | Accepted; the existing =dropMax= scroll handles length. |
| R-35 | Version clash with the S18 literal =0.13.0= | D-41 and the amended D-S18-6. |
| R-37 | A hook on a Blizzard frame instance writes an instance key, can't be removed, and runs our code inside Blizzard's call path | Minimal hook body, no argument read, inert when not owned (AC-88/AC-89); taint.log at G-S24-GAME; stop on any taint line. |
| R-38 | Blizzard resets alpha every frame, so we re-zero every frame (CPU) | The =alphaResets= value in =target plate=. Report a large count at the gate and replan if it is excessive. |
| R-39 | The token fix makes =styled plates= and =target cast= work for the first time and exposes real drift (tap-denied, attackability changes without =UNIT_FACTION=) | Report the raw values, then fix the check or the code with evidence (R-D11). Never loosen a check silently. |
| R-40 | The =AuraContainer= API is unverified on the live client and may change between Forever builds. Intrinsic forbidden aspects may block =SetScale=, =SetPoint= or =Hide= | T-29 source read (done at planning, pinned commit), S23 P3-P5, stop on any error. |
| R-41 | Blizzard's =AuraButton= looks different from the 0.14.0 icons, and it can't be skinned (forbidden aspects) | Accepted UX; the user comments at G-S25-GAME step 7. |
| R-42 | Layout limits: LEFT may grow away from the plate (first icon farthest); TOP centring may need a content-sized container | Informational P4 rows; the D-47 variants are confirmed by the user. |
| R-43 | Container geometry is restricted or secret for the checks | =readPoint= returns "points restricted" (existing pattern). |
| R-44 | Between S24 and S25 no debuffs show in combat at all: ours are hidden by S18, Blizzard's by S24 | Stated at G-S24-GAME step 2. Release S25 soon, or ship S24 and S25 in one release (D-45). |
| R-36 | LGPL v2.1 notice ships only in the file header (trunk has no LICENSE file) | Mitigated. Superseded by the SEC-S22-01 remediation (S22-RB1): the LGPL-2.1 text ships as =Libs/LibSharedMedia-3.0-LICENSE.txt=, copied from the locked nixpkgs =spdx-license-list-data.text= (=text/LGPL-2.1-only.txt=); the =.lua= still ships unmodified. |
| R-45 | The C side rejects =!CROWD_CONTROL= or =CROWD_CONTROL= in container or =GetUnitAuras= filters, or CC auras don't match it | =Nameplates/cc filters= out of combat. Pre-approved fallback F-CC1: if only the =!= sums are wrong and the plain =CROWD_CONTROL= calls work, =DebuffFilter= ignores =ccSeparate= (a CC you cast shows in both places). An error from =CROWD_CONTROL= itself is a stop: S29 =BLOCKED=, replan. |
| R-46 | Registering an event the client doesn't know throws in AceEvent and breaks =OnEnable= | All five new events are documented on the forever branch (context subsection); the first =/mui test= after install shows any enable failure as an =enabled state= FAIL. Stop condition. |
| R-47 | =GetCreatureDifficultyColor= isn't loaded on Forever or returns another shape | =apis= path; =LevelColor= falls back to white when the argument isn't a table with numeric =r= (pure, specced). A missing global is a stop. |
| R-48 | =SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN= / =isActive= behave differently on Forever (no event at cast, =isActive= stuck, GCD reported as active without =isOnGCD=) | =CooldownVoice/cooldown info= and =ticker= values at G-S30-GAME step 5; stop and replan, no speculative timers. |
| R-49 | No TTS voice on the gaming PC, or speech is muted by the client's voice settings | =CooldownVoice/voices= count; "Test voice" step. A count of 0 is a stop (the user installs a Windows voice). |
| R-50 | Several spells ready at once talk over each other or queue late | Batched message per scan (D-68); A-40 checked by ear at the gate. |
| R-51 | Overlaps: EnemyCooldowns anchored RIGHT vs the level text, LEFT vs the CC icons; level text vs a wide health bar text at small widths | Accepted; EnemyCooldowns has X/Y sliders, debuffs get them in S28. The user comments at G-S30-GAME. |
| R-52 | A second container per plate doubles in-combat pool growth (SEC-RB3-01, =initializeFrame= outside =pcall=) | CC container is created with the debuff one in =CreateStyledFrame=; G-FU-NP step 1 plus the CC step in combat exercise it; any error is a stop. |
| R-53 | A spell whose info disappears (talent swap, =nil= info) keeps the ticker running forever | =Step(cooling, unknown)= stays =cooling= but =SPELLS_CHANGED= → =Resolve()= drops removed ids; =ticker= check shows a stuck count. Reported at the gate; fix with evidence. |
| R-54 | The CC container's size is 0 when it has no auras, so LEFT-anchored debuffs chained to it sit at the plate edge, and grow left over the CC icons once a CC appears | That is the intended layout (debuffs left of CC icons). If the gate shows overlap, the user moves debuffs with the S28 offsets. |
| R-55 | A media addon registers a file the client doesn't know; LSM reports it through =geterrorhandler()= | A BugSack line attributed to LibSharedMedia / the pack, not MerrinxUI; informational at G-S33-GAME, not a stop. |
| R-56 | =SetStatusBarTexture= resets bar colours (A-41), leaving the cast bar white | Swap only on a path change, re-apply the cast colour, G-S33-GAME visual line; stop if more than that is needed. |
| R-57 | A burst of =LSM:Register= calls (a large pack) triggers many relays | Consumers filter on the resolved key (D-74), so only a matching key re-applies. |
| R-58 | The resolved global font is unusable on this client (profile imported from elsewhere) | =ns.Media:SetFont= falls back to LSM's default font with one chat line per key; =Media/global font= FAIL shows it. |
| R-59 | Another addon calls LSM =SetGlobal=, which silently wins in =LSM:Fetch= for every addon | Non-goal; the check values show the fetched path, so a mismatch is visible. |
| R-60 | ruRU and Asian clients: the LSM default font differs from Friz Quadrata, so their default look changes | Intended (LSM's locale default); the user is enUS (A-28). |

* Stop conditions
- An S0 probe contradicts something the section relies on: the class list, a default spell ID, an API or a frame global.
- A path in a flake input differs from this plan. Report the real tree.
- A git-hooks hook name differs.
- An action SHA can't be confirmed as a commit of the stated tag.
- The user refuses =git add -N=, or a step needs a push or tag that only the user can do.
- A game gate shows a Lua error, =ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED=, forbidden-frame errors, or MerrinxUI entries in =Logs/taint.log=. Report the text verbatim.
- An assumed API is missing.
- A change needs writes outside the role's owned paths, or would copy third-party UI code or media.
- Any need for Haskell tooling or =direnv=. None is expected.
- EnemyCooldowns (S11-S13): any STOP row in the S11 verdict table; any need to edit S5/S6-owned files or =flake.nix=; any need for rank-ID lists; Q-06 unanswered or no hostile player available for a game gate (mark the section =BLOCKED=).
- Options track (S14-S17): any STOP row in the S14 verdict table; any need to write a field on a Blizzard frame or table beyond AC-37; a module's options needing a type outside D-27; a render loop; Esc not closing the window; any =flake.nix=, =embeds.xml= or =.github= change.
- Debug track (SD1-SD5): a check would need a side effect, a new module field or a change outside its owned paths; a trace call would sit on a frequent path (AC-D12); =date= missing on Forever or AceGUI widgets missing at runtime; the user refuses =git add --intent-to-add= for the new files; any =flake.nix=, =flake.lock= or =.github/= change; SEC-NP-02 (=SetTexture= rejects a secret aura icon) during the folded nameplate steps.
- S18: =C_Secrets= or =ShouldAurasBeSecret= is missing (an =apis= FAIL or a nil-index error); the predicate value logs as =<secret>=; any BugSack error from =Nameplates.lua= in combat, including at the =GetAuraDuration= / =GetAuraApplicationDisplayCount= lines (R-S18-3); a =Logs/taint.log= MerrinxUI line; the busted count changes; any edit outside =MerrinxUI/Modules/Nameplates.lua=, =.luacheckrc= and line 4 of =MerrinxUI/MerrinxUI.toc=.
- Font dropdown and accent (S19-S22):
  - an LSM load error (=C_UIFileAsset= nil) or any BugSack line from =LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua=;
  - a Font list item shows a path or blank text; Outline or Anchor is drawn in a preview font; migration didn't select "Arial Narrow" at G-S22-GAME step 3;
  - the locked narHash or the shipped file's sha256 differs from AC-76; =flake.lock= changes any other node; the supply-chain diff shows more than line 4; CurseForge SVN is unreachable;
  - AceConfigRegistry rejects =dialogControl= (an error from =RegisterOptionsTable=);
  - a busted count other than the section's expected count; an edit outside the section's owned paths; any need to edit =OptionsLogic.lua=, =Core.lua=, =.luacheckrc=, =.github/= or =Nameplates.lua=;
  - the user refuses =git add --intent-to-add= for the two new S21 files.
- S23:
  - any STOP row in the S23 verdict table (mark the dependent section =BLOCKED= and replan with the user);
  - a probe line errors because of a wrong signature (fix that line, then rerun only that row);
  - the user can't reach a hostile mob.
- S24:
  - the S23 P1 or P2 verdict is STOP;
  - luacheck needs a new global;
  - a stack overflow or recursion in the hook;
  - in combat, =target plate= shows alpha 1 with hooked =true= (a C-side reset after all);
  - a Blizzard plate or Blizzard debuffs visible in combat at G-S24-GAME;
  - any =ADDON_ACTION_*=, BugSack or taint.log MerrinxUI line;
  - busted ≠ 144;
  - edits outside the owned paths, or any need to touch =AurasFrame=, =SetParent= or =UnregisterAllEvents=.
- S25:
  - any S23 P3-P5 STOP row: S25 becomes =BLOCKED= and the S18 behaviour stays;
  - an error from =CreateFrame("AuraContainer", ...)= or any container method at runtime;
  - icons missing in combat, or not on our plate;
  - a forbidden-aspect error from =AuraButton=;
  - busted ≠ 144 (147 if S26 landed first);
  - any edit to =NameplateLogic.lua=, the specs or =EnemyCooldowns.lua=.
- S26:
  - Q-11 unanswered or answered "leave";
  - =SetFont= throws instead of returning false;
  - the selected font disappears from the list;
  - busted ≠ B+3.
- S27-S30:
  - busted differs from the section's count (S27 149, S28 149, S29 150, S30 156), or any existing spec needs a change (=DebuffLayout= specs stay as they are, D-48);
  - an edit outside the section's owned paths; any need to touch =EnemyCooldowns.lua=, =Core.lua=, =OptionsWindow.lua=, =OptionsLogic.lua=, =flake.nix=, =flake.lock=, =.github/= or =embeds.xml=; a new option type outside D-27;
  - the user refuses =git add --intent-to-add= for the three S30 files;
  - at G-S30-GAME: an =enabled state= FAIL or an "unknown event" error (R-46); =Nameplates/apis= missing =GetCreatureDifficultyColor=; an error from a filter containing =CROWD_CONTROL= (R-45; the broken-=!=-sums case alone triggers F-CC1, not a stop); =cc icons= count 0 while the user sees a CC on the target out of combat; =CooldownVoice/cooldown info= shows =isActive= not boolean; =CooldownVoice/voices= count 0; no speech at a listed spell's cooldown end or speech on a GCD-only spell; any taint, =ADDON_ACTION_*= or BugSack line from new code.
- S31-S33:
  - busted ≠ 159 after any of them; an edit outside the section's owned paths; any change to =Core.lua=, =OptionsLogic.lua=, =EnemyCooldowns.lua=, =CooldownVoice*.lua=, =flake.nix=, =flake.lock=, =.github/= or =embeds.xml=;
  - a consumer needs a Media API before =OnEnable=; a design needs to rewrite stored keys at load (beyond legacy-path =Migrate=);
  - the bar-colour reset needs more than re-applying the cast colour plus the existing =Update*= calls (R-56);
  - S27-S30 changed =InitializeDebuffButton= differently from their plan (re-read before S33);
  - the user refuses =git add --intent-to-add MerrinxUI/Util/Media.lua=;
  - at G-S33-GAME: any Lua error, =ADDON_ACTION_*= or taint from MerrinxUI; =Media/global font= or =global statusbar= FAIL with default settings; a default-settings visual difference from 0.16.0 on enUS (AC-110); =font preview= > 0 for MerrinxUI keys.

* Ownership (one owner per writable path)
| Owner role            | Paths |
|-----------------------+-------|
| =backend (nix/ci)=    | =flake.nix=, =flake.lock=, =.gitignore=, =.github/workflows/ci.yml= |
| =backend (lua-wow)=   | =MerrinxUI/**=, =spec/**=, =.luacheckrc=, =stylua.toml= |
| user                  | GitHub repo creation, pushes and tags, =git add -N= approval, in-game probes and gates |
| lead                  | =merrinxui-plan.org= (status, evidence and continuation state) |

Debug track: every =MerrinxUI/**=, =spec/**= and =.luacheckrc= path it touches stays with =backend (lua-wow)=; each SD section lists the exact files it may edit.

Sections run strictly one after another. Within a section the lua-wow workstream runs first, then nix/ci. Exception: in S20 nix/ci (WS-22) runs first, because the package check fails on an =embeds.xml= reference to a library that isn't copied yet. Files that later sections append to (the TOC, =embeds.xml=, =Core.lua=, =.luacheckrc=, =flake.nix=) each have a single role owner.

* Target repository layout (after S9)
#+begin_example
flake.nix  flake.lock  .gitignore  .luacheckrc  stylua.toml
.github/workflows/ci.yml
MerrinxUI/
  MerrinxUI.toc  embeds.xml  Core.lua
  Debug/DebugLogic.lua  Debug/Debug.lua                                            (SD1)
  Util/MoverLogic.lua  Util/Mover.lua  Util/CombatQueue.lua  Util/Combat.lua  Util/Hider.lua
  Util/FontLogic.lua                                                                (S21)
  Util/Media.lua                                                                    (S31)
  Libs/LibSharedMedia-3.0/LibSharedMedia-3.0.lua   (build output only, from the flake input; S20)
  Options/Theme.lua  Options/OptionsLogic.lua  Options/OptionsWindow.lua            (S15, S17)
  Options/GameMenuLogic.lua  Options/GameMenuButton.lua                             (S16)
  Modules/RangeTextLogic.lua    Modules/RangeText.lua
  Modules/HideBlizzardLogic.lua Modules/HideBlizzard.lua
  Modules/NameplateLogic.lua    Modules/Nameplates.lua
  Modules/ActionBarLogic.lua    Modules/ActionBars.lua
  Modules/DamageMeterLogic.lua  Modules/DamageMeter.lua
  Modules/EnemyCooldownLogic.lua Modules/EnemyCooldowns.lua   (S12)
