spec/
  range_text_logic_spec.lua mover_logic_spec.lua combatqueue_spec.lua hideblizzard_spec.lua
  nameplate_spec.lua actionbars_spec.lua damagemeter_spec.lua enemy_cooldown_logic_spec.lua
  options_logic_spec.lua game_menu_logic_spec.lua
  debug_logic_spec.lua (SD1) debug_coverage_spec.lua (SD4) font_logic_spec.lua (S21)
  cooldown_voice_logic_spec.lua (S30)
#+end_example
- The EnemyCooldowns pair is appended after whatever module pair is last in the TOC when S12 runs.
- TOC load order:
  1. =embeds.xml=, =Core.lua=, then (from SD1, D-D3) =Debug\DebugLogic.lua=, =Debug\Debug.lua=;
  2. the =Util/*= files in the order listed above (S4's Util files go after =Util\Mover.lua=);
  3. the =Options/*= files, in this order: =Theme.lua=, =OptionsLogic.lua=, =OptionsWindow.lua=, =GameMenuLogic.lua=, =GameMenuButton.lua=; each section inserts its lines at that position, before the first =Modules\= line;
  4. the modules, each =*Logic.lua= before its module.
- Write the TOC and XML with LF line endings.

* Shared interfaces and contracts
** Core (S2; extended in S3)
#+begin_src lua
local addonName, ns = ...
local MerrinxUI = LibStub("AceAddon-3.0"):NewAddon(addonName, "AceConsole-3.0", "AceEvent-3.0")
ns.addon = MerrinxUI
-- OnInitialize: self.db = LibStub("AceDB-3.0"):New("MerrinxUIDB", { profile = {}, global = {} }, true)
--   OnProfileChanged/Copied/Reset -> "RefreshModules"; RegisterChatCommand("mui"|"merrinxui", "SlashCommand")
-- OnEnable: root = { type="group", name="MerrinxUI", childGroups="tree", args = { profiles = AceDBOptions(order 100) } }
--   args[name] = module:GetOptions() for each module with GetOptions; AceConfig:RegisterOptionsTable("MerrinxUI", root)
-- RefreshModules(): module:Refresh() for each module
-- SlashCommand(input): "" -> AceConfigDialog:Open("MerrinxUI"); "unlock"/"lock" (S3) -> ns.Mover:SetUnlocked(bool)
#+end_src
** Module contract
| Member | Contract |
|---|---|
| =MerrinxUI:NewModule(name, "AceEvent-3.0")= | =name= is also the options key and the DB namespace. |
| =OnInitialize= | =self.db = ns.addon.db:RegisterNamespace(name, defaults)=, then =self:SetEnabledState(self.db.profile.enabled)=. |
| =GetOptions()= | Returns an AceConfig group. Getters and setters read =self.db.profile= at call time. Option keys equal the DB keys. Only the D-27 types and fields are used, so the S17 window renders every module. |
| =Refresh()= | Applies the current profile, or disables the module. Positions go through =ns.Mover:Apply=. |
| =GetDebugChecks()= (from SD1, D-D16) | Required for every module. Returns the module's read-only checks (debug check contract). Enforced by =spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua= from SD4. |
| Non-module runtime files (Util, Options) | Call =ns.Debug:RegisterChecks(source, provider)= at file load (from SD2 for Util; Options files from S16/S17 on). Pure =*Logic.lua= files are exempt and covered by busted. |
| Font options (from S21, D-36) | A module that offers a font stores the LSM key, declares =type = "select", dialogControl = "LSM30_Font", values = function() return ns.FontLogic.Values(LSM:HashTable("font")) end=, applies it with =LSM:Fetch("font", key, true)= plus a fallback, and migrates legacy values with =FontLogic.Migrate= in =OnInitialize= and =Refresh=. Never a free-text font field. |
** Mover (S3)
#+begin_src lua
ns.MoverLogic.CenterOffset(cx, cy, frameEffScale, parentW, parentH, parentEffScale) -> x, y
--   (500,400,1,1000,800,1) -> 0,0 ; (250,200,2,1000,800,1) -> 0,0 ; (600,400,1,1000,800,1) -> 100,0
ns.Mover:Register(frame, label, getPosition) -- getPosition() -> live {point="CENTER", x=, y=}
ns.Mover:Apply(frame) -> boolean             -- false/no-op if frame:IsProtected() and InCombatLockdown()
ns.Mover:SetUnlocked(unlocked) -> boolean    -- refused in combat with a printed reason
ns.Mover:IsUnlocked() -> boolean
#+end_src
- =PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED= stops any drag in progress, saves it, and locks.
** Combat queue and Hider (S4)
#+begin_src lua
local q = ns.CombatQueue.New(); q:Push(key, fn); q:Drain()   -- first-push order, last fn per key wins, snapshot drain
ns.Combat:Run(key, fn) -> boolean                            -- run now, or queue until PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED
ns.Hider.parent                                              -- hidden "MerrinxUIHiddenParent"
ns.Hider:Hide(frame, unregisterEvents) -> boolean            -- nil -> false; idempotent; deferred via Combat if protected in combat
#+end_src
=Hider:Hide= steps, in order:
1. clear =isShownExternal= if =frame.system= is set;
2. =UnregisterAllEvents()= if =unregisterEvents= is true;
3. =HideBase()= or =Hide()=;
4. =SetParent(ns.Hider.parent)=.

A hidden frame is restored only by =/reload=.
** Pure logic APIs (busted)
| Module | Function | Examples |
|---|---|---|
| RangeText | =NormalizeSpell(v)= | =133→133=, ="133"→133=, ="  Fireball "→"Fireball"=. =""=, =0=, =-5=, =1.5=, =nil= and ={}= all give =nil=. |
| RangeText | =ResolveSpell(configured, classFile)= | =(" ", "MAGE")→133=, =("Frostbolt", "MAGE")→"Frostbolt"=, =("", "UNKNOWN")→nil=. Every key in =DEFAULT_SPELLS= is one of the nine classes and maps to a positive integer. |
| RangeText | =Classify(r)= | =true→"in"=, =false→"out"=, =nil→"unknown"= |
| RangeText | =Color(state)= | =in= = 0,1,0; =out= = 1,0,0; =unknown= = 1,1,1 |
| RangeText | =FormatRange(min, max)= | =(20,25)→"20 - 25"=, =(0,5)→"0 - 5"=, =(40,nil)→"40+"=, =(nil,nil)→"?"= |
| HideBlizzard | =Groups(profile, forced)= | Returns sorted, unique keys. |
| Nameplates | =Color(reaction, isPlayer, classFile, tapDenied, classColors)= | Tap-denied → grey. Player with a known class → class colour. Reaction ≤3 → red, 4 → yellow, ≥5 → green, =nil= → red. |
| ActionBars | =Slot(page, i)= | =Slot(1,1)=1=, =Slot(6,1)=61=, =Slot(15,12)=180= |
| ActionBars | =PagingDriver(v, t)= | ="[possessbar] v; [shapeshift] t; [bar:2] 2; [bar:3] 3; [bar:4] 4; [bar:5] 5; [bar:6] 6; [bonusbar:1] 7; [bonusbar:2] 8; [bonusbar:3] 9; [bonusbar:4] 10; [bonusbar:5] 11; 1"= |
| ActionBars | =Layout(n, perRow, size, spacing)= | Cases: perRow 12, perRow 5 (last row partial), perRow 1. |
| ActionBars | =BindingPrefix(bar)= | Bar 1 → =ACTIONBUTTON=; bars 2-5 → =MULTIACTIONBAR1..4BUTTON=; bars 6-8 → =MULTIACTIONBAR5..7BUTTON=. |
| DamageMeter | =Title(session, metric)= | =("Current", "DamageDone")→"Damage Done - Current"= |
| DamageMeter | =VisibleRows(count, rows)= | =(3,8)→3=, =(20,8)→8=, =(0,8)→0= |
| DamageMeter | =WindowHeight(rows, rowHeight, spacing, headerHeight)= | =(8,18,2,22)→22+8*18+7*2=180= |
| DamageMeter | =EnumKey(tbl, key)= | Validates a key against an enum table. |
| DamageMeter | =IconCoords(coords, zoom)= | Insets the class icon coordinates by =zoom=. With =zoom= 0.06 on a full {0,1,0,1} square: {0.06, 0.94, 0.06, 0.94}. |
| EnemyCooldowns | (see the EnemyCooldownLogic subsection) | =BuildIndex=, =Lookup=, =Record=, =Prune=, =Active=, =Layout= |
| Fonts | (see the FontLogic subsection, S21) | =DEFAULT=, =IsPath=, =Values=, =Migrate= |
** FontLogic (S21; =MerrinxUI/Util/FontLogic.lua= → =ns.FontLogic=; pure, busted)
Starts with the D-12 header and reads no WoW globals. The caller passes =LSM:HashTable("font")=.
#+begin_src lua
FontLogic.DEFAULT = "Friz Quadrata TT"
FontLogic.IsPath(value) -> boolean
--   true for a string containing "\" or "/"; false for anything else
FontLogic.Values(hash) -> { [key] = key }
--   every key of hash, mapped to itself (labels are names, never paths); nil hash -> {}
FontLogic.Migrate(value, hash, default) -> key
--   non-string or "" -> default
--   not IsPath(value) -> value unchanged, even if not registered (a font another addon registers later still works)
--   path -> the key whose path matches, comparing lower-case with "/" turned into "\"
--   several matching keys -> default if it is one of them, else the smallest key by "<"
--   no match, or hash nil -> default
#+end_src
Busted examples (=spec/font_logic_spec.lua=, 7 =it= blocks; fixture =H = { ["Friz Quadrata TT"] = "Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF", ["Arial Narrow"] = "Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF", Morpheus = "Fonts\\MORPHEUS_CYR.TTF" }=, =D = "Friz Quadrata TT"=):
1. =DEFAULT == "Friz Quadrata TT"=.
2. =Values(H)= → ={ ["Friz Quadrata TT"] = "Friz Quadrata TT", ["Arial Narrow"] = "Arial Narrow", Morpheus = "Morpheus" }=; =Values(nil)= → ={}=.
3. =IsPath= is true for ="Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF"= and ="Interface/AddOns/X/f.ttf"=; false for ="Friz Quadrata TT"=, =""=, =nil=, =5=.
4. =Migrate("Morpheus", H, D)= → ="Morpheus"=; =Migrate("Expressway", H, D)= → ="Expressway"= (unregistered key kept).
5. =Migrate("Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF", H, D)= → ="Arial Narrow"=; =Migrate("fonts/arialn.ttf", H, D)= → ="Arial Narrow"=.
6. =Migrate("Fonts\\NOPE.TTF", H, D)=, =Migrate("", H, D)=, =Migrate(nil, H, D)=, =Migrate(42, H, D)= and =Migrate("Fonts\\ARIALN.TTF", nil, D)= all → =D=.
7. Shared path: =Migrate("Fonts\\X.TTF", { B = "Fonts\\X.TTF", A = "Fonts\\X.TTF" }, "Z")= → ="A"=; =Migrate("Fonts\\X.TTF", { B = "Fonts\\X.TTF", Z = "Fonts\\X.TTF" }, "Z")= → ="Z"=.
** EnemyCooldownLogic (S12; =MerrinxUI/Modules/EnemyCooldownLogic.lua= → =ns.EnemyCooldownLogic=)
Reads no WoW globals at load. The caller passes the time and the name resolver.
#+begin_src lua
L.CATEGORIES = { interrupt = true, defensive = true, cc = true, racial = true }
L.SPELLS = { { id = 6552, class = "WARRIOR", category = "interrupt", cd = 10 }, ... } -- curated table, rows in order
L.BuildIndex(spells, getName) -> { byId = { [id] = entry }, byName = { [name] = entry } }
--   every entry goes into byId; byName only when getName(id) returns a string; on a duplicate name the first entry wins
L.Lookup(index, spellID, name) -> entry | nil  -- byId[spellID] or (name ~= nil and byName[name]) or nil
L.Record(state, key, entry, now)               -- state[key][entry.id] = { entry = entry, start = now, expires = now + entry.cd }; a recast replaces it
L.Prune(state, now) -> changed                 -- drops records with expires <= now and empty keys; changed = { [key] = true }
L.Active(state, key, now, maxIcons) -> array   -- records with expires > now, sorted by expires asc then entry.id asc, at most maxIcons; maxIcons < 1 -> {}
L.Layout(anchor, i, n, size, spacing, ox, oy) -> point, relPoint, x, y
--   TOP:    "BOTTOM","TOP",  x = (i-1)*(size+spacing) - (n-1)*(size+spacing)/2 + ox, y = oy
--   BOTTOM: "TOP","BOTTOM",  same x, y = oy
--   RIGHT:  "LEFT","RIGHT",  x = (i-1)*(size+spacing) + ox, y = oy
--   LEFT:   "RIGHT","LEFT",  x = -(i-1)*(size+spacing) + ox, y = oy
--   any other anchor behaves as TOP
#+end_src
Busted examples (=spec/enemy_cooldown_logic_spec.lua=). Fixtures: =kick = {id=1766, class="ROGUE", category="interrupt", cd=10}=, =cs = {id=2139, class="MAGE", category="interrupt", cd=30}=, =van = {id=1856, class="ROGUE", category="defensive", cd=300}=, =names = {[1766]="Kick", [1856]="Vanish"}= (2139 unresolved), =idx = BuildIndex({kick, cs, van}, function(id) return names[id] end)=.
- Table shape: every =SPELLS= id is a unique positive integer; =class= is one of the nine classes or ="ANY"=; =category= is in =CATEGORIES=; =cd= is a positive integer.
- Lookup: =(idx,1766,"Kick")→kick=; =(idx,1769,"Kick")→kick= (higher rank by name); =(idx,2139,nil)→cs=; =(idx,9,"Fireball")→nil=; =(idx,9,nil)→nil=. Duplicate name ="X"= for ids 1 and 2: =byName.X= is the id-1 entry.
- Record/Active/Prune, in order on one =st=:
  1. =Record(st,"G",kick,100)=; =Active(st,"G",105,4)= → kick with start 100, expires 110.
  2. =Record(st,"G",van,101)=; =Active(st,"G",105,4)= → kick, van; max 1 → kick; max 0 → ={}=.
  3. =Active(st,"G",110,4)= → van only.
  4. =Active(st,"none",105,4)= → ={}=.
  5. =Record(st,"G",kick,108)= → still one kick record, expires 118.
  6. Two records with equal =expires= are ordered by id ascending.
  7. =Prune(st,118)= → ={G=true}=; only van remains.
  8. =Prune(st,401)= → ={G=true}= and =st.G == nil=.
  9. =Prune(st,500)= → ={}=.
- Layout: =("TOP",1,3,20,2,0,4)→"BOTTOM","TOP",-22,4=; =("TOP",2,3,20,2,0,4)= x 0; =("TOP",3,3,20,2,0,4)= x 22; =("TOP",1,1,20,2,0,0)= x 0; =("RIGHT",2,3,20,2,5,0)→"LEFT","RIGHT",27,0=; =("LEFT",3,3,20,2,-5,0)→"RIGHT","LEFT",-49,0=; =("bogus",1,3,20,2,0,4)= equals the TOP result.
** EnemyCooldowns runtime (S12/S13; =MerrinxUI/Modules/EnemyCooldowns.lua=)
- =ns.addon:NewModule("EnemyCooldowns", "AceEvent-3.0")=; DB namespace and options key =EnemyCooldowns=.
- Module-owned state (nothing written onto Blizzard frames):
  - =self.state=: logic state keyed by GUID (D-20);
  - =self.units[unit] = guid=: eligible plates only;
  - =self.unitPlate[unit] = plate=;
  - =self.containers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })=: plate → container;
  - =self.index=: built once on first =OnEnable= with =L.BuildIndex(L.SPELLS, C_Spell.GetSpellName)=.
- Frames:
  - container =CreateFrame("Frame", nil, plate)= with plate = =C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit(unit)=, frame level plate + 10;
  - icons created lazily up to =maxIcons= and reused; each is a Frame with a Texture (=C_Spell.GetSpellTexture(entry.id)=, fallback =Interface\\Icons\\INV_Misc_QuestionMark=) and =CreateFrame("Cooldown", nil, icon, "CooldownFrameTemplate")= with =SetAllPoints=, driven by =SetCooldown(rec.start, rec.entry.cd)=;
  - =Render(unit)= places icons with =L.Layout(profile.anchor, i, n, profile.iconSize, 2, profile.x, profile.y)= and hides unused ones.
- Events:
  - =NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED(unit)= and =UNIT_FACTION(unit)= (only =nameplate*= units; catches duel start and end):
    1. plate nil or =plate:IsForbidden()= → return;
    2. =UnitIsPlayer(unit)= or =UnitCanAttack("player", unit)= secret, or not both true → clear the unit, hide its container;
    3. =UnitGUID(unit)= secret or nil → clear the unit;
    4. else =units[unit] = guid=, =unitPlate[unit] = plate=, =Render(unit)=.
  - =NAME_PLATE_UNIT_REMOVED(unit)=: hide the container of =unitPlate[unit]=, clear both maps.
  - =UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED(_, unit, _, spellID)=: =key = units[unit]=; no key or =issecretvalue(spellID)= → return; name = =C_Spell.GetSpellName(spellID)= (secret → nil); =entry = L.Lookup(index, spellID, name)=; if found, =L.Record(state, key, entry, GetTime())= and =Render(unit)=.
  - =PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD=: wipe =state=, =units=, =unitPlate=; hide every container.
  - Ticker (D-25): =L.Prune(state, GetTime())=, then =Render= every unit whose key is in =changed=.
- Lifecycle: =OnDisable= cancels the ticker, wipes state, hides all containers (AceEvent unregisters events). =Refresh()= enables and re-renders every unit in =units=, or disables.
** OptionsLogic (S15; =MerrinxUI/Options/OptionsLogic.lua= → =ns.OptionsLogic=)
Pure: reads no WoW globals at load, and starts with the D-12 header. The spec loads it with =loadfile("MerrinxUI/Options/OptionsLogic.lua")("MerrinxUI", ns)=. Before writing it, the worker reads the AceConfigDialog source at =result/MerrinxUI/Libs/AceConfig-3.0/AceConfigDialog-3.0/AceConfigDialog-3.0.lua= (after =nix build .#default=): =isInherited= (around line 131), the method-string rule (around line 142), =compareOptions= (around line 280), validation (around line 741) and =ActivateSlider= (around line 875). The AceGUI Dropdown =sortTbl= (around line 584) gives the =values= sort.
#+begin_src lua
L.INHERITED      = { set=true, get=true, func=true, confirm=true, validate=true, disabled=true, hidden=true }
L.STRING_LITERAL = { name=true, desc=true, usage=true }   -- confirmText is always read raw
L.SUPPORTED      = { group=true, toggle=true, range=true, select=true, input=true, execute=true, description=true, header=true }
L.Node(root, path) -> node|nil                 -- path = array of keys; {} -> root; walks .args only (no plugins)
L.Member(root, path, name) -> raw              -- INHERITED: last non-nil from root..node (inclusive); else node[name]
L.Handler(root, path) -> table|nil             -- last truthy .handler from root..node (inclusive; AceConfigDialog's group.handler or handler)
L.Info(root, path, appName) -> info            -- info[1..n]=path, info[0]=appName, options=root, appName, arg=node.arg,
                                               -- handler, option=node, type=node.type, uiType="dialog", uiName=appName
L.Value(root, path, name, appName, ...) -> a,b,c,d
  -- raw=Member; function -> raw(info, ...); string and not STRING_LITERAL[name] -> handler[raw](handler, info, ...);
  -- missing method -> error("Method <raw> doesn't exist in handler for <name>"); else raw
L.Sorted(root, path, appName) -> keys          -- order=Value("order") or 100; negatives after non-negatives;
                                               -- tie: upper(name or ""), then tostring(key)
L.IsHidden(root, path, appName) -> boolean     -- first non-nil of dialogHidden, guiHidden; else not not Value("hidden")
L.IsDisabled(root, path, appName) -> boolean   -- first non-nil of dialogDisabled, guiDisabled; else not not Value("disabled")
L.Pages(root, appName) -> { {label=, path=, leavesOnly=} }
  -- {label="General", path={}, leavesOnly=true} first iff root has a visible non-group arg; then visible root groups by Sorted
L.Flatten(root, path, appName, leavesOnly) -> rows  -- row = {kind=, path=, depth=}; kind = type | "section" | "unsupported";
  -- visible nested group -> "section" row + its rows at depth+1, emitted only if it yields >=1 row; leavesOnly skips groups
L.SortedValues(values) -> keys                 -- numeric when both keys are numbers, else by tostring
L.SnapRange(v, min, max, step) -> number       -- min default 0, max default 100; step nil or <=0 -> no snapping; clamp
L.FormatNumber(v) -> string                    -- "%.2f" with trailing zeros and "." trimmed
L.CheckValidate(result, name, usage) -> ok, msg
L.ConfirmText(confirm, confirmText, name, desc) -> needed, text
L.Scroll(offset, delta, step, contentH, viewH) -> clamp(offset - delta*step, 0, max(0, contentH-viewH))
L.Stack(heights, gap) -> offsets, total
#+end_src
Busted examples (=spec/options_logic_spec.lua=).

Fixture:

H2 = {}
t = { type = "toggle", arg = "common" }
root = { type = "group", handler = H, args = { g = { type = "group", name = "G", args = { t = t } } } }

  - Handler: with =g.handler=H2= → =H2=; with =t.handler=H= as well → =H=.
  - Member: with =root.get=f= → =f=; with =t.get=false= as well → =false=; with =g.disabled=true=, =IsDisabled(root,{"g","t"})==true=; =name= isn't inherited (=g.name="G"= leaves =Member(...,"name")==nil= for =t=).

- Sorted: args ={b={order=2,name="B"}, a={order=1,name="A"}, c={order=2,name="a"}, z={name="Z"}, n={order=-1,name="N"}, m={order=-2,name="M"}}= → ={"a","c","b","z","m","n"}=. Equal name and order: the key decides (=k1= before =k2=).

Window geometry and style (S17):
- The content area is 720×556.
- Header: "MerrinxUI" in the accent colour at size 20 on the left, the page label in =textDim= next to it, an "×" close button on the right. Dragging the header moves the window (=StartMoving=).
- Sidebar: one 34 px row per page. The active row gets a 3 px accent bar on the left plus the =hover= background; hovered rows get =hover=.
- Footer: the validation or error message, in the =error= colour.
- Content: a =ScrollFrame= scrolled with the mouse wheel through =L.Scroll=. Rows alternate =rowA=/=rowB=. The label sits on the left with an optional dim =desc= below it; the control is right-aligned and 240 wide. Section rows use the =section= colour at size 12, with no background.
- Overlays, inside the =FULLSCREEN_DIALOG= strata: the dropdown list at window level +20, the confirm overlay at +30.
- If S14 P5 shows ARIALN missing, =Theme.font= becomes =Fonts\\FRIZQT__.TTF= (decided at the S14 checkpoint).
** GameMenuLogic (S16; =MerrinxUI/Options/GameMenuLogic.lua= → =ns.GameMenuLogic=; pure)
#+begin_src lua
GML.Plan(buttons, targetText) -> targetIndex|nil, shift   -- buttons[i] = {text=, y=} (TOPLEFT y offset, negative = lower)
-- target = the entry with text == targetText and the largest y (topmost); entries with a nil y are ignored
-- shift = ascending indices i with buttons[i].y <= target.y (the target included)
#+end_src

** Options runtime (S16/S17)
- =ns.addon:OpenOptions()= (=Core.lua=): S16 calls =LibStub("AceConfigDialog-3.0"):Open("MerrinxUI")=; S17 calls =ns.OptionsWindow:Open()=. =SlashCommand='s else branch calls =self:OpenOptions()=.
- =ns.OptionsWindow= (=Options/OptionsWindow.lua=, S17):
  - =W:Open()= builds the frame on first use, then shows and raises it and renders the last page shown (the first page initially). It works in combat.
  - =W:Close()=; =W:IsShown() -> boolean=.
  - The Esc handler and the =ConfigTableChange= callback are registered once, when the frame is created.
  - Data: =AceConfigRegistry:GetOptionsTable("MerrinxUI", "dialog", "MerrinxUI")=, then =L.Pages= and =L.Flatten=. Row frames come from per-kind pools; every render releases all rows first.
  - Widgets:
    - toggle: a switch; a click calls =set(not get)=.
    - range: a =Slider= with =SetObeyStepOnDrag(true)=. Only =OnValueChanged(_, v, userInput)= with =userInput= true acts: snap with =SnapRange=, call =set= only when the value changed, show =FormatNumber(v)=, re-render on mouse-up. The mouse wheel doesn't move the slider.
    - select: our button plus our list (=SortedValues=, at most 10 visible, wheel scroll). Choosing a value runs the confirm check, then =set=. With =get=false= the button shows nothing.
    - input: an =EditBox= with =SetAutoFocus(false)=. A non-string =get= shows =""=. Enter runs =CheckValidate(Value("validate", text))= only when =validate= is a function or a method-name string (otherwise the input is valid, as AceConfigDialog's =validated = true= default), then =set=, or shows the error in the footer. Escape or focus loss restores the text.
    - execute: a button; the confirm check, then =func=.
    - description: wrapped text. header: a section row.
    - Disabled rows use =disabledAlpha= and ignore input; hidden rows are skipped.
  - Confirm: resolve =Value("confirm", value)=, then =ConfirmText=; if needed, show the overlay. Confirm runs the stored =set= or =func=; Cancel or a page change discards it.
  - Refresh per D-29: =RequestRender()= after every =set=/=func=; =AceConfigRegistry.RegisterCallback(W, "ConfigTableChange", "OnConfigTableChange")= handles =appName == "MerrinxUI"= while the window is shown.
- =Options/GameMenuButton.lua= (S16) exports nothing. Its button and hook are file-local and installed when the file loads:
  #+begin_src lua
  local btn = CreateFrame("Button", nil, GameMenuFrame, "MainMenuFrameButtonTemplate")
  btn:SetText("MerrinxUI")
  btn:Hide()
  btn:SetScript("OnClick", function()
      if not InCombatLockdown() then HideUIPanel(GameMenuFrame) end
      ns.addon:OpenOptions()
  end)
  hooksecurefunc(GameMenuFrame, "Layout", function(menu) ... end)
  #+end_src
  The hook body, in order:
  1. If =InputUtil.IsGamepadUIEnabled()=, hide =btn= and return.
  2. For each =b= in =menu.buttonPool:EnumerateActive()=, record =p, rel, rp, x, y = b:GetPoint(1)= and ={text = b:GetText(), y = y}=, keeping =b= and its point.
  3. =idx, shift = GML.Plan(list, ADDONS)=. If =idx= is nil, hide =btn= and return.
  4. With target =t=: =h = t:GetHeight()=; =btn:SetSize(t:GetSize())=; =btn:ClearAllPoints()=; =btn:SetPoint(p_t, rel_t, rp_t, x_t, y_t)=.
  5. For each =i= in =shift=: =ClearAllPoints()= and =SetPoint(p, rel, rp, x, y - h)=.
  6. =menu:SetHeight(menu:GetHeight() + h)=, then =btn:Show()=.
  The hook doesn't check combat: it only moves non-protected buttons (S14 P3 confirms), and the menu can already be open when combat starts.
** Debug: DebugLogic (SD1; =MerrinxUI/Debug/DebugLogic.lua= → =ns.DebugLogic=; pure, busted)
The =--= notes in the debug contract blocks are plan text; do not copy them into source.
#+begin_src lua
DebugLogic.CAP = 500          -- log lines
DebugLogic.MAX_LINE = 400     -- chars per log line
DebugLogic.MAX_PART = 200     -- chars per described value
DebugLogic.STATUSES = { PASS = true, FAIL = true, SKIP = true, INFO = true }

DebugLogic.Describe(v, isSecret) -> string
-- isSecret(v) first, before type(). secret -> "<secret>"; nil -> "nil"; boolean -> "true"/"false";
-- number -> tostring; string -> "\n"/"\r" become " ", "|" becomes "||", cut to MAX_PART then "...";
-- anything else -> type(v). Never indexes tables, never errors.
DebugLogic.Join(parts, n, isSecret) -> string        -- Describe(parts[1..n]) joined by " "; n keeps nil gaps
DebugLogic.FormatLine(stamp, source, text) -> string -- "[" .. stamp .. "] " .. source .. ": " .. text, cut to MAX_LINE
DebugLogic.Push(log, line, cap) -> removed           -- append, then drop oldest until #log <= cap (cap >= 1)
DebugLogic.Flatten(tbl, isSecret) -> string          -- "k=v" sorted by key; nested tables one level as "k.sub=v"; deeper -> "k.sub=table"
DebugLogic.RunCheck(check, ctx, isSecret) -> { name, status, detail }
DebugLogic.RunChecks(source, checks, ctx, isSecret) -> results   -- order kept; each result gets .source; nil checks -> {}
DebugLogic.RunSource(source, provider, ctx, isSecret) -> results
-- pcall(provider); throw -> { {source=, name="checks", status="FAIL", detail="error: " .. Describe(err)} };
-- non-table, non-nil return -> { {source=, name="checks", status="FAIL", detail="not a table"} }; else RunChecks
DebugLogic.FormatResult(r) -> string                 -- "FAIL EnemyCooldowns/spell table: mismatched 6346 180000 30000"
DebugLogic.Summary(results) -> text, counts          -- "12 checks: 9 PASS, 1 FAIL, 1 SKIP, 1 INFO", {PASS=9,FAIL=1,SKIP=1,INFO=1,total=12}
DebugLogic.SortSources(names) -> array               -- "Debug" first, rest alphabetical
#+end_src
=RunCheck= error semantics: non-table check or non-function =run= → =FAIL "malformed check"=; =run= throws → =FAIL "error: " .. Describe(err)= (secret error → =error: <secret>=); status nil, secret or not in =STATUSES= → =FAIL "invalid status: " .. Describe(status)=; a non-string name goes through =Describe=; otherwise =detail = Join(values after status)=.

Busted examples (=spec/debug_logic_spec.lua=; =S= is a sentinel table, =isS = function(v) return v == S end=):
| Call | Result |
|---|---|
| =Describe(S, isS)= | ="<secret>"= |
| =Describe(nil, isS)= / =Describe(false, isS)= / =Describe(1.5, isS)= | ="nil"= / ="false"= / ="1.5"= |
| =Describe({}, isS)= | ="table"= |
| =Describe(PIPE_INPUT, isS)= | =PIPE_OUTPUT= (literals below) |
| =Describe(string.rep("x", 300), isS)= | 200 × ="x"= then ="..."= |
| =Join({1, nil, S}, 3, isS)= | ="1 nil <secret>"= |
| =FormatLine("12:00:01", "EnemyCooldowns", "hit 2139 Counterspell")= | ="[12:00:01] EnemyCooldowns: hit 2139 Counterspell"= |
| =FormatLine= with a 1000-char text | length 400 |
| =Push({"a","b"}, "c", 2)= | log ={"b","c"}=, returns 1 |
| =Push({"a","b","c","d"}, "e", 2)= | log ={"d","e"}=, returns 3 |
| =Flatten({b=1, a="x", position={x=0, point="CENTER"}}, isS)= | ="a=x b=1 position.point=CENTER position.x=0"= |
| =Flatten({k=S}, isS)= | ="k=<secret>"= |
| =RunCheck({name="x", run=function() return "PASS", 133, "Fireball" end}, {}, isS)= | ={name="x", status="PASS", detail="133 Fireball"}= |
| check whose =run= does =error("boom")= | =FAIL=, detail starts with ="error: "= and contains ="boom"= |
| check whose =run= does =error(S)= | =FAIL=, ="error: <secret>"= |
| check returning ="OK"= / =S= as status | =FAIL=, ="invalid status: OK"= / ="invalid status: <secret>"= |
| =RunCheck("x", {}, isS)= | =FAIL=, ="malformed check"= |
| =RunChecks("RangeText", nil, {}, isS)= | ={}= |
| =RunSource("Mover", function() error("bad index") end, {}, isS)= | one result: =source="Mover"=, =name="checks"=, =FAIL=, detail contains ="bad index"= |
| =RunSource("Mover", function() return 5 end, {}, isS)= | one result =FAIL "not a table"= |
| =RunSource("Mover", function() return { {name="a", run=function() return "INFO", true end} } end, {}, isS)= | ={ {source="Mover", name="a", status="INFO", detail="true"} }= |
| =Summary= of PASS, PASS, FAIL | ="3 checks: 2 PASS, 1 FAIL, 0 SKIP, 0 INFO"= |
| =SortSources({"RangeText","Debug","EnemyCooldowns"})= | ={"Debug","EnemyCooldowns","RangeText"}= |
Pipe literals (Lua): =PIPE_INPUT = "a|cffb\nc"=, =PIPE_OUTPUT = "a||cffb c"=.

** Debug: runtime (SD1; =MerrinxUI/Debug/Debug.lua= → =ns.Debug=)
#+begin_src lua
local Debug = ns.addon:NewModule("Debug")
ns.Debug = Debug
-- OnInitialize: self.db = ns.addon.db:RegisterNamespace("Debug", { global = { enabled = false } })
--               self.db.global.log = self.db.global.log or {}
-- OnEnable:     if active -> Log("Debug", "session", version, interface, classFile, locale)

Debug:IsActive() -> boolean
Debug:SetActive(value)            -- not not value; logs "debug on"/"debug off" (logs before turning off);
                                  -- prints one chat line; AceConfigRegistry:NotifyChange("MerrinxUI")
Debug:Log(source, ...)            -- returns at once if not active or self.db is nil; otherwise
                                  -- Push(log, FormatLine(date("%H:%M:%S"), source, Join({...}, select("#", ...), issecretvalue)), CAP)
Debug:RegisterChecks(source, provider)  -- provider() -> checks array; works before OnInitialize (file-scope table)
Debug:RunTests() -> results | nil -- inactive: prints "debug mode is off (/mui debug on)", returns nil
Debug:ShowLog()                   -- open or refresh the single log window; SetText -> SetFocus -> HighlightText()
Debug:ClearLog()
Debug:Slash(verb, arg)            -- "debug" [on|off], "test", "log"; anything else prints usage
Debug:GetDebugChecks()            -- core checks (debug coverage matrix)
Debug:GetOptions()                -- group "Debug", order 1000
#+end_src
=RunTests= order:
1. =Log("Debug", "test run", version, interface, classFile, locale, "combat", InCombatLockdown())=.
2. =ctx = { inCombat = InCombatLockdown(), hasTarget = UnitExists("target") }=.
3. For each source in =SortSources(module names ∪ registered sources)=:
   - module with =GetDebugChecks=: =ctx.enabled = module:IsEnabled()=; =RunSource(name, function() return module:GetDebugChecks() end, ctx, issecretvalue)=;
   - every module except Debug that has =db.profile=: also the automatic =settings= (INFO, =Flatten(profile)=) and =enabled state= (PASS if =IsEnabled() == profile.enabled=) results;
   - registered providers: =ctx.enabled = nil=; =RunSource= per provider under that source.
4. Every =FormatResult= goes to the log; =Summary= is logged and printed with up to 10 FAIL lines; then =ShowLog()=.

Options group (=order = 1000=): =active= toggle; =run= execute (=disabled= while inactive); =show= execute; =clear= execute with =confirm = true=; =info= description whose =name= is a function returning ="Log: N/500 lines"=.

Core change (=SlashCommand= only): keep =command == "unlock"= / ="lock"= on the full trimmed command; then =local verb, arg = command:match("^(%S*)%s*(.-)$")=, and =verb= ∈ {=debug=, =test=, =log=} goes to =ns.Debug:Slash(verb, arg)=; everything else opens the options as before.

** Debug: check contract (append-only extension point)
#+begin_src lua
-- Ace modules (discovered like GetOptions):
function Module:GetDebugChecks()
  return {
    { name = "spell", run = function() return "PASS", 133, "Fireball" end },
    { name = "visibility", run = function(ctx)
        if ctx.enabled == false then return "SKIP", "module disabled" end
        return "PASS", self.frame:IsShown()
      end },
  }
end
-- Non-module runtime files (Util/*, later Options/*), at file load:
ns.Debug:RegisterChecks("Mover", function()
  return { { name = "unlocked", run = function() return "INFO", isUnlocked end } }
end)
#+end_src
- =status= ∈ =PASS=, =FAIL=, =SKIP=, =INFO=; trailing values are raw (Debug describes them). Omit =ctx= when unused (luacheck 212).
- =ctx.inCombat=, =ctx.hasTarget=, =ctx.enabled= (module sources only; =nil= for registered providers).
- Target checks return =SKIP, "no target"= when =not ctx.hasTarget=; running-state checks return =SKIP, "module disabled"= when =ctx.enabled == false=.
- Read-only. Forbidden inside checks and providers: =CreateFrame=, =C_CVar.SetCVar=/=SetCVar=, =:Show(=, =:Hide(=, =SetText=, =SetAlpha=, =SetSize=, =SetPoint=, =ClearAllPoints=, =SetParent=, =RegisterEvent=, protected calls, Blizzard-field writes (D-20, AC-37), =UnitGUID=, and every module method with side effects: =GetContainer=, =GetIcon=, =GetDebuffIcon=, =CreateStyledFrame=, =Render=, =Update*=, =Apply*=, =Style*=/=Unstyle*=, =SyncCastBar=, =Refresh=, =Enable=, =Disable=. Read existing objects directly (=self.containers[plate]=, =container.icons[i]=, =frame.debuffIcons[i]=).
- Never compare a value before =issecretvalue= has cleared it; report secret-ness as INFO. Getters handed to pure helpers map secret values to nil.
- Check names are stable strings; the log line is =STATUS Source/name: detail=.
- Rule for future work (D-D16): every new runtime file in the TOC either defines =GetDebugChecks= on its module or calls =ns.Debug:RegisterChecks=; the coverage spec fails the build otherwise.

** Debug: =EnemyCooldownLogic.Audit= (SD3; pure, busted)
#+begin_src lua
L.Audit(spells, getName, getBaseCooldownMs) -> { unresolved = {ids}, duplicates = {ids}, mismatched = { {id=, expected=, actual=} }, unknownCd = {ids} }
-- getName(id) -> string|nil ; getBaseCooldownMs(id) -> number|nil
-- unresolved: name nil. duplicates: second and later ids whose name was already seen.
-- mismatched: actual ~= nil and actual ~= cd * 1000. unknownCd: actual == nil. Lists keep spells order.
#+end_src
Examples (=spec/enemy_cooldown_logic_spec.lua=):
- spells ={ {id=1,cd=10}, {id=2,cd=20}, {id=3,cd=30} }=, names ={[1]="A",[2]="A"}=, base ={[1]=10000,[2]=5000}= → ={ unresolved={3}, duplicates={2}, mismatched={ {id=2,expected=20000,actual=5000} }, unknownCd={3} }=.
- spells ={}= → all four lists empty.
- every =getBaseCooldownMs= returns nil, names unique → only =unknownCd= (all ids).

** Debug: coverage spec (SD4; =spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua=)
- Read =MerrinxUI/MerrinxUI.toc= with =io.open=; strip =\r=; skip blank and =#= lines; keep entries ending =.lua=; convert =\= to =/=; read each file.
- =EXEMPT= = ={ "MerrinxUI/Core.lua", "MerrinxUI/Debug/DebugLogic.lua", "MerrinxUI/Util/CombatQueue.lua", "MerrinxUI/Options/Theme.lua" }= plus any path ending =Logic.lua= (=Options/Theme.lua= added in S15: pure data loaded by busted with a bare =ns=; its runtime use is checked by =OptionsWindow/font=).
- Every other file must satisfy: (=src:find(":NewModule(", 1, true)= and =src:find("function%s+[%w_]+:GetDebugChecks%(")=) or =src:find("ns.Debug:RegisterChecks(", 1, true)=. Use plain =find= for the literal strings.
- Assert at least 5 =:NewModule(= files were found, so a parse bug cannot pass vacuously.
- Assert the =Debug\Debug.lua= line comes before the first =Util\= line (D-D3).

** Debug: coverage matrix (which check verifies which feature)
"auto" = a named check verifies it; "trace" = SD5 trace lines plus a re-run of =/mui test=; "eye" = the user looks.
| Feature (AC) | Source/check names | How |
|---|---|---|
| Interface (AC-08) | =Debug/interface= (PASS if 16001) | auto |
| Addon version (AC-08) | =Debug/test run= header, =Debug/version= (INFO; FAIL if =C_AddOns= missing) | auto |
| Libraries (AC-03) | =Debug/libraries= (INFO: =LibStub(name, true)= loaded for each embedded lib; FAIL if any missing — loaded, not proof of embedding; from S20 the list includes =LibSharedMedia-3.0=, so the count is 13, AC-77) | auto |
| Hub, slash, tree nodes (AC-09) | =Debug/slash= (=SlashCmdList.ACECONSOLE_MUI= and =ACECONSOLE_MERRINXUI= are functions), =Debug/options= (root has =unlock=, =profiles= and one node per module with =GetOptions=) | auto |
| Profiles refresh modules (AC-10) | automatic =<Module>/enabled state= and =<Module>/settings=; =Debug/profile= INFO (count, shared or per-character, no names) | auto (re-run after a profile switch) |
| Coverage (AC-D9) | =Debug/coverage <Module>= | auto |
| Secret API | =Debug/secret api= | auto |
| Debug log (AC-D3) | =Debug/log= (INFO lines/cap) | auto |
| Unlock, positions, combat relock (AC-14) | =Mover/unlocked= (FAIL if unlocked in combat), =Mover/position <label>= (anchor equals saved =point/x/y= within 0.5; SKIP if =frame:GetNumPoints() == 0= or =entry.dragging=), =Mover/overlay <label>= (shown == unlocked) | auto; drag = eye; relock = trace |
| Combat queue (AC-17) | =Combat/queue= (INFO pending; FAIL if out of combat with pending > 0) | auto |
| Hidden frames (AC-17) | =Hider/parent= (hidden), =Hider/frame <Name>= (each frame in =hidden=: parent is =Hider.parent= and not =IsVisible()=) | auto |
| Range text value (AC-11) | =RangeText/range= (target: =GetRange= min/max INFO; PASS if =fsMain:GetText()= equals =FormatRange=, secret-guarded) | auto |
| Range colour, secret path (AC-12) | =RangeText/in range= (target: INFO result + secret flag; non-secret: PASS if =fsMain= colour equals =Color(Classify(r))= within 0.01; secret: PASS if both font strings shown) | auto |
| Per-class default spell (AC-13) | =RangeText/spell= (resolved id + =C_Spell.GetSpellName=; INFO "yards only" if none) | auto |
| Font, visibility (AC-15, AC-79..AC-81) | =RangeText/font= (until S21: =lastGoodFont == profile.font=; from S21: =lastGoodFont == LSM:Fetch("font", profile.font, true)= and that is non-nil; =GetFont= size rounds to =fontSize=, flags equal =outline=; returns key, path, size, flags), =RangeText/visibility= (module running: shown == target or unlocked) | auto |
| Font preview (AC-82, S22) | =OptionsWindow/font preview= (INFO: count, then the sorted keys whose preview font couldn't be set) | auto; look = eye |
| Accent (AC-75, S19) | none (pure data, busted Theme spec) | eye at G-S22-GAME step 3 |
| HideBlizzard toggles (AC-17) | =HideBlizzard/frame <Name>= (each frame of each group in =CurrentGroupSet()=: PASS if parent is =ns.Hider.parent= and not =IsVisible()=; INFO if the global is missing), =HideBlizzard/reload needed <key>= (INFO for each group not in =CurrentGroupSet()= with a frame still parented to =ns.Hider.parent=), =HideBlizzard/meter cvar= (PASS if =CurrentGroupSet().damageMeter= implies CVar ="0"=; INFO CVar and =meterCVarOwned=) | auto |
| Spell table (AC-30) | =EnemyCooldowns/spell table= (=Audit=: FAIL lists unresolved, duplicate and mismatched ids, else PASS), =EnemyCooldowns/spell cooldowns= (INFO =unknownCd= ids; SKIP if =GetSpellBaseCooldown= missing; split from one row in SD3, DU-07) | auto |
| Tracker running (AC-24, AC-28) | =EnemyCooldowns/index=, =EnemyCooldowns/ticker= (module running), =EnemyCooldowns/tracked= (INFO count of tracked units and of state records; never GUIDs) | auto |
| Icons, order, cap, options (AC-24, AC-26, AC-28) | =EnemyCooldowns/icons <unit>= (shown icons equal =#L.Active(self.state, guid, GetTime(), maxIcons)=; SKIP if any record's =expires= is within =[now - 2 * TICK_INTERVAL, now]=; each icon width ≈ =iconSize=; each =GetPoint(1)= equals =L.Layout(anchor, i, n, iconSize, LAYOUT_SPACING, x, y)= relative to the container), =EnemyCooldowns/container parent <unit>= (=container:GetParent() == plate=, D-21) | auto after a cast; the cast = trace |

| Enemy plates (AC-18) | =Nameplates/apis=, =Nameplates/styled plates= (every visible plate with non-secret =UnitCanAttack= true has a shown styled frame; no friendly plate has one; secret → counted as unknown), =Nameplates/target plate= (size ≈ width/height; =nameText:GetFont()= size ≈ =nameSize=; =plate.UnitFrame:GetAlpha() == 0=), =Nameplates/health colour= (=healthBar:GetStatusBarColor()= ≈ =NameplateLogic.Color(...)= with the module's secret→nil mapping), =Nameplates/highlight= (target =highlight:GetAlpha() == 1=, others 0, when =UnitIsUnit= is non-secret; else INFO secret) | auto; textures = eye |

| Debuffs (AC-42..AC-44) | =Nameplates/debuffs= (target: shown icons ≤ =debuffMax=; width ≈ =debuffSize=; =GetPoint(1)= equals =DebuffLayout(anchor, i, n, debuffSize, DEBUFF_SPACING)=; relative frame follows =UpdateDebuffs= (BOTTOM → castBar/powerBar/healthBar, LEFT/RIGHT → frame, else nameText); INFO counts for the "HARMFUL plus PLAYER" filter and for =HARMFUL=; from S18, both counts are ="auras secret"= and =GetUnitAuras= isn't called while =C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret()= is true) | auto, out of combat and in combat (S18) |
| Secret flags (NP probe step 0, AC-45) | =Nameplates/secret flags= (target, INFO =issecretvalue= only: =UnitHealth=, =UnitHealthMax=, =UnitHealthPercent(unit, true, CurveConstants.ScaleTo100)=, =UnitPower=, =UnitPowerMax=, =UnitPowerPercent(unit, nil, false, CurveConstants.ScaleTo100)=, second return of =UnitPowerType=, =UnitReaction=, =UnitIsUnit(unit, "target")=, =UnitCanAttack=; for the first aura of the "HARMFUL plus PLAYER" filter and of =HARMFUL=: =aura.icon=, =GetAuraDuration=, =GetAuraApplicationDisplayCount=; from S18, the aura part is skipped while =C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret()= is true, and the raw predicate value is appended as the last value) | auto, out of combat and in combat |

** Shared interfaces: S27-S30
Pure additions to =MerrinxUI/Modules/NameplateLogic.lua= (busted in =spec/nameplate_spec.lua=; existing functions unchanged):
#+begin_src lua
NameplateLogic.LevelText(level, classification) -> string|nil
-- level nil or 0 -> nil; level -1 or classification "worldboss" -> "??"
-- otherwise tostring(level) .. suffix: "elite" "+", "rare" "r", "rareelite" "r+", anything else ""
NameplateLogic.LevelColor(level, classification, color) -> r, g, b
-- level -1 or "worldboss" -> 1, 0, 0
-- color is a table with numeric r -> color.r, color.g, color.b ; else 1, 1, 1
NameplateLogic.CC_FILTER = "HARMFUL|CROWD_CONTROL"
NameplateLogic.DebuffFilter(mineOnly, ccSeparate) -> string
-- (true, false) "HARMFUL|PLAYER"   (false, false) "HARMFUL"
-- (true, true)  "HARMFUL|PLAYER|!CROWD_CONTROL"   (false, true) "HARMFUL|!CROWD_CONTROL"
#+end_src

#+begin_src lua
L.ParseList(text, normalize) -> tokens        -- split on ",", normalize each, drop nil, keep first of duplicates, keep order
L.ResolveSpells(tokens, getInfo) -> { tracked = { {id=, name=} }, unknown = { tokens } }
-- getInfo(token) -> id|nil, name|nil (caller maps secret values to nil); duplicate ids dropped; name nil -> tostring(id)
L.Step(prev, obs) -> state, announce          -- states "ready" | "cooling"; obs "cooling"|"gcd"|"busy"|"ready"|"unknown"
L.AnyCooling(states) -> boolean
L.Message(names) -> string|nil                -- {} nil; {"A"} "A ready"; {"A","B"} "A, B ready"
L.ResolveVoice(stored, voices) -> voiceID|nil -- stored if present in voices, else voices[1].voiceID, else nil

- =AnyCooling({})= → false; =AnyCooling({[1]="ready",[2]="cooling"})= → true.

CooldownVoice runtime (=MerrinxUI/Modules/CooldownVoice.lua=):
- =defaults.profile = { enabled = true, spells = "", voice = nil, rate = 0, volume = 100 }=.
- Options (D-27 types only, =order= 1-6): =enabled= toggle; =spells= input ("Spells", =usage= "Comma-separated spell names or IDs", =desc= a function listing tracked names and unresolved tokens); =voice= select (=values= function); =rate= range −10..10 step 1; =volume= range 0..100 step 5; =test= execute ("Test voice"). Every =set= writes the profile, then calls =Refresh()= (spells also =Resolve()=).
- State: =self.tracked= (array of ={id, name}=), =self.unknown=, =self.states= (id → state), =self.ticker=, =self.spoken= (counter). =OnEnable= registers =SPELL_UPDATE_COOLDOWN= → =OnSpellUpdateCooldown= and =SPELLS_CHANGED= → =Resolve=, then =Resolve()=; =OnDisable= cancels the ticker, clears states, calls =C_VoiceChat.StopSpeakingText()=.
- =Speak(text)= is the only =SpeakText= caller (scan and test button).
- Trace (=ns.Debug:Log= only when =ns.Debug:IsActive()=): ="resolve", #tracked, #unknown= on =Resolve=; ="ready", id, name= per announced spell; ="ticker", "on"|"off"= on ticker start/stop. None on the ticker tick itself.
- Checks (read-only; =SpeakText=, =StopSpeakingText=, =Speak=, =Scan=, =Resolve=, =NewTicker=, =Cancel= forbidden inside them):

  - =voices=: =status, count, resolvedVoice, storedPresent=; FAIL when count is 0.
  - =spells=: SKIP "no spells" when the list is empty; PASS =#tracked= when nothing is unknown; INFO =#tracked, unknown tokens...= otherwise.
  - =ticker= (module running): PASS when =(self.ticker ~= nil) == L.AnyCooling(self.states)=; returns =running, coolingCount=.
  - =cooldown info= (first tracked id; SKIP "no spells"): INFO =infoPresent, type(isActive), isOnGCD, issecretvalue(startTime), C_Secrets.ShouldCooldownsBeSecret()=.
  - =spoken=: INFO =self.spoken=.

Media:Fetch(mediatype, key) -> path|nil      -- LSM:Fetch(mediatype, self:Resolve(mediatype, key), true)

** flake.nix design (nix/ci owned; sketch only, not final code)
#+begin_src nix
{
  description = "MerrinxUI - World of Warcraft: Forever addon suite";
  inputs = {
    nixpkgs.url = "github:nixos/nixpkgs/nixpkgs-unstable";
    flake-parts.url = "github:hercules-ci/flake-parts";
    git-hooks = { url = "github:cachix/git-hooks.nix"; inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs"; };
    ace3 = { url = "github:WoWUIDev/Ace3"; flake = false; };                         # S2
    librangecheck = { url = "github:WeakAuras/LibRangeCheck-3.0"; flake = false; };  # S3
    wow-api = { url = "github:Ketho/vscode-wow-api"; flake = false; };               # S10 only
  };
  outputs = inputs: inputs.flake-parts.lib.mkFlake { inherit inputs; } {
    systems = [ "x86_64-linux" "aarch64-linux" ];
    perSystem = { pkgs, system, lib, ... }: let
      pre-commit-check = inputs.git-hooks.lib.${system}.run {
        src = ./.;
        hooks = { statix.enable = true; deadnix.enable = true; nil.enable = true; nixfmt.enable = true;
                  luacheck.enable = true; stylua.enable = true; };
      };
      addonLibs = pkgs.runCommand "merrinxui-libs" { } ''...'';   # S2 Ace3 dirs; S3 + LibRangeCheck-3.0
      merrinxui = pkgs.runCommand "merrinxui" { } ''...'';        # S2 MerrinxUI/ + Libs + reference check
      release = pkgs.runCommand "merrinxui-release" { nativeBuildInputs = [ pkgs.zip ]; } ''
        cp -r ${merrinxui}/MerrinxUI . && chmod -R u+w MerrinxUI
        find MerrinxUI -exec touch -h -d '1980-01-02T00:00:00Z' {} +
        mkdir -p $out
        find MerrinxUI -type f | LC_ALL=C sort | zip -X -D -@ $out/MerrinxUI.zip
      '';
    in {
      formatter = pkgs.nixfmt;
      packages = { inherit merrinxui release; default = merrinxui; };   # S2
      checks = { inherit pre-commit-check; };                           # S1; S2 + merrinxui, lua-syntax; S3 + busted
      devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell {
        inherit (pre-commit-check) shellHook;
        packages = pre-commit-check.enabledPackages ++ (with pkgs; [
          lua5_1 lua51Packages.luacheck lua51Packages.busted stylua lua-language-server zip unzip ]);
      };
    };
  };
}
#+end_src
- =addonLibs= copies these Ace3 directories: =LibStub=, =CallbackHandler-1.0=, =AceAddon-3.0=, =AceConsole-3.0=, =AceEvent-3.0=, =AceDB-3.0=, =AceDBOptions-3.0=, =AceGUI-3.0=, =AceConfig-3.0=. S3 adds =LibRangeCheck-3.0=.
- =merrinxui= builds in four steps:
  1. copy =MerrinxUI/= and add =Libs=;
  2. =chmod -R u+w=;
  3. treat every non-comment, non-blank TOC line as a file path (=\= becomes =/=) and require it to exist;
  4. require every =file="..."= in =embeds.xml= to exist; on a miss, print =missing: <path>= and exit 1.
- Checks:
  - =lua-syntax=: =luac -p= on every =.lua= file under =MerrinxUI=.
  - =busted= (S3): export =HOME=$TMPDIR=; run =busted spec= on a =lib.fileset.toSource= of =MerrinxUI= and =spec=.
- =.gitignore=: =result=, =result-*=, =.pre-commit-config.yaml=. S10 adds =.luarc.json=.
- =embeds.xml=, in this order:
  1. =<Script file="Libs\LibStub\LibStub.lua"/>=;
  2. =<Include file="Libs\X\X.xml"/>= for CallbackHandler-1.0, AceAddon-3.0, AceEvent-3.0, AceConsole-3.0, AceDB-3.0, AceDBOptions-3.0, AceGUI-3.0 and AceConfig-3.0;
  3. S3 adds the LibRangeCheck-3.0 script line.
** CI workflow design (=.github/workflows/ci.yml=, nix/ci owned; sketch only)
#+begin_src yaml
name: ci
on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    tags: ["v*"]
  pull_request:
permissions: {}
jobs:
  check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0 (confirm commit SHA)
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: cachix/install-nix-action@13d8dd58da0234aa297dedd986986ccb8e7f3e24 # v31.11.1
      - run: nix flake check --keep-going -L
      - run: nix build .#release -L
  release:
    if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v')
    needs: check
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    permissions:
      contents: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: cachix/install-nix-action@13d8dd58da0234aa297dedd986986ccb8e7f3e24 # v31.11.1
      - name: Check tag matches TOC version
        env:
          TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}
        run: |
          v=$(sed -n 's/^## Version: *//p' MerrinxUI/MerrinxUI.toc | tr -d '\r')
          case "$TAG" in "v$v"|"v$v"-*) ;; *) echo "tag $TAG does not match TOC version $v" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
      - name: Build and publish release
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}
        run: |
          nix build .#release -L
          cp result/MerrinxUI.zip "MerrinxUI-$TAG.zip"
          pre=""; case "$TAG" in *-*) pre="--prerelease" ;; esac
          gh release create "$TAG" "MerrinxUI-$TAG.zip" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --title "MerrinxUI $TAG" --generate-notes $pre
#+end_src
- =${{ }}= expressions appear only in =env:= blocks, never inside =run= scripts.

* Working directory and validation commands
All commands run in =/home/merrinx/Projects/merrinxui=, in a plain shell. =nix develop -c= provides the tools. No =direnv=, no Haskell.
#+begin_src sh
git add --intent-to-add <new paths>          # user approval required
nix flake check -L                           # exit 0
nix build .#default -L                       # result/MerrinxUI/...
nix build .#release -L && unzip -l result/MerrinxUI.zip   # single top-level MerrinxUI/ incl. Libs/
nix develop -c luacheck MerrinxUI spec       # 0 warnings / 0 errors
nix develop -c stylua --check MerrinxUI spec # exit 0
nix develop -c busted spec                   # N successes / 0 failures / 0 errors
nix fmt -- --check flake.nix                 # exit 0
#+end_src

How each game gate gets its build (user, on the gaming PC):
1. Push the section's commits.
2. Push a prerelease tag =v<TOC version>-<section>.<n>=, for example =v0.1.0-s2.1=.
3. Wait for the green release. The lead can watch it read-only with =gh run list= and =gh run view=.
4. Download =MerrinxUI-<tag>.zip= from the release and extract it into =<WoW Forever>/Interface/AddOns/=, replacing =MerrinxUI/=.
5. Restart the client if the TOC gained files; otherwise =/reload=.
6. Run =/console scriptErrors 1= and =/console taintLog 1=.

Debug track additions (SD1-SD5):
#+begin_src sh
git add --intent-to-add MerrinxUI/Debug/DebugLogic.lua MerrinxUI/Debug/Debug.lua spec/debug_logic_spec.lua   # SD1, user approval
git add --intent-to-add spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua                                                         # SD4, user approval
nix develop -c busted spec                      # N successes / 0 failures / 0 errors (N > 56 from SD1 on)
nix build .#release -L && unzip -l result/MerrinxUI.zip | grep -E 'MerrinxUI/Debug/(DebugLogic|Debug)\.lua'   # both listed (SD1 on)
#+end_src
Read-only grep gates for the debug track (verifier, =eca__grep= over =MerrinxUI=), plus a read of every =GetDebugChecks= / =RegisterChecks= body:
- =function Debug:(IsEnabled|Enable|Disable|SetEnabledState)= → no match.
- =UISpecialFrames= → no match.
- =tostring\(= in =MerrinxUI/Debug/Debug.lua= → no match (only DebugLogic may =tostring=).
- inside check bodies: =CreateFrame|SetCVar|:Show\(|:Hide\(|RegisterEvent|SetPoint|ClearAllPoints|SetParent|SetText|SetAlpha|SetSize|UnitGUID|GetContainer|GetIcon|GetDebuffIcon|CreateStyledFrame|Render\(|:Update|:Apply|Style|Refresh\(|:Enable\(|:Disable\(= → no match.
- (SD5) =ns.Debug:Log= inside =OnUnitHealth=, =OnUnitPower=, =OnUnitAura=, =OnTick=, =OnUpdate= scripts → no match.

From SD4 on, game gates add after step 6: =/mui debug on=; each gate step ends with =/mui test=; at the end =/mui log=, Ctrl+C (Ctrl+A first if nothing is selected) and paste the whole log. That paste is the evidence.

* Implementation sections
Lead protocol for each section:
1. Mark it =IN_PROGRESS=.
2. Run the lua-wow workstream, then the nix/ci workstream.
3. Run the gates in order: verifier, reviewer, security (required every section), then the user game gate where there is one.
4. Mark it =PASSED= only when the latest verifier report is =PASSED= and both the reviewer and security reports are =CLEAR=. A game gate passes only on the user's pasted result.
5. Record the actual commands, outputs and report IDs in the evidence fields. The expected results in this plan are not evidence.
6. If a stop condition is hit, mark the section =BLOCKED= and record why.

** S0 - In-game probes (user, gaming PC)

- Style spec (our code; values follow EllesmereUI's observed defaults; stock assets only):
  | Element | Spec |
  |---|---|
  | Window | width =width= (220). No border. Background =WHITE8X8= black at alpha 0.75. Height = =WindowHeight(rows, rowHeight, 2, 22)=. Registered with =ns.Mover=. |
  | Header | 22 px tall. Background =#1B1B1B= at alpha 1. Left: =Title(session, metric)= in =ARIALN= 11, accent colour =ns.Theme.color.accent= (light blue from S19, D-40; originally the teal =(12,210,157)/255=). Right: three 22 px text buttons: session (=C=/=O=) toggles Current/Overall, metric (=D=/=H=) toggles Damage/Healing, =R= resets. Buttons are white, accent on hover. |
  | Rows | =rowHeight= (18) tall, 2 px spacing, no border, bar background alpha 0. |
  | Class icon | Square, =rowHeight= in size, on the left. =UI-CHARACTERCREATE-CLASSES= with =CLASS_ICON_TCOORDS= and a zoom of 0.06. Hidden when =classFilename= is secret or unknown. |
  | Bar fill | =WHITE8X8= StatusBar right of the icon, alpha 1. Class colour when readable, otherwise the accent colour (=ns.Theme.color.accent=). =SetMinMaxValues(0, sources[1].totalAmount)=, =SetValue(src.totalAmount)=. |
  | Left text | =SetText(src.name)=, white, =ARIALN= =fontSize= (11), shadow. Realm stripped only when the name isn't secret. |
  | Right text | =string.format("%s (%s)", AbbreviateNumbers(total), AbbreviateNumbers(amountPerSecond))=, white, same font. |
  | Refresh | The three =DAMAGE_METER_*= events. Redraws are throttled to at most one per second with =C_Timer.After(1, ...)=. |

** S11 - EnemyCooldowns probes (user, gaming PC)
- Section ID: S11
- Status: PASSED (table corrections go into =EnemyCooldownLogic.SPELLS= in an S12 remediation batch)

- Setup: =/console scriptErrors 1= and =/console taintLog 1=. Each line is at most 252 characters, so it also fits in a macro.
  #+begin_example
  P1  /run local c=CreateFrame("Cooldown",nil,UIParent,"CooldownFrameTemplate") print(GetLocale(),type(GetSpellBaseCooldown),type(C_Spell.GetSpellTexture),type(c.SetCooldown),type(c.SetHideCountdownNumbers),type(UnitGUID)) c:Hide()
  P2  /run local f=CreateFrame("Frame") print("CLEU",pcall(f.RegisterEvent,f,"COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED")) f:UnregisterAllEvents()
  P3  /run MUIS=function(t) for _,id in ipairs(t) do print(id,C_Spell.GetSpellName(id),GetSpellBaseCooldown and GetSpellBaseCooldown(id),C_Spell.GetSpellTexture(id)) end end
  P3a /run MUIS({6552,72,5246,18499,1766,408,2094,1856,5277,2139,1953,11958,122})
  P3b /run MUIS({8122,15487,6346,853,642,1022,633,6789,5484,8042,8177,16188})
  P3c /run MUIS({19503,1499,19263,5384,5211,16979,22812,29166,7744,20589,20594,20549,20554})
  P3d /run MUIS({5579,23273,23274,23276,23277})
  P4  /run MUIC=MUIC or CreateFrame("Frame")MUIC:RegisterEvent("UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED")MUIC:SetScript("OnEvent",function(_,_,u,g,s)if u:find("^namep")then local i=issecretvalue print(u,i(g),i(s),i(s)or s,i(s)or C_Spell.GetSpellName(s))end end)
  P5  /run local i=issecretvalue for n=1,40 do local u="nameplate"..n if UnitExists(u) then print(u,i(UnitGUID(u)),i(UnitIsPlayer(u)),i(UnitCanAttack("player",u)),i(select(2,UnitClass(u))),i(UnitIsUnit(u,"target"))) end end
  P6  /run for n=1,40 do local u="nameplate"..n if UnitExists(u) and UnitIsPlayer(u) then print(u,UnitGUID(u),UnitClass(u),UnitCanAttack("player",u)) end end
  P7  /run local p=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target") if p then local c=CreateFrame("Cooldown",nil,p,"CooldownFrameTemplate") c:SetSize(24,24) c:SetPoint("BOTTOM",p,"TOP") c:SetCooldown(GetTime(),10) end print(p and p:IsForbidden(),InCombatLockdown())
  P8  /run MUIC:UnregisterAllEvents()
  #+end_example
  P4 never prints a raw secret: when =s= is secret it prints =true= in the last two columns.
- Procedure:
  1. Run P1-P3d anywhere.
  2. Run P4. Start a duel; after the countdown and *before any damage*, have the partner cast a curated instant (Kick or Blink). Then fight, and in combat have them cast the same spell again, plus one curated spell at a rank above 1 (for example Earth Shock or Hammer of Justice at max rank).
  3. In combat, target the partner and run P5. Run P6 only if every P5 value printed =false=. Then run P7: a 10-second swipe should appear above their plate.
  4. Run P8. =/reload= afterwards clears =MUIS= and =MUIC=.
  5. If possible, repeat P4/P5 in a battleground.

  | Outcome | Verdict |
  |---|---|
  | P1 all =function= | PASS (locale recorded for information) |

  1. (T-30, user) Setup:
     - =/console scriptErrors 1= and =/console taintLog 1=; clear BugSack;
     - Nameplates on, debuffs on, "Only my debuffs" on;

     Run P1a, P1b, then P2a, P2b, P2c and P2d. Pull the mob, wait about 5 s, and run P2d again. If =SA= lines flood the chat, copy the first five and go on.
  2. =/reload= (it removes the P2 hooks). Target a fresh mob out of combat and run P3a-P3c, then P4a-P4c.
  3. Apply your own debuff (Mage: Frostbolt or Fireball; Druid: Moonfire). You are now in combat. Wait about 3 s and run P4d. Then apply a second, different debuff without any =/run= and note whether a second icon appears by itself. Run P4d again.
  4. Still in combat:
     - target a second mob that carries your debuff and run P5a;
     - run P5b;
     - run P5c-P5e (they create a second container below the plate);
     - run P5f, note whether the icons grow;
     - run P5g, note whether they shrink.
  5. Leave combat, then =/reload=. This removes =MUIU=, =MUIC=, =MUIC2= and every probe hook.
  6. Paste all the output. Add one line per visual question in the verdict table (icons visible, swipe, count, growth direction, centring, size), and say whether BugSack or =Logs/taint.log= has any entry.
  7. (T-31, lead) Record every verdict, then freeze:
     - the D-43 field;
     - the D-44 hooked methods;
     - the D-47 variants (size strategy, growth and centring, =UNIT_AURA= handling, option changes in combat);

- Probe lines (each at most 255 characters). If P1 shows a field other than =unitToken=, replace =.unitToken= in P4c, P5a and P5e with it:
  #+begin_example
  P1a /run for i,p in ipairs(C_NamePlate.GetNamePlates())do local u=p.UnitFrame print(i,p.namePlateUnitToken,p.unitToken,u and u.unit,p==C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit(p.unitToken or "none"),p:IsForbidden())end
  P1b /run local p=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target")print(p.namePlateUnitToken,p.unitToken,p.UnitFrame.unit,p.unitToken and UnitIsUnit(p.unitToken,"target"))
  P2a /run local u=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target").UnitFrame MUIU=u hooksecurefunc(u,"SetAlpha",function(_,a)print("SA",issecretvalue(a)and"S"or a,debugstack(2,4,0))end)
  P2b /run hooksecurefunc(MUIU,"SetAlphaFromBoolean",function()print("SAB",debugstack(2,4,0))end)
  P2c /run print("P2c",MUIU.AurasFrame and MUIU.AurasFrame:GetParent()==MUIU,MUIU.AurasFrame and MUIU.AurasFrame:IsShown())
  P2d /run local p=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target")local u=p.UnitFrame local a=u:GetAlpha()print("P2d",InCombatLockdown(),u==MUIU,issecretvalue(a)and"S"or a,u:IsShown())
  P3a /run local ok,c=pcall(CreateFrame,"AuraContainer",nil,UIParent,"CustomAuraContainerTemplate")MUIC=ok and c or nil print("P3a",ok,ok and c:GetObjectType() or c,type(CustomAuraContainerSharedMixin))
  P3b /run local t={}for k,v in pairs(MUIC)do if type(v)=="function"then t[#t+1]=k end end table.sort(t)print("P3b",#t,table.concat(t," "))
  P3c /run local c=MUIC print("P3c",type(c.SetUnit),type(c.UpdateAllAuras),type(c.AddAuraGroup),type(c.SetAuraGroupMaxFrameCount),type(c.SetAuraGroupFilterString),type(c.SetAuraGroupLayout),type(c.SetScale))
  P4a /run local p=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target")local c=CreateFrame("AuraContainer",nil,p,"CustomAuraContainerTemplate")MUIC=c c:SetPoint("BOTTOM",p,"TOP",0,40)c:SetSize(120,20)print("P4a",c:GetParent()==p)
  P4b /run MUIC:AddAuraGroup("d","HARMFUL|PLAYER",{maxFrameCount=4,layout={elementWidth=18,elementHeight=18,elementSpacing=2}})print("P4b",MUIC:GetNumChildren())
  P4c /run MUIC:SetUnit(C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target").unitToken)MUIC:UpdateAllAuras()local w=MUIC:GetWidth()print("P4c",MUIC:GetNumChildren(),issecretvalue(w)and"S"or w)
  P4d /run local w,h=MUIC:GetSize()print("P4d",InCombatLockdown(),C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret(),MUIC:GetNumChildren(),issecretvalue(w)and"S"or w,issecretvalue(h)and"S"or h)
  P5a /run MUIC:SetUnit(C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target").unitToken)MUIC:UpdateAllAuras()print("P5a",InCombatLockdown())
  P5b /run MUIC:SetAuraGroupFilterString("d","HARMFUL")MUIC:SetAuraGroupMaxFrameCount("d",2)MUIC:UpdateAllAuras()print("P5b",InCombatLockdown())
  P5c /run local p=C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target")local c=CreateFrame("AuraContainer",nil,p,"CustomAuraContainerTemplate")MUIC2=c c:SetPoint("TOP",p,"BOTTOM",0,-30)c:SetSize(120,30)print("P5c",InCombatLockdown())
  P5d /run MUIC2:AddAuraGroup("d","HARMFUL",{maxFrameCount=4,layout={elementWidth=18,elementHeight=18,elementSpacing=2}})print("P5d",InCombatLockdown())
  P5e /run MUIC2:SetUnit(C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit("target").unitToken)MUIC2:UpdateAllAuras()print("P5e",InCombatLockdown())
  P5f /run MUIC2:SetAuraGroupLayout("d",{elementWidth=28,elementHeight=28,elementSpacing=2})MUIC2:UpdateAllAuras()print("P5f",InCombatLockdown())
  P5g /run MUIC2:SetScale(0.5)MUIC2:Hide()MUIC2:Show()print("P5g",InCombatLockdown(),MUIC2:IsShown())
  #+end_example

- Steps (T-34, one owner). The sketch assumes the PASS rows with the =SetAuraGroupLayout= size variant (P5f); T-31 rewrites it for any other variant before the hand-off:
  1. Constants: remove =DEBUFF_COUNT_SIZE=; add =local DEBUFF_GROUP = "debuffs"=.
  2. =CreateStyledFrame=: replace =frame.debuffIcons = {}= with:
     #+begin_src lua
     local profile = self.db.profile
     local debuffs = CreateFrame("AuraContainer", nil, frame, "CustomAuraContainerTemplate")
     debuffs:AddAuraGroup(DEBUFF_GROUP, profile.debuffsMineOnly and "HARMFUL|PLAYER" or "HARMFUL", {
     	maxFrameCount = profile.debuffMax,
     	layout = { elementWidth = profile.debuffSize, elementHeight = profile.debuffSize, elementSpacing = DEBUFF_SPACING },
     })
     debuffs:Hide()
     frame.debuffs = debuffs
     #+end_src
     For variant B, add the fixed =SetSize= that T-31 records from P4.
  3. Delete =Nameplates:GetDebuffIcon=.
  4. =UpdateDebuffs(unit, frame)=:
     #+begin_src lua
     function Nameplates:UpdateDebuffs(unit, frame)
     	local profile = self.db.profile
     	local debuffs = frame.debuffs
     	if not profile.debuffs then
     		debuffs:Hide()
     		return
     	end

     	local anchor = profile.debuffAnchor
     	local relativeTo = frame
     	if anchor == "BOTTOM" then
     		if profile.castBar then
     			relativeTo = frame.castBar
     		elseif profile.powerBar then
     			relativeTo = frame.powerBar
     		else
     			relativeTo = frame.healthBar
     		end
     	elseif anchor ~= "LEFT" and anchor ~= "RIGHT" then
     		relativeTo = frame.nameText
     	end

     	local point, relPoint, x, y = NameplateLogic.DebuffLayout(anchor, 1, 1, profile.debuffSize, DEBUFF_SPACING)
     	debuffs:ClearAllPoints()
     	debuffs:SetPoint(point, relativeTo, relPoint, x, y)
     	debuffs:SetAuraGroupFilterString(DEBUFF_GROUP, profile.debuffsMineOnly and "HARMFUL|PLAYER" or "HARMFUL")
     	debuffs:SetAuraGroupMaxFrameCount(DEBUFF_GROUP, profile.debuffMax)
     	debuffs:SetAuraGroupLayout(DEBUFF_GROUP, {
     		elementWidth = profile.debuffSize,
     		elementHeight = profile.debuffSize,
     		elementSpacing = DEBUFF_SPACING,
     	})
     	debuffs:SetUnit(unit)
     	debuffs:UpdateAllAuras()
     	debuffs:Show()
     end
     #+end_src
     The S18 =C_Secrets= guard and every runtime =C_UnitAuras= call go away. With the =SetScale= variant (P5g), =SetAuraGroupLayout= is replaced by =debuffs:SetScale(profile.debuffSize / 18)=, the layout uses a fixed 18, and =x= and =y= are divided by the scale.

WS-37..WS-39 run strictly one after another with one lua-wow owner: WS-37 and WS-38 share =OptionsWindow.lua=, and WS-38/WS-39 consume the WS-37 interface. None of them runs while an S27-S30 workstream is open.

  "tasks": [
    {"id": "T-00", "ws": "WS-00", "text": "Run S0 probes; report outputs, testable classes, GitHub owner/name"},
    {"id": "T-01", "ws": "WS-01", "text": "flake.nix scaffold, .gitignore, lock"},
    {"id": "T-02", "ws": "WS-02", "text": "TOC (16001, Version 0.1.0), embeds.xml, Core.lua hub, .luacheckrc, stylua.toml"},
    {"id": "T-03", "ws": "WS-03", "text": "ace3 input, addonLibs, merrinxui + ref check, lua-syntax, release zip, ci.yml with verified SHAs"},
    {"id": "T-04", "ws": "WS-04", "text": "RangeTextLogic (incl. DEFAULT_SPELLS/ResolveSpell) + MoverLogic + specs, Mover, RangeText, unlock; Version 0.2.0"},
    {"id": "T-05", "ws": "WS-05", "text": "librangecheck input, Libs copy, busted check"},
    {"id": "T-06", "ws": "WS-06", "text": "CombatQueue + spec, Combat, Hider, HideBlizzard (+Logic, spec, Ensure); Version 0.3.0"},
    {"id": "T-07", "ws": "WS-07", "text": "NameplateLogic.Color + spec, Nameplates core; Version 0.4.0"},
    {"id": "T-08", "ws": "WS-08", "text": "Nameplate cast bar; Version 0.5.0"},
    {"id": "T-09", "ws": "WS-09", "text": "ActionBarLogic + spec, ActionBars with per-class paging; Version 0.6.0"},
    {"id": "T-10", "ws": "WS-10", "text": "BindingPrefix + spec, override bindings; Version 0.7.0"},
    {"id": "T-11", "ws": "WS-11", "text": "DamageMeterLogic + spec, EllesmereUI-style DamageMeter; Version 0.8.0"},
    {"id": "T-12", "ws": "WS-12", "text": "wow-api input, luarc, shellHook, .gitignore"},
    {"id": "T-13", "ws": "WS-13", "text": "Run S11 probes P1-P8; verdicts per table; curated-table corrections; confirm any PASS-degraded row"},
    {"id": "T-14", "ws": "WS-14", "text": "EnemyCooldownLogic + spec examples; EnemyCooldowns runtime with enabled option; TOC/luacheck appends; version per D-23"},
    {"id": "T-15", "ws": "WS-15", "text": "iconSize/maxIcons/anchor/x/y options; Refresh re-renders visible plates; version per D-23"},
    {"id": "T-16", "ws": "WS-16", "text": "Run S14 P1-P8 (P9 only if P8 fails); record verdicts; choose the Esc variant and font"},
    {"id": "T-17", "ws": "WS-17", "text": "Spec first; OptionsLogic per the OptionsLogic subsection; Theme per the Theme subsection; TOC lines after the last Util line; no version bump"},
    {"id": "T-18", "ws": "WS-18", "text": "GameMenuLogic + spec; GameMenuButton per D-32; Core OpenOptions/SlashCommand; TOC + version per D-33; luacheck S16 row"},
    {"id": "T-19", "ws": "WS-19", "text": "OptionsWindow per D-26..D-31; Core OpenOptions -> window, profiles order -1; TOC + version per D-33; luacheck S17 row"},
    {"id": "T-D1", "ws": "WS-D1", "text": "DebugLogic.lua + spec/debug_logic_spec.lua with all DebugLogic examples"},
    {"id": "T-D2", "ws": "WS-D1", "text": "Debug.lua: module, namespace, lazy log, IsActive/SetActive/Log/RegisterChecks/RunTests/ShowLog/ClearLog/Slash/GetOptions, RunSource for providers, automatic settings and enabled-state results, AceGUI log window"},
    {"id": "T-D3", "ws": "WS-D1", "text": "Core checks: version, interface, secret api, libraries, slash, options, profile, coverage <Module>, log"},
    {"id": "T-D4", "ws": "WS-D1", "text": "Core.SlashCommand routing (unlock/lock unchanged); TOC Debug lines after Core.lua; .luacheckrc SD1 row"},
    {"id": "T-D6", "ws": "WS-D2", "text": "Mover, Combat, Hider RegisterChecks providers"},
    {"id": "T-D7", "ws": "WS-D2", "text": "RangeText GetDebugChecks: spell, font, visibility, range, in range"},
    {"id": "T-D8", "ws": "WS-D2", "text": "HideBlizzard GetDebugChecks: frame <Name>, reload needed <key>, meter cvar"},
    {"id": "T-D9", "ws": "WS-D3", "text": "EnemyCooldownLogic.Audit + spec cases"},
    {"id": "T-D10", "ws": "WS-D3", "text": "EnemyCooldowns GetDebugChecks: spell table, index, ticker, tracked, icons <unit>, container parent <unit>, no foreign icons"},
    {"id": "T-D11", "ws": "WS-D4", "text": "Nameplates GetDebugChecks: apis, styled plates, target plate, health colour, highlight, cast events, cast anchor, target cast, health text, power bar, power text, debuffs, secret flags"},
    {"id": "T-D12", "ws": "WS-D4", "text": "spec/debug_coverage_spec.lua (TOC-driven, EXEMPT list, >= 5 modules, Debug before Util); TOC 0.9.0"},
    {"id": "T-D14", "ws": "user", "text": "G-SD4-GAME steps 1-14; paste the copied log and step notes"},
    {"id": "T-D15", "ws": "WS-D5", "text": "Trace calls at the listed points; RangeText lastTraceState; TOC 0.10.0"},
    {"id": "T-D16", "ws": "user", "text": "G-SD5-GAME; paste the copied log"},
    {"id": "T-20", "ws": "WS-20", "text": "UpdateDebuffs early return also when C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret(); apis/debuffs/secret flags checks secret-aware; .luacheckrc C_Secrets; TOC 0.13.0"},
    {"id": "T-21", "ws": "user", "text": "G-S18-GAME steps 1-6 (in-combat repro of the bug); paste the copied log and step notes"},
    {"id": "T-22", "ws": "lead", "text": "planning amendments recorded in this file: D-27/D-34/A-22/D-S18-6, non-goals, S9 style table, defaults, coverage matrix, layout, module contract, sections S19-S22 (done at planning time)"},
    {"id": "T-23", "ws": "WS-21", "text": "Theme spec case first; Theme accent/switchOn/fill light blue"},
    {"id": "T-24", "ws": "WS-22", "text": "flake input libsharedmedia (file+https SVN bc/182, flake=false); addonLibs copy; nix flake lock"},
    {"id": "T-25", "ws": "WS-23", "text": "embeds.xml Script line after LibRangeCheck; Debug libraries + LibSharedMedia-3.0"},
    {"id": "T-26", "ws": "WS-24", "text": "FontLogic + 7 busted cases; TOC Util\\FontLogic.lua after Util\\Hider.lua; RangeText default, MigrateFont, ApplyFont, LSM callback, select option, font check"},
    {"id": "T-27", "ws": "WS-25", "text": "isFontSelect/setPreviewFont; configureSelect and openDropdown font reset; font preview check; ## Version per D-41"},
    {"id": "T-28", "ws": "user", "text": "G-S22-GAME steps 1-9; paste the copied log and notes; tag v<Version>-s22.1"},
    {"id": "T-29", "ws": "lead", "text": "researcher read of forever AuraContainer and NamePlateBase sources at a pinned commit; adjust P3-P5 lines if signatures differ (done at planning: SetAuraGroupLayout added to P3c, P5f/P5g split)"},
    {"id": "T-30", "ws": "WS-26", "text": "run S23 P1-P5 per the procedure; paste output, visual answers, BugSack/taint status; /reload"},
    {"id": "T-31", "ws": "lead", "text": "record S23 verdicts; freeze D-43, D-44 methods, D-47 variants; update S24/S25 step text"},
    {"id": "T-32", "ws": "WS-27", "text": "D-43 token at 4 sites; hooked/owned sets, OnUnitFrameSetAlpha, StyleUnit/UnstyleUnit/OnDisable ordering; target plate appends; TOC 0.15.0"},
    {"id": "T-33", "ws": "user", "text": "G-S24-GAME steps 1-5 on v0.15.0-s24.1; paste log and notes"},
    {"id": "T-34", "ws": "WS-28", "text": "AuraContainer per styled frame; UpdateDebuffs via container; remove GetDebuffIcon/pool/runtime aura calls; UNIT_AURA per D-54; debuffs/debuff icons checks; no TOC change (0.15.0, D-55)"},
    {"id": "T-35", "ws": "user", "text": "commit, push, tag v0.15.0-s25.1 (D-55); G-S25-GAME steps 1-8 with /mui test only (D-51); paste /mui log and visual lines a-h"},
    {"id": "T-36", "ws": "WS-29", "text": "3 FontLogic specs first; Values(hash, isUsable, keep); RangeText fontProbe and option values"},
    {"id": "T-39", "ws": "WS-31", "text": "LevelText/LevelColor specs first; levelText font string, UpdateLevel, LEVEL_EVENTS, DebuffAnchorFrame, option, apis paths, level check; luacheck globals; TOC 0.16.0"},
    {"id": "T-40", "ws": "WS-32", "text": "debuffX/debuffY defaults, options (16, 17), SetPoint offsets, debuffs check offsets"},
    {"id": "T-41", "ws": "WS-33", "text": "DebuffFilter spec first; CreateAuraContainer/ResizeAuraButtons; frame.cc; UpdateCC; DebuffFilter in UpdateDebuffs; DebuffAnchorFrame LEFT; options 18-20; cc, cc icons, cc filters checks"},
    {"id": "T-42", "ws": "WS-34", "text": "6 CooldownVoiceLogic specs first; CooldownVoiceLogic; CooldownVoice module (Resolve, Scan, ticker, Speak, options, checks, traces); TOC lines; C_VoiceChat in luacheck"},
    {"id": "T-43", "ws": "WS-35", "text": "integrated VER/REV/SEC over S27-S30"},
    {"id": "T-44", "ws": "WS-36", "text": "commit, push, tag v0.17.0-s30.1 (D-81), restart client, G-S30-GAME steps 1-8 (step 6 G-FU-NP only if G-S9-GAME left it unanswered, D-90); paste /mui log"},
    {"id": "T-45", "ws": "WS-37", "text": "3 FontLogic specs first; FontLogic GLOBAL/Resolve/Values label; Util/Media.lua module, page, API, relay, checks; TOC version + Media line; Debug enabled-state guard; RangeText adopts; OptionsWindow setPreviewFont + drop local LSM"},
    {"id": "T-46", "ws": "WS-38", "text": "windowFont at build(); setFont order; LSM30_Statusbar previews with item.preview; font check"},
    {"id": "T-47", "ws": "WS-39", "text": "Nameplates font/barTexture defaults and options 21-22; ApplySize via ns.Media with barPath and cast colour; count fonts tracked; MEDIA_CHANGED handler; media check; then integrated gates S31-S33"},
    {"id": "T-48", "ws": "WS-40", "text": "commit, push, tag v<Version>-s33.1, restart client, G-S33-GAME steps 1-9; paste /mui log"}

* Gate assignment template (lead)
Each verifier, reviewer and security assignment includes:
- =Workflow intent: <verification|review|security>=;
- the section ID, workstream and task IDs, and AC IDs;
- the owned paths;
- the literal commands to run from =/home/merrinx/Projects/merrinxui=;
- the evidence field to fill.

Outcomes: verifier =PASSED= / =FAILED= / =UNVERIFIED=; reviewer and security =CLEAR= / =FINDINGS= / =UNVERIFIED=.

Every security review covers:
- workflow permissions, action pins and script injection;
- flake input pinning;
- release asset contents;
- global and namespace leaks;
- protected calls in combat, and taint;
- CVar side effects;
- fields or entries written on Blizzard frames and tables (AC-37), and hooks on Blizzard methods;
- license compliance (no copied third-party code or media).
